# CODADAPT ROUND 69 — Tensor-Train coded residual trainability resolution

**COMPLETE. Gate A PASS; real gate FAIL. REDUNDANT_WITH_PAIR as operational verdict, with GENERALIZATION_FAILURE/downside. No frozen candidate or Round70.**

A small TT can learn the deliberately representable higher-order functions. This
does not give a robust useful residual model on the known real panel. The frozen
Base is retained throughout real training: this round removes the additive
scaffold replacement confound identified in Round68, yet severe downside remains.
“Redundant” here means no demonstrated positive median advantage over the tested
pair control; it is NOT a mathematical equivalence claim or a proof of zero gain.

## Scope, protocol and audit limitations

- Native Base is fitted once per primary synthetic fixture and reused per arm; real Base/LGB are archived frozen fits on identical splits. Function-only synthetic arms diagnose known representation and are never deployed in place of Base. Residual oracles consume true function labels and are explicitly diagnostic.
- QR/normalization preserves the represented function at the transformation step (tested). However per-core ridge is gauge-dependent: subsequent optimization changes effective regularization. This is not a gauge-invariant global-objective proof. All methods use the same stated local ridge scale, not a claim of identical invariant regularization.
- Discovery rule is a bounded sequential pair-residual scoring heuristic (max128pairs among first32features), not an exhaustive or ANOVA-pure interaction score. Chosen globally by synthetic validation:interaction9.7934%,pair2.45495%,random−.08893%,univariate−.18634%. Test medians9.95052/2.94081/−.02210/−.08286%. Oracle-known-feature and discovery pipelines also differ in finite fixture versus8-bin encoder layout; not a pure isolated selection-error estimate.
- OriginalR65parity true distribution is uniform continuous; new core trainability fixtures deliberately use exact finite states. Historical quantization-floor analysis is separate and stops overclaiming a pure solver cause.
- Full-grid small references and SVDinitializations are diagnostics only; real learner does not materialize an exponential tensor. CPcontrol is a bounded simple ALS prototype, not a tuned CPoptimum. HGBwas optional and not run; native Base,matchedpair,TTandLightGBMare complete.
- Existing duplicate diagnostic key rows caused by cachedCSVround trips were deduplicated by explicit variant keys for aggregation; original table retained as diagnostic_results_before_key_dedup.csv. No fitted model or prediction was removed. Exact tensor aliases pooled in GateA to avoid inflated mechanism counts. The design file hash remained unchanged; this is a conservative accounting clarification, not a recipe change.
- Export initially hit a Python module-name collision with the new real_panel.py before exporting inputs; isolated import path fixed, log retained, no fit lost or repeated. Deep-size accounting was corrected to include NumPy base buffers; final real_costs.csvrecomputed measurements from saved models, without Base/TTrefits. Raw earlier costs remain in real_results.csv.
- Known real development only; no independent transfer or universal TTimpossibility conclusion. No claim of scientific novelty.

# CODADAPT ROUND 68 — Why synthetic context works but real context does not

**COMPLETE — MIXED. The Round67 global contextual recipe remains CLOSED. No new recipe or Round69 execution.**

The principal explanation is a weak replacement scaffold plus poorly reproducible residual values, with source-dependent tail amplification. Constructed functions contain a strong repeatable contextual signal by design; real residuals need not. Low support makes estimation harder but is not sufficient to explain the real failure. The measured contribution does **not** mostly collapse to additive main effects. Small task benefit is not proof of additive equivalence.

## Scope and resumed execution

All18 known real sources ×5 archived splits were audited; the preregistered12-source causal panel contains42splits (three per source, five for Diabetes/RAM/water). These are development diagnostics, not independent confirmation. Base, additive scaffold, bins, splits and old primary models were reused. K4, penalty20 and table capacity remained fixed. No core/API/compiler change or remote operation.

On resumption, oracle, collapse, all51 synthetic settings, half-training fits and failure rows were COMPLETE. Replay had passed. Private tests were PARTIAL:10passed, one test contained `y[2,None]` instead of `y[2][:,None]`; this was a test-only indexing typo, not a model error. Report/memory/final audit were NOT_STARTED. No valid experimental fit was rerun and no partial artifact was removed. Original failure logs remain. Earlier oracle indexing failure was fixed before valid predictions for that arm; existing checkpoint fits were reused.

## Requested26-point answers

1. **Fit/solve:**1267 checkpointed fitting operations:261 mapping fits and1006 table solves. Original R67fits are reused, not counted again. Aggregate candidate scoring and closed-form best-pair projection calculations are additional work, not independent checkpointed solves. Tests contain small independent fixtures.
2. **Real datasets:**18sources/90splits audited;12sources/42splits receive new causal oracle/grouping/assignment interventions. No new real source.
3. **Real oracle:**target-residual quartile oracle +45.84411%RMSE versus fixed additive (+43.55376%versus Base); equal residual ranks +45.05404%versus additive. Fixed trained values with target-chosen assignments give +15.28969%global and +38.48384%greedy per-feature. These consume heldout target information and are NONDEPLOYABLE attained diagnostic solutions, not certified optimal bounds.
4. **Discovered context:**frozen primary on this causal subset +0.91463%versus additive and−1.26972%versus Base. The unchanged full18-source R67 result remains−0.04356%versus additive and−0.51498%versus Base. Do not substitute the selected causal panel for the full panel.
5. **Oracle/discovery gap:**finite four-mapping best-test oracle improves1.91444%relative to frozen primary; validation selection improves1.12401%. Huge target-oracle gains are not clean discovery evidence: the same target-coding procedure recovers33.09780%on independently shuffled residuals. Labels themselves provide side information even without predictable context. Finite oracle failure would likewise not prove impossibility of all context partitions.
6. **Additive collapse:**only3.86506%median heldout delta variance explained by the fixed regularized additive projection;0/18source means exceed90%. ADDITIVE_COLLAPSE is not supported. This is a finite ridge projection, not the best possible unconstrained additive approximation. The old+0.06315%task difference measures usefulness, not contribution equivalence.
7. **Global/grouped:**one/two/four groups improve0.00000%/0.22288%/0.47883%relative to primary (source-balanced medians). Same4Σbin table entries; grouping has different routing metadata. No model selected.
8. **Feature-specific:**+0.46437%relative to primary, still−0.10940%versus Base. Diabetes improves1.31717%relative to primary but remains−7.43350%versus Base; RAM unchanged; water worsens0.92441%relative to primary. GLOBAL_CONTEXT_BIAS is not established as the main cause.
9. **Support:**oracle gains grow3.57→48.54%as mean active context×bin support grows2→100; discovered1.12→20.18%. No universal threshold: continuous estimation/shrinkage effect under fixed penalty, not a new gate.
10. **Assignment stability:**half-training ARI median0.35693. Label-aligned assignment switching with fixed values yields residual-normalized prediction MSE0.05402 and median RMSE harm0.51081%.
11. **Value stability:**holding assignment fixed, half-sample value variation produces NMSE0.08191 and delta-only correlation−0.08554. This exceeds assignment variation in median, but contrasts are not additive percentages of failure. The whole-model correlation can remain high because the scaffold dominates. Discovery and value fits share halves; frozen scaffold used full training, so this is not a fully independent cross-fit experiment.
12. **Entropy:**training imbalance changes local support and signal mixture together; it cannot identify pure entropy causality at fixed N. With one frozen trained model and only test-mixture reweighting, oracle gain43.01→41.36%, discovered21.81→21.43%as entropy2→0.242bits. Entropy alone is not the main failure explanation here.
13. **Occupancy:**reducing only inactive-feature occupied bins to50%/25%keeps oracle gain43.83%/44.32%and discovered18.92%/19.37%(balanced reference43.27%/18.19%). Not a broad occupancy bottleneck for this fixed function.
14. **Weighted drift:**important-cell intervention keeps context frequency L1exactly0.06164 while weighted cell drift rises0.01729→0.07666. Function MSE falls0.09645→0.05199: hidden cell drift exists, but this intervention does not demonstrate harmful drift. Across90real splits, Spearman with RMSE harm is0.274frequency,0.225weighted context,0.275weighted cell; exploratory associations, not causal proof.
15. **Tails:**among negative splits, source-balanced top1/5/10%rows explain24.58%/48.34%/63.60%of positive excess SSE. Net-harm fractions are separately stored and may exceed100%. Removing rows using each model's own errors is not a fair common-mask performance comparison.
16. **Diabetes:**Base→additive accounts for79.996%of pooled excess MSE; contextual delta contributes the remainder. Additive loses7.17966%RMSE, primary8.87308%, incremental context1.58681%. MAE also worsens6.48%, own5%trim RMSE8.15%, common-mask17.28%: not just a few tails. Assignment ARI0.00157, fixed-assignment value NMSE0.04983 versus assignment NMSE0.03096; values and scaffold, not proof of missing context alone.
17. **RAM_price:**one feature; observed context is100%determined by its main code. A context here cannot establish a new multivariate regime. Assignment ARI1.0 and swap NMSE0, but half-value NMSE1.00887 and delta correlation−0.99153: value instability despite stable assignments. Additive already loses43.41%RMSE; primary62.63%. About68.88%pooled excess MSE arises before context. MAE improves15.57%and model-own trim improves53.49%, while common-mask trim worsens72.64%: severe heterogeneous tail risk, not a deployable robust-metric rescue.
18. **Water-treatment:**worst seed66004 BaseRMSE0.83127→additive2.79412→context2.97362 (−257.71835%gain). Additive adds7.11610MSE and context1.03528;87.30%of this split's harm predates context. Context residual correlation flips+0.66596train→−0.22443validation→−0.15577test. Largest harmful row55 has target99.8, Base98.97782, additive part−8.31238, delta−2.31954, final88.34590. All row identities are archived, not just the ten listed worst rows. Top5%accounts for43.77%positive harm; own/common trims still fail strongly. Base is unusually good on this target-concentrated test split, magnifying the ratio.
19. **Pair redundancy:**bounded sum-of-pairs projection explains12.98611%heldout delta variance, best single pair4.85938%, constant context approximately0%. Neither additive nor tested pairs explain most fitted contribution. This does not imply useful higher-order signal: unpredictable noisy corrections also resist these projections.
20. **Higher order:**37existing-input splits/11sources admit bounded three-way controls within budget; median improvement versus contextual model0.59019%. Synthetic R67matched-K4 pair/triple results are reused: genuine constructed higher-order examples exist, but performance is source-dependent. At unchanged16-bin precision the new8-feature synthetic budget cannot fit a full17³table; no resolution/budget rescue was added. New empirical triples fit only3sources. Bounded lexicographic controls are not exhaustive best-pair/three-way oracles.
21. **Realistic-support synthetic:**same real input rows/codes/missingness/context counts/cell supports,12sources: oracle median39.50253%, discovered28.71068%versus known frozen offset. True noise is unidentifiable; training residual MAD is a proxy. Strong representable contextual labels were injected, so this rejects support-alone sufficiency, not proves real context exists. RAM is a univariate exception. In controlled noise sweep, discovered gain22.10%atσ0.1→0.64%atσ2, oracle67.08%→5.11%: signal/noise and residual-value reproducibility matter.
22. **Verdict:MIXED.** Scaffold replacement harm + residual-value generalization + source-specific tail amplification; some bounded mapping opportunity. Neither universal representation impossibility, pure discovery limitation, additive collapse nor global-context bias is established.
23. **Close:**the R67general contextual recipe stays closed. No classification, confirmation, deployment integration, tuning or rescue. Large target-coded oracle gains cannot justify reopening it.
24. **RETRY_IF:**Only a separately authorized falsifier of reproducible residual-value signal at fixed Base scaffold, fixed mapping/capacity and equal sample sizes, with label-permutation negative control and no stress-test downside. No K/penalty/hash/gating rescue, no favorable-subset confirmation.
25. **Justified hypothesis:**real residual corrections have little reproducible conditional value signal at this fixed granularity, even when the same cells can represent deliberately injected effects. This is a falsifiable learning/generalization hypothesis, not a new architecture or established universal law.
26. **Proposed Round69:**PROPOSAL_ONLY_NOT_STARTED: test whether reproducible residual-value signal exists above frozen Base before any new context discovery; compare cross-half correction covariance and heldout utility against label-permutation null at fixed assignments, equal sample counts and unchanged budget. Stop if no reproducible signal.

## Identification limits and negative controls

- Exact full-code-stratum assignment permutation preserves every cell support but moved0%of rows in these deterministic mappings. This is an explicitly powerless control, not evidence of semantic-context benefit. Ordinary global shuffle would break the required cell supports. No claim that these two properties were simultaneously randomized.
- Effect modulation counts:3069SHAPE_CHANGE,2767AMPLITUDE_ONLY,731OFFSET_ONLY,86SIGN_CHANGE on supported feature/context slices. These compare main versus main+delta; fitted shape changes are not heldout evidence that those changes are predictive. Curvature uses code order and is omitted for categoricals.
- The synthetic generator freezes the true joint two-feature regime; the R67guided hash family is restricted and can alias four combinations into three states. Its oracle/discovered gap includes this mapping-family restriction, not only noisy discovery.
- Shuffled residual oracle is an explicit negative control for target-coded ceiling interpretation. Its33.10%gain makes a simple oracle-positive⇒DISCOVERY_LIMIT conclusion invalid.
- No per-source teacher, preprocessing, main-effect code or capacity was selected. A finite bank is not all possible context partitions. Grouped mapping fit metadata differs even when value capacity matches.
- Real stress metrics, support, drift and assignment evidence remain observational except for the specified frozen-component interventions. Support/noise/occupancy identification applies to constructed functions. Ten inherited additive fits hit the R67iteration cap; no optimization was repeated to rescue them.
- Timing is not a deployment benchmark; mapping and solve times remain in checkpoints. No system advantage is inferred from concurrently running diagnostics.

## Scope and audit limitations

- The initial synthetic gate compared K4 discovery with true-K pair controls. Corrected matched-K4 controls were added after8real contexts; real execution paused, then resumed only after the corrected gate passed. Preliminary gate, logs and every completed fit remain. No real result selected the correction or recipe.
- Internal discovery scoring is training-only but not fully cross-fitted: its residual scaffold used all training rows. No outer validation/test labels enter mapping or values.
- Ten of90real additive scaffold solves hit the fixed200iteration cap. All contextual delta solves stop before that cap, but this does not make the inherited scaffold converged. No solver rescue was performed; outcomes cannot identify a pure representation limit. EBM is a fixed finite-budget reference, not a tuned best additive oracle.
- Oracle context is a finite noisy-label fit, not an exact global bound. In noisy_context, latent flips are response noise; stored truth is a latent realization, not asserted to be the Bayes conditional mean.
- More parameters alone are not causal proof: actual value counts/bytes/lookups differ slightly, pair/triple coarsening favors some functions and harms others. Positive higher-order evidence is restricted to the explicitly constructed examples.
- Additive/irrelevant nulls are duplicated intentionally and are not counted as independent supporting discoveries. Full independent context tables and unshrunk oracle variants are diagnostics; no outcome-based replacement.
- Local warm timings can overlap audit work. LightGBM native allocations may not be fully captured by Python deep-size accounting; no complete native-memory superiority claim.
- Public tests:371passed/4xfail plus4initial packaging access failures; authorized offline retry4passed. Effective375passed,4xfail across separate runs. Nine private tests pass. Public Ruff and private correctness Ruff pass.
- Rounds1–66 preserved verbatim in history; Round51 remains incomplete. Private artifacts ignored, research memory trackable. Public core unchanged. No commit/push/tag/release or Round68.

## Prior-art sources

# Prior art: no novelty claim

- [Hastie & Tibshirani, Varying-Coefficient Models (1993)](https://rss.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1111/j.2517-6161.1993.tb01939.x): effects can depend on other covariates; the present hard discrete context is a restricted coded version of conditional/varying effects, not a new statistical principle.
- [Jacobs et al., Adaptive Mixtures of Local Experts (1991)](https://www.cs.toronto.edu/~fritz/absps/jjnh91.pdf): contextual specialization and expert allocation are established. Here assignment is hard, nonrecursive, and a small train-only mapping; values are lookup deltas with shared additive scaffold, without neural gates or jointly alternating experts.
- [Latent class distributional regression (2020)](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7666475/): latent heterogeneity and nonlinear additive predictors overlap conceptually. Round67 does not estimate latent outcome distributions, mixture likelihoods or posterior class probabilities. The article's indexed abstract was available; direct full-text access returned a browser check.
- [Markov-switching GAMLSS (2021)](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S2452306221000502): regime-specific smooth effects are established. This prototype has no time transition model or Markov state. Indexed abstract inspected; full page failed to open.

Clustered/contextual GAM, latent-class regression and switching regression all
overlap in the use of group-dependent effects. The experiment tests whether the
restricted coded implementation is useful, not whether this broad family is new.
Internal R22 originals remain absent; external papers cannot resolve that gap.

13. **Catastrophic>10%:**4/18source means;20/90splits. Source Wilson95%CI[9.00%,45.21%]; split Wilson[14.87%,31.85%]ignores dependence. Source-cluster bootstrap of split rate[10.00%,36.67%]. Both risk gates fail.
14. **MAE versus RMSE:**median MAE gain -1.795755%; losses are not merely RMSE sensitivity. Tail concentration is severe on RAM_price, whereas Diabetes also suffers broad MAE loss.
15. **Additive control:**-0.725427% median,CI[-13.837683699807162, 0.6268373593034972]; same32codes and inherited .001ridge unit-denominator readout.
16. **Rational-specific gain:**0.081002percentage points,CI[-1.1082328623305315, 1.1676282048414646],W/T/L[9, 3, 6]. Neither superiority nor ±.5pp equivalence is demonstrated. Do not label the whole architecture ADDITIVE_REDUNDANT based only on non-significance.
17. **Numerator-only:**posthoc median source gain -11.655243%; no numerator refit or candidate replacement.
18. **Denominator contribution:**constant training-mean denominator median -0.828327%; paired full-minus-constant median -0.409384pp. See source-wise contrasts; aggregate medians alone do not identify a causal contextual benefit.
19. **Near-zero denominator:**0cases. Frozen formula1+sum(nonnegative cells) guaranteesD>=1. No prediction clipping exists; optimization box constraints are not inference clipping.
20. **Train/test denominator drift:**source-median test/train mean ratio 1.000134. Quantiles, Wasserstein distance and support drift are persisted for every split/partition.
21. **Support:**Diabetes minimum-row support averages 5.8876; 18.88%test rows touch a state with<5training examples. Wide MTP also fails despite higher cell counts: parameter-to-row ratio matters, not a universal rarity threshold.
22. **Tails:**RAM_price seed66002 includes target314573, Base2.456million, Rational67.908million withD=1 and support7. This is an univariate bin-residual/target-tail problem, shared almost exactly by the additive control. Diabetes top1%SSE share 9.70% versus Base8.52% cannot explain its broad loss.
23. **Diabetes root cause:**exact cached replay gives train gain 70.0260%,validation-36.8887%,test-45.7370%,MAE-41.3109%. Numerator SD grows 1.1204→1.5439; correction SD 43.4190→61.5606. D mean ratio1.0082, no unseen codes or denominator singularities. RemovingD worsens to-68.7605%; constantD still loses37.9474%. Low-support fitted numerator sums amplify unseen joint combinations; denominator partly damps them but its contextual variation does not repair generalization. This is evidence from interventions, not proof of a unique causal mechanism. All5optimizers hit their inherited stopping limit; no solver rescue was attempted.
24. **Synthetic mechanism results:**12cases×5splits; table below. Conditional interaction and noisy context deteriorate. Positive division/amplitude cases do not constitute real confirmation; the additive-null gain is nearly identical for Rational and Additive.
25. **Oracle gap:**9bounded constructive witnesses match known coded truth to floating-point precision (6.66e-16maximum). A same-class standalone Rational learner is also fit to noisy training targets: source-median excess RMSE over oracle 16.8152%. This matches representation; the Base+residual contrast is NOT a formal oracle for its fixed residual class. For near-zero true denominators/pure and conditional interactions, no bounded witness is supplied; Bayes truth is explicitly not called a Rational oracle. Oracle comparisons change truth knowledge and noise together and are not isolated optimization causal tests.
26. **Stability:**common raw-feature probes are independently remapped using each seed's training vocabulary. Numerator/denominator shape, standardized correction and full prediction correlations are in stability.csv; constant shapes have undefined correlations, not invented perfect stability. Outcome-group summaries are exploratory.
27. **Sample size:**two metadata-largest eligible sources and two smallest; five seeds per size. Table below. Primary50k and full-small contexts were reused. No new source count comes from this scaling. Whole-pipeline affine max normalized discrepancy 4.82674e-10; correction-only maximum absolute discrepancy<8e-12 on selected controlled synthetics. Absolute scale is not the main explanation, though target shape/tails remain relevant.
28. **LightGBM gap:**source-median Base gap -2.583131%→Rational1.702584% (positive=worse). Fraction-recovery is meaningful only where Base is worse; source-specific values retained, not a ratio of aggregate medians.
29. **Fit ratio:**3.8979× with actual fit clocks plus separately replayed shared preparation/residual computation. Raw cached training-only ratio 3.9113×. Timing is exploratory on one host; scaling diagnostics may overlap lightweight audit processing. No historical timing denominator reused for primary.
30. **Inference:**1.3393×Base on encoded inputs; 1.2524× including shared training-vocabulary mapping of schema-normalized raw inputs, seven interleaved repetitions. The latter is a separate post-fit cost replay; no model optimization.
31. **Memory:**serialized 1.2314×,deep 1.1711×,including common category vocabulary. Sampled process peakRSS ratio1.0000× includes inputs/references and is not an isolated model-memory increment.
32. **Verdict:**QUALITY_NOT_CONFIRMED. Quality,risk andspecificity gates all fail under unchanged preregistration.
33. **Close:**this frozen Rational32 Base-residual recipe and its direct public-promotion/cost-optimization path; no favorable-subset rescue.
34. **Keep open:**representability of certain rational coded functions as a mathematical fact; the public frozen Base and previously documented compiler scope remain unchanged. Broad rational-function families are not disproven.
35. **Round67 justified:**NO for cost/runtime optimization or integration of this recipe. Quality/risk prerequisites failed.
36. **Precise next direction:**archive this recipe as failed confirmation. Require a genuinely different, historically screened and falsifiable learning hypothesis before another candidate; no new Round67experiment is started or invented to rescue this result.

## Source-level primary results

For Diabetes, the exact identity is
`MSE(Base+c)-MSE(Base) = mean(c²)-2*mean((y-Base)*c)`.
The held-out correction energy is 3874.37,
while its error-alignment term is only 552.584.
Correction/residual correlation falls from 0.957998
to 0.071566. This directly accounts for increased
error despite a nonsingular, fairly stable denominator. It does not identify
which new learning rule would fix the frozen recipe, and none is tested here.

For RAM_price seed66002, the culprit32-quantile state spans training years1957–1975.
Its7training targets include411,041,792 and67,947,725, alongside values around
400,000. The held-out1974target is314,573. Fitting this heterogeneous cell's
tail-dominated residual average produces the67.9million prediction. This is
coarse-cell heterogeneity plus target-tail sampling; it is not evidence of a
singular denominator. The exact rows are in ram_root_cause.json.

eligible preregistered regimes require at least3sources. Source weighting gives
small datasets the same vote; this panel is relatively small-n heavy. This is a
failure of the prespecified broad confirmation, not proof of harm on all future
datasets. Removing both ordinal sources or the single worst source does not

## Scope, provenance, costs and audit limits

- Panel schema/roles and aliases were frozen before outcomes. Known historical
  aliases were excluded (Fiat500,Sberbank,CPS,SARCOS,Miami,Basildon/Essex,MIP,
  UJI,CT,used cars,avocado and Bengaluru). Potential SoilHydroDB overlap was also
  excluded. Early missing originals are explicitly acknowledged.
- ESL/SWD are ordinal expert assessments from distinct populations. Time/block
  splits estimate grouped interpolation rather than chronological forecasting.
  Measured solver runtimes include timeout effects. These limitations are not
  changed after observing outcomes.
- Numeric strings are parsed by frozen schema. No new imputation, target scale,
  feature engineering, category encoder, denominator floor or fallback was added.
- Optimizer nonconvergence fraction 0.400 is retained,
  not hidden or repaired. It alone does not identify the cause of bad test results.
- Weighted support records both sum(1/D²), the numerator-design curvature,
  and sum(N²/D⁴), the denominator Jacobian curvature, with its Kish effective
  support. Neither is a frequency count. Counts and extreme-row contributions are
  separately retained. Posthoc failure labels are descriptive, unadjusted and
  confounded; they are not a fitted safety gate.
- No novel algorithm claim: ratio/rational-response/contextual modulation overlap
  remains, as does unresolved historical Round17/18 source availability.
- Full model fit includes teacher-free Base and correction. Source acquisition
  and ARFF parsing are excluded from estimator fit; shared train vocabulary and
  residual setup are separately replayed in cost_accounting.csv. Peak RSS is
  sampled every10ms, process-wide, not peak incremental allocation.
- Frozen prediction replay, source hashes, continuity1–66, Round51preservation,
  public core, pytest/Ruff and Git privacy are checked in final_audit.json.
  All Round65 artifacts are preserved. Existing Git working-tree changes are
  not reset. No commit,push,tag orrelease; no Round67execution.
- Test coverage: initial full pytest had371passes,4expected failures and4packaging
  access failures. Authorized offline retries resolved all4packaging tests
  (3read tests, then1isolated install/persistence test with a fresh temporary
  directory). Effective coverage375passes+4expected failures; not misreported as
  one uninterrupted clean run. Round66's7tests and public/private-correctness
  Ruff checks pass. Original failure logs remain. Final compiler/package smoke
  availability differs from the historical blocker cited when omitting optional
  EBM; its comparator is not retroactively added after outcomes.

# Current continuity: ROUND65 completed — rational research candidate frozen

Development tournament complete; rational32 residual passes overall Gate B/C with severe downside; no public promotion. TT frozen learner rejected for now. Round66 not started. Older banners below are historical. Round51 remains explicitly incomplete.

# CODADAPT ROUND65 — Architecture reset and mechanism tournament

**A rational coded residual is the development leader, not a promoted public model.** Its source-median RMSE gain is **3.0091%**, descriptive95%CI **[0.8236, 11.4306]%**, with W/T/L **12/1/3** (±0.1% tie). It passes the frozen overall Gate B/C, narrowly above3%. Diabetes worsens45.737% on average across five splits. Freeze the unchanged32-bin configuration only for proposed independent regression confirmation. Round66 has not started.

## Requested26-point report

1. **Initial families:** twelve: residual VQ, local affine cells, sparse rule dictionary, quadratic factorized memory, Nyström/RBF, adaptive spline basis, non-tree hash router, prototype-conditional additive, sparse projected bins, histogram latent-class density, all-order tensor train and rational coded response. Their representation/training/inference/costs and five prior hypotheses are in the original frozen design.
2. **Historical filter:** ten excluded/deferred for rejected/equivalent/uncertain overlap, including R10 affine, R14 quadratic factors, R17/18 bases, R22 prototypes/dictionaries, R24/32 additive and R60–64 oblique. Original R17/18 sources remain missing; no absolute internal novelty claim. No completed historical experiment rerun.
3. **Shortlist:** two minimal families, not six cosmetic variants. TT rank2/3,8/16 bins,five sweeps; rational16/32 bins,.001 ridge,100 L-BFGS iterations. Fixed before outcomes; both standalone and Base-residual, two capacities.
4. **Prior art:** known tensor trains/Exponential Machines and rational approximation, with fourteen comparison families documented in prior_art.md. No scientific novelty claim, no neural/tree core.
5. **Synthetic oracle:**17 settings×3 seeds, covering16 requested regimes plus rational-context. TT passes eight distinct fitted/constructive mean-function cases; rational passes only via two distinct constructive cases, with no passing finite-solver oracle case. Conditional/heteroscedastic mean duplication counts once. Original finite-oracle failure is retained, not overwritten by constructive success.
6. **Ordinary discovery:**51 regression contexts, both capacities/standalone/residual and shuffled controls. Binary XOR/conditional/categorical fixtures add9 contexts. Every failed and nonconverged fit remains. The oracle gap changes true support and noiseless labels together: not a pure causal discovery effect.
7. **Early closures:** ten families filtered before fitting. Both Gate-A survivors entered the fixed16-source real panel. TT fails the real gate and receives no extra solver rescue; only rational gets conditional attribution and real classification work.
8. **Representation gap:** explicit TT parity and rational-context equations are compact witnesses. They are not general upper bounds. Finite oracle-pair8 bins versus moderateTT16 bins and different bytes confound uniquely higher-order attribution. The constructive parity witness supplies a separate algebraic test.
9. **Discovery/training gap:** TT often collapses to constants on real data; finite-sweep, locally penalized, non-orthogonalized ALS is not a globally optimized TT method. Rational finite oracle also struggles despite a witness. Neither negative result proves the entire mathematical family impossible. No post-outcome solver repair.
10. **Real regression:**16 sources×5 archived splits. Source means are aggregated with equal source weight; test metrics below are development selection evidence. California, SDSS, Diabetes, large and wide/mixed cases all retained. No dataset-specific tuning or cherry-picked subset.
11. **Real classification:** three known sources×5 with the regression-selected rational configuration and Base/LightGBM. Squared probability-residual training; clipping only for logloss. This is exploratory compatibility, not a claim that the classifier should change. Source results below.
12. **Stability:** fixed all-feature structure has Jaccard1 by construction, not learned selection stability. Correction predictions compared on common unlabelled source rows across five seeds, with category identity remapping. Constant outputs have undefined correlation and are counted. Source seed consistency is reported separately; overlap of splits limits independence.
13. **Memory:** full deployment includes Base for residual models and external category vocabulary. Actual bytes, not parameter count, determine capacity flags. Some moderateTT cases exceed2x and are high-capacity diagnostics. Deep estimates are Python-retained traversal, not complete native LightGBM C-heap/RSS; serialized size is the reproducible reference.
14. **Fit:** historical Base/LightGBM times reused; new learner fit includes its encoding, and reconstructed totals add residual initialization. These are exploratory cross-date ratios, not controlled same-session speed claims. Diagnostic fixed-readout costs conservatively include the parent rational training.
15. **Inference:** five new warm repetitions on complete predictors and identical heldout prepared inputs, single thread. Raw upstream category conversion excluded equally. No independent hardware or release performance claim.
16. **LightGBM:** Base source-median relative RMSE gap is18.7626%; leader gap is20.0645%. The marginal median gap is not clearly reduced even though most paired Base comparisons improve; medians of differently normalized distributions are not interchangeable. LightGBM remains a strong reference.
17. **Residual signal:** leader source-median residual MSE reduction is5.9034%. Compare with main/pair controls, TT shuffled and matched rational shuffled controls. Contribution energy is not called explained signal.
18. **Failure cases:** Diabetes deteriorates45.737% on average, all5seeds; Bodyfat and Melbourne also have negative source means. Worst split and variability are in real_source_summary.csv. True NYC Taxi Fare remains EXTERNAL_UNVERIFIED; green-taxi tips is a different task.
19. **Success cases:** California+4.449%, Diamonds+13.974%, Superconduct+13.909%, House sales+6.018%, SGEMM+11.431%, with5/5positive splits for these sources. Pyrim's large mean has high variance and is not the promotion basis. Removing the best source leaves a positive median but below3%; the frozen rule required survival abovezero, not a second3% threshold.
20. **Best candidate:** unchanged rational32 Base-residual. It alone passes the overall real gate among the eight original candidate arms. No ablation or Joint row may replace it based on these results. Selected-development intervals are not multiplicity-adjusted.
21. **Mechanistic difference:** output division by a shared, positive context denominator is nonseparable, unlike another additive sum or projected1Dmemory. TT's all-order chain differs from R14 quadratic pair sums. Both are established mathematics applied to coded states.
22. **Risks:** severe small-sample downside, finite optimizer convergence, denominator estimation, convenience development panel, selected configuration, lack of exact byte equality, historical baseline fit timings and no fresh confirmation. The3% threshold is passed only narrowly; no public promotion.
23. **Close:** frozenTT learner for now (TRAINING_LIMIT, broad representation INCONCLUSIVE), TT solver/rank sweeps, and historical oblique/pair-admission/confidence/artificial-splitting/Joint rescue lines. Rational stays one bounded research branch; no per-dataset routing.
24. **RETRY_IF:** TT requires a genuinely different, justified identifiable learning principle that recovers existing constructive witnesses at fixed bytes under irrelevant-feature/null controls before real data. Not more passes or favorable splits. Rational may continue only unchanged into independent confirmation with explicit downside accounting; failure closes this recipe.
25. **Freeze:** architecture,32bins,.001ridge,100iterations,nonnegative denominator[0,8],all-feature train-only coding,Base-residual combination in leading_candidate_freeze.json. This is a research freeze, not an API/default change. Conditional ablations are diagnostic only.
26. **Proposed Round66, not started:** FROZEN REGRESSION CONFIRMATION of that exact candidate on genuinely independent sources, with Base, fixedLightGBM and matched32-bin additive/unit-denominator control. Preregister source-level uncertainty, downside reporting and fixed capacity; no tuning, gating, optimizer repair, classifier change or Joint substitution. Specifically falsify whether contextual division transfers beyond an equally resolved additive correction. If not, close this candidate rather than reopening the direct-additive line.

| unit_denominator_value_refit | 1.96834 | 1.01265 | 5.09998 | 14 | -58.5326 | 0.267099 | -0.00146942 | 3.842 | 4.65405 | 2.10484 | 1.33746 |

The median paired advantage of the gated candidate over the32-bin unit-denominator additive value refit is0.2671 percentage points of Base RMSE, CI[-0.0015,3.8420]. Positive favors the rational denominator. This comparison is exploratory attribution on the same panel, not a second confirmation. Fixed-denominator readout isolates numerator value estimation approximately; its penalized intercept differs from the original objective, so it is not a certified optimum under an identical penalty. No discovery algorithm exists beyond train-only per-feature coding; capacity/ordinary-versus-oracle contrasts cover that limitation without inventing a discovery-only module.

Main-Base ablation removes native interaction tables from a copy of the already fitted Base; it does not retrain a separately optimized main-only Base. C and D (without native pairs / with Base main effects) are the same intervention here. The joint diagnostic refits a fixed readout over Base cells and rational numerator features with denominator frozen; it is not a tuned historical Joint rescue. Both its provenance and costs are explicit. Noise control uses the same rational estimator on permuted training residuals. No best ablation is substituted for the frozen candidate.

**Attribution remains partial:** the paired rational-versus-additive interval includes zero. The overall Base gain passes the preregistered gate, but the specifically non-additive advantage is not established. This is the main falsifier for the proposed confirmation. Refitting numerator values with a fixed denominator changes performance negligibly; removing ridge increases downside; the diagnostic Joint readout does not earn promotion or replace the leader. These are bounded comparisons of frozen arms, not causal identification of a universal failure source.

## Resume and audit

The interruption left1,020completed model checkpoints,1,224oracle metric rows and1,836ordinary synthetic metric rows. Resume audit replayed saved models/predictions bitwise and preserved1,960existing files; no interrupted fit required rerun. The missing real/attribution/classification phases were added with atomic caches. The preliminary negative-report template was never executed: the completed real panel passed the rational gate and triggered the conditional work. No negative conclusion was carried forward from partial results.

The gate's positive outcome is preserved despite downside; no posthoc safety threshold is used to falsify it. Likewise the result is not called confirmation. All old hash-frozen files, valid checkpoints and historical gaps remain intact. Public core/API/default/compiler unchanged. No commit/push/tag/release. research_private is ignored, continuity documents trackable. Existing Git changes were preserved. R51 retains its six explicit unfinished diagnostics; original R17/18 remain unavailable.

See verification.json and final_audit.json for actual replay/test results. Available public Ruff and research-specific checks are separate; frozen private import/style exceptions are disclosed. Optional EBM/packaging has an inherited local environment limitation, not a green full-release claim. Round66 is proposed only and not started.

## 1. Project identity

> **Continuity note:** this file and `RESEARCH_STATE.json` are intentionally versioned so a new Codex/session can reconstruct the project without the private experiment tree. Links to `research_private/` are historical source references from the original development workspace and may be unavailable in a clean clone; the round summaries below preserve the decisions, metrics and reopen conditions needed to continue safely.


CodAdapt, versione locale **0.2.0rc1** (candidata interna non pubblicata; core nativo **0.1.0** invariato), è una libreria sperimentale CPU per dati tabellari: memorie compatte indicizzate da codici e residual corrections, con un budget di lookup. Non è un albero né una rete. Non è dimostrata superiorità generale sui boosting.

API sklearn-style: `CodAdapt` è alias di `CodAdaptClassifier`; `CodAdaptRegressor` supporta regressione single-target. `fit`, `predict`, `predict_proba` per classificazione binaria, `get_params`/`set_params`, cloning/pipeline/CV, sample weights, eval_set, random_state e pickle/joblib. **Multiclass nativo non supportato**: i dataset originariamente multiclass dei benchmark sono stati binarizzati. La versione è verificata nel codice locale, non una verifica dello stato di pubblicazione remoto.

## 2. Current public core

**R42:** il namespace opzionale `codadapt.experimental` è ora implementato; vedere [contratto pubblico](../EBM_COMPILER.md). Non sostituisce il core descritto sotto. Il solo file pre-esistente modificato in src è `_version.py`; gli altri7 hash sono invariati.


Percorso effettivo verificato in `_base.py`, `_training.py`, `preprocessing.py`:

```text
raw DataFrame / dense numeric array
→ validazione input, pesi, split training/validation
→ preprocessing nativo train-only (quantili/categorie/missing)
→ shared finest encoding e mappe intere coarse-to-fine
→ fino a tre livelli di adaptive coded memory
→ main effects selezionati sul residuo
→ poche interaction memories a codici fissi
→ somma ordinata con intercetta; sigmoid per binary / score per regression
```

Al default `n_bins=32`, il finest effettivo è `min(16,n_bins)`: livelli **4/8/16**, non 32 bucket effettivi. Quantili float64 su massimo 20k training rows; `searchsorted(side="right")`; missing numerico separato. Categoriche: top 128, rare osservate/missing/unseen distinti e membership delle categorie viste; schema train-only. Una singola trasformazione fine alimenta mappe di livello, non tre fit separati.

Intercetta iniziale media/log-odds pesati, poi fissa. Budget totale default 24 lookup, ripartito tra livelli; circa 2/3 dei posti ai main. Prefiltro deterministico fino 512 righe, screening e feature stopping permanente. Tabelle ridge aggregate via `bincount`, valore B/(A+l2), l2 default 5. Classificazione usa il surrogato con curvatura globale 1/4. Le coppie vengono proposte da ranking univariato; codici interi pseudocasuali **non riadattati dal trainer corrente**; indirizzo somma-modulo, table_size default 256. Utility penalizza collisioni. Un bucket non supportato azzera l’interazione in prediction; celle vuote zero. Una tabella su una sola feature conta come main effect, non interazione.

Validation MSE/logloss non pesata ammette un livello solo oltre soglia relativa; al primo livello rifiutato termina. Non è il vecchio training iterativo a patience. `max_iter`, `adapt_codes`, `adapt_every`, `max_code_updates`, `n_candidates`, `min_code_count`, `patience` sono in gran parte parametri legacy validati ma non controllano questo percorso; `features_per_table` influenza stima RAM, non la coppia effettiva. Vedere audit per caveat esatti. Non usare timer legacy lasciati zero come profilazione reale. Nessun backfitting globale di default.

**Discrepanza documentale importante:** [docs/ALGORITHM.md](../ALGORITHM.md) descrive anche un motore legacy con coordinate di riindirizzamento, intercetta aggiornata e iterazioni non eseguite dal trainer pubblico attuale. Per ricostruire il comportamento hanno precedenza codice + [audit R7](../../research_private/round7_audit.md). Non correggere automaticamente codice/API per farli coincidere con quel documento.

**Pubblico e congelato:** tutto `src/codadapt/`, classifier, regressor, encoder, interaction engine, API/default v0.1.0. Da R42 il compiler EBM opzionale è integrato nel namespace pubblico experimental; le varianti di ricerca restano private e non sostituiscono il default. Limite preventivo 512MiB del fit è stima conservativa, non tetto RSS del processo.

Convenzioni: AUC gain = candidato−Base; RMSE gain =100×(1−candidato/Base), positivo migliore. Gap RMSE verso un riferimento =100×(modello/riferimento−1), positivo peggiore. Recovery teacher è la somma dei gain sui dataset teacher-positive, non il rapporto delle mediane né una misura universale di fidelity.
