Fourteen unique upstream refits per context share half fits between HALF/CF2 and bootstraps with OOB;
do not add those shared fits twice. Whole fits count private discovery/base, original replay, native
Base and two R48 controls. Subtable estimates are additional aggregate computations, not whole fits.
Fit charge includes existing encoder/planning plus original fixed-table fit, projection and confidence
metrics; all eight diagnostic metrics are computed, so this is not an optimized production cost.
Primary regression fit ratios: REUSE1.216x, CF2 1.228x, CF5 1.255x, independent3 1.427x; R48 component
1.094x. Bootstrap normal-regression1.797x/classification2.090x. Statistical confidence work dominates
the very cheap upstream refits. Temporary arrays are explicit prefix/draw bytes, NOT peak RSS;
projection scratch is separate (median1,820,804B). Whole diagnostic cache can hold several protocols
at once. Allocator/BLAS scratch is not counted, so do not interpret these as a peak-memory guarantee.

All private deployed models keep identical serialized bytes, cells and lookup count within context;
deep ratio medians1.0. Predict code is identical: confidence estimators are discarded. Three warm
timing repeats per model (1/up-to1000 rows) are recorded; some classification/OOB ratios are noisy
and exceed1.2. No inference speedup or stable latency-equivalence guarantee is inferred from this
microbenchmark. Fit/native-Base ratios and per-case costs are preserved in CSV.

## 9. Decision and falsifiable next experiment

- Residual reuse universally inflates reliability: **REJECTED_FOR_NOW**, conditional on frozen selection.
- Independent estimates better predict utility: **PARTIAL** (Pearson improves, rank/quality not reliably).
- CF5 necessary for shrink quality: **REJECTED_FOR_NOW**; nearly identical to REUSE at additional cost.
- CF2 weighted-alpha rule: **REJECTED_FOR_NOW**; lower harmful rate trades away useful correction.
- Bootstrap raw shape stability: **REJECTED_FOR_NOW** as an independent-confidence proxy (overlap).
- Whole-table shrink sufficient everywhere: **PARTIAL**; simple and competitive, not uniformly best.
- Independent component shrink: **PARTIAL**, small synthetic improvement, no real Base advantage.
- Selection bias removed / real-data replacement: **INCONCLUSIVE**, not tested independently.

**Answer:** independent residual stability is not shown necessary for deciding alpha; generic reuse
shrinkage captures much of the gain. Separate components have conditional value, not a universal
mandate. The useful signal may be honest marginal predictive utility rather than parameter agreement.

**Proposed Round50, NOT RUN:** one preregistered train-only whole-table alpha derived from honest
three-way marginal loss/energy, against REUSE weighted alpha, matched-sample placebo and R48
sequential shared shrink. Keep structure and correction fixed; match pair sample mass/ridge and
report the conditional-selection limitation. First falsify on product/XOR, weak/noisy, additive,
wide and the same three real controls. Stop unless incremental paired utility survives and exceeds
native Base on real controls at justified cost. No new ranking/admission search, broad tuning,
architecture, public change or confirmation is authorized by this proposal.

Remaining: discovery conditioning; sample-mass vs residual-independence confounding; pure-component
identifiability under collisions; correlation-to-alpha calibration; frozen versus sequential residual
updates; only three known real datasets. Do not reopen closed directions without new evidence.

## 10. Audit and reproduction

`run49.py --panel synthetic`, then `--panel collision`, then `--panel known` (completed runs resume);
`analyze49.py`, `synthesize49.py`, `verify49.py`, `finalize49.py`, `seal49.py`.
Do not rerun the one-shot `audit49.py` after memory update. Primary files/design are hash-frozen.
17 private unit tests PASS; Ruff PASS. All17,680 saved-model metrics reconstructed;260 fixed-capacity
groups;2,600 selector checks; maximum metric error0; table reconstruction3.55e-15. No public runtime
tests were changed. Prior/public hashes, JSON/history1–49 and Git privacy checked in final seal.
Raw runs, joined utility table, per-case paired CIs, cross-seed shapes, all alternative modes and
confidence costs are local under `research_private/round49/`. No heldout oracle is deployable.


## Round 50 — One marginal-utility signal to alpha (2026-09-22)


10,145 model evaluations in 260 contexts;
2,865 fit executions = 785 whole-model
fits (260 laboratory discovery,260 original-table replay,260 public Base,5 fixed HGB)
plus 2080 upstream refits. 1040 of the evaluations
reuse R49 saved correction tables, with no old confidence fitting. 87,210
pair records and 11,475 confidence records saved. Scalar/grid oracle
selection and subtable statistics are separately diagnosed, not miscounted as whole fits.

40 normal configurations x5 seeds, plus6 x5 x2 address controls. Same seeds44001–44005,
same outer splits and all R48/R49 diagnostic families: pure/difficult mains, product strong/weak,
XOR/noisy, mixtures, correlations, wide, categorical/missing, uniform/imbalanced support,
n400/1600/6400 and collisions. Primary summary has20 families (18regression,2classification).
Strength/size/noise repeats are separate strata, not extra independent datasets.
Known development has8 configurations, **only3 real**; no synthetic result is called real.

Private Base = strict C1U1P4 experimental laboratory, original pair model. Alpha0 is its
main-only ablation. Neither is the native public Base. Public Base was fitted afresh with
the same outer validation and matches R49 saved predictions bitwise. Archived original,
REUSE whole,R48 sequential whole/component,independent3 component replay within1e-12.
R48 refits downstream residuals after shrink; current methods only scale original final
tables. This is an explicit procedural difference, not a hidden baseline substitution.

## 2. Equal-count controls and independence

Three disjoint deterministic thirds, m=floor(n_train/3). Every matched comparison uses
m upstream observations, m pair-value observations and m utility-assessment observations,
three rotations and six subtable estimates per slot. At most2 rows are trimmed only from
confidence estimation. Original final corrections always retain all training data.
REUSE_MATCHED upstream uses pair-fit A; INDEPENDENT_MATCHED uses disjoint H, then pairs
fit/evaluate symmetrically on A/B. CROSS5_MATCHED excludes each row's own fold and limits
every upstream fit to m rows. All row hashes, counts and overlap checks are saved.

REUSE_FULL uses full-training upstream and half-sized pair/assessment sets; PREFIX_MATCHED
keeps full upstream but uses the same thirds. Equal-count independence is therefore directly
measured, while the upstream-mass bridge **also changes overlap** and is not a pure causal
sample-size intervention. Cross-fitting still permits cross-fold label connections. All
protocols condition on discovery/encoder fitted using outer training: no unconditional
selection-independence claim. Independent comparison gains exclude both A/B labels from H.

Paired primary regression contrasts, positive = RMSE reduction of arm versus reference:

| contrast | median | mean | ci_low | ci_high |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| pair_assessment_mass | -0.110927 | -0.378426 | -0.786334 | -0.00560256 |
| upstream_mass_and_overlap | -0.36489 | -0.995062 | -1.68562 | -0.426954 |
| equal_mass_independence | 1.16076 | 1.34873 | 0.341088 | 2.45775 |
| equal_mass_crossfit | 0.0143817 | 0.689364 | 0.122418 | 1.31904 |

Independence at equal mass: **+1.160757% median**, mean
1.348728% CI[0.341088,2.457745].
But versus full-budget utility REUSE: **-0.268744% median**, mean
+0.048871% CI[-0.364793,0.513678]. Independence is not merely
a sample-count artifact, yet it is not shown necessary at an unrestricted reuse budget.
The primary classification contrast has only2 related synthetic families; do not generalize it.

## 3. Signal, map lock and predictive gains

c = average over3 rotations of 1[g(A→B)>0 AND g(B→A)>0]. Gains are loss reductions
normalized by their own pre-correction loss. Final table = c * original table. One scalar
per slot, no component split, capacity/lookup change, new discovery, admission or final refit.
Positive directional agreement is the primary signal, not an optimized ensemble of metrics.

The four preregistered maps were compared on primary synthetic VALIDATION only. Normalized
validation-loss averages: continuous .903012, keep/drop .917515, positive-mean gain .913257,
piecewise .904327. **Continuous c was locked before the known panel**; test outcomes did
not change it. `selected_rule.json` and source hashes preserve that lock. Alternatives stay
diagnostic, never become per-dataset fallbacks.

## 5. Oracles and validation selection

Continuous alpha has analytic bounded squared-loss optimum and deterministic logistic bisection.
Per-pair is one coordinate sweep from the original model, **not an exhaustive upper bound**.
Global is one alpha; keep/drop and component use frozen R49 finite-grid selectors. All test
oracles are diagnostic and cannot be deployed. Separate validation versions use validation only.

| kind | reference | median | mean | ci_low | ci_high |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| global | validation_global | 0.0847529 | 0.140866 | 0.0761101 | 0.222721 |
| global | INDEPENDENT_MATCHED_continuous | 0.427374 | -0.920112 | -2.0555 | 0.0701243 |
| per_pair | validation_per_pair | 1.34343 | 1.57422 | 1.35434 | 1.87178 |
| per_pair | INDEPENDENT_MATCHED_continuous | 1.7677 | 1.91746 | 1.62455 | 2.25769 |
| keep_drop | validation_keep_drop | 1.52934 | 1.57657 | 1.27398 | 1.91879 |
| keep_drop | INDEPENDENT_MATCHED_continuous | 1.32815 | 1.44083 | 1.18793 | 1.72376 |
| component | validation_component | 2.00802 | 2.11387 | 1.85065 | 2.42703 |
| component | INDEPENDENT_MATCHED_continuous | 3.17011 | 3.57307 | 2.8332 | 4.41705 |

Continuous per-pair oracle gap versus utility-alpha1.767702% paired median; component3.170109%.
Validation→test oracle selection gap: per-pair1.343427%, component2.008022%, global.084753%.
Greater selector freedom still overfits validation. A global oracle need not dominate a
per-table heuristic: these are different feasible classes. Component oracle is also greedy,
so neither is a mathematical ceiling on all possible corrections.

collided table may have positive conditional marginal utility while the whole model remains
badly biased: utility is not structural fidelity. Direct controls run only where grid fits256;
fallbacks logged. Operational add/joint energy is diagnostic, not pure decomposition under aliases.

## 7. Real development and public Base

Selected rule vs public Base, paired five-seed mean and descriptive bootstrap95%CI:

| case | mean | ci_low | ci_high | wins | ties | losses | mean_lab | classification | lab_recovery |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| r44_breast_cancer | -0.000132275 | -0.000859788 | 0.000529101 | 2 | 1 | 2 | 0.000198413 | PUBLIC_BASE_NEUTRAL | False |
| r44_diabetes | -1.82558 | -5.13875 | 1.37942 | 3 | 0 | 2 | 3.91571 | PUBLIC_BASE_NEUTRAL | True |
| r44_wine_binary | 0.000694444 | 0 | 0.00208333 | 1 | 4 | 0 | 0 | PUBLIC_BASE_NEUTRAL | False |

Diabetes improves laboratory RMSE3.915715% (CI[3.085962,4.745467]) but is **1.825577% worse**
than native Base on mean, CI[-5.138750,+1.379421] for gain. Three of5seed wins do not establish
reproducibility. This is LAB_RECOVERY; PUBLIC_BASE_NEUTRAL means insufficient evidence,
not equivalence. Breast cancer -.000132AUC, wine+.000694 with1win/4ties; wine's gain already
exists in the original laboratory. No genuine real public win among any of the four mappings;
positive-mean keep/drop is a PUBLIC_BASE_LOSS on Diabetes (-4.052679%,4/5losses).

The public-win gate requires mean≥.001AUC or≥1%RMSE, CI lower>0,≥4/5wins. No model passes.
Synthetic public improvements (selected rule median15.482998% regression) do not substitute
for this real-data condition. All per-case lab/public labels are saved.

## 8. REAL_WORLD_FAILURE_CASES coverage

No old external raw regression datasets are present in this canonical repository. Round7–43
historical conclusions remain authoritative summaries, not locally reproducible new measurements.
Fixed HGB on locally available Diabetes was preregistered as the only regression stress
reference. Public Base/HGB RMSE disadvantage averages **-1.404553%**,
CI[-6.257034,5.326154]; Base loses only1/5splits.
Thus Diabetes does **not** qualify as a clear Base-behind-tree failure case. It was not replaced
after observing results. Coverage remains incomplete; no external leaderboard or downloaded
data is silently mixed into the controlled experiment. No claim of success on a real strong
tree-booster failure is possible. This is the next evidence gap, not justification for more tuning.

The laboratory pipeline itself is expensive: selected real regression total fit5.425834x

are distinct. Archived R48/R49 comparator **fit** seconds are historical and flagged;
cross-session ratios are not a controlled proof that the new rule is cheaper. New inference
was remeasured in interleaved5-repeat warm loops, batch1 and up-to1000rows (actual rows logged).
No production latency guarantee is claimed from these microbenchmarks.

## 10. Hypotheses and decision

- **H1** SUPPORTED_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY: positive marginal-gain agreement predicts original-pair utility in pooled synthetics; not calibrated on real regression
- **H2** PARTIAL: whole-table utility alpha recovers complex-shrink laboratory benefit, no consistent dominance over R48 components
- **H3** PARTIAL_NOT_SOLE_CAUSE: equal-mass independence helps relative to matched reuse, but does not beat full-budget reuse; sample/overlap changes matter
- **H4** SUPPORTED_ON_AVAILABLE_REAL_PANEL: Diabetes LAB_RECOVERY, no reproducible public Base gain on any of three real datasets
- **H5** SUPPORTED_FOR_CURRENT_RULE_TRANSFER_ONLY: real calibration unstable; insufficient as a general improvement rule, not proof that every confidence mechanism must fail

**Can one signal guide laboratory shrinkage? PARTIALLY. Does it improve public CodAdapt
reproducibly on real data? NO. Architectural candidate? NO.** Criteria1–2 have synthetic
support; criterion3 is only met against original, not matched/full reuse; criterion4–5 fail
on available real evidence; incremental cost is small but total laboratory/public cost is large.

Stability/confidence estimation is useful for understanding pair-memory, but is not yet a
sufficient principle for improving CodAdapt. Close this mapping family for now: no more
threshold search, confidence metric catalogs, component recombinations or confirmation to
rescue it. Keep the raw pair diagnostics and the conditional matched-independence observation.

**RETRY_IF:** first obtain a reproducible local real regression failure versus a fixed booster;
identify new falsifiable evidence for train-only utility calibration on that case, and require
paired improvement over both full-budget REUSE and native Base with justified total cost.
Changing alpha thresholds on these observed seeds is not a reopen condition.

**Proposed Round51, NOT RUN:** REAL_WORLD_FAILURE_CASES audit with user-provided/locally
available known raw data, fixed splits and preprocessing, frozen public Base and fixed booster;
decompose a verified real gap before proposing any learning rule. No architectural confirmation
or integration is justified now. Independent confidence is not universally disproved; its
unconditional selection bias, sparse-support transfer and real calibration remain unresolved.

## 11. Audit and reproduction

`run50.py --panel synthetic`, `select50.py` (one-shot global lock), `run50.py --panel known`,
`run50.py --panel collision`, `analyze50.py`, `diagnose50.py`, `verify50.py`, `finalize50.py`,
`seal50.py`. Finished contexts resume without refit. Do not rerun one-shot audit/selection on
sealed evidence. The selection script reads validation columns only; source frozen before runs.

12 private tests PASS, including third/fold label exclusion, equal counts, scalar optima and
immutable tables. Ruff PASS. All10,145 saved-model metrics recomputed;260 matched/capacity
groups;7,540 alpha/table reconstructions. Maximum metric round-trip error
9.09e-13. Prior/public/source hashes checked:5353.
No public runtime code or tests changed; both research-memory files updated losslessly,
continuous index1–50, valid JSON, private artifacts ignored and docs trackable.


## Pending Round 51 — Partial real-data failure audit (2026-09-22)


**IN PROGRESS / REQUIRED DEPENDENCY BLOCKED. Not a completed Round51.**
LightGBM is absent from the canonical environment and no usable local wheel/library was
found in the searched Desktop, Downloads, cache and Python environments. No download or
remote operation was attempted. A local package/environment path was requested. All
booster numbers below refer explicitly to **HistGradientBoosting (HGB), a secondary
control**, not LightGBM. Do not relabel them or close the requested audit on this basis.

## 1. Panel, integrity and counts

Eight real-data tasks, seven sources, five paired60/20/20 random splits: California
Housing20,640×9, Auto MPG398×7, SDSS99,999×5 (redshift regression and STAR/rest binary),
Diabetes442×10, Adult48,842×14, breast cancer569×30, wine binary178×13. SDSS tasks share
one source and are not independent datasets. Only ugriz are predictors; no ID/redshift/
class-derived fields. Local source provenance/hashes are in `round51/inventory.json`.
External provenance was not reverified. No Taxi data found; no500k-row experiment.

**NYC Taxi Fare:** user-reported approximately500k rows, CodAdapt5.26990,
LightGBM3.68943, metric unspecified: **EXTERNAL_UNVERIFIED**, never an internal result.

200 panel model fits +760 diagnostic model fits = **960 fitted/evaluated models**;
2,880 scored train/validation/test partitions. Aggregate per-pair value estimates are
internal to model fits, not extra independent fits. 460 sparse solves, zero iteration
limit exits (numerical tolerances do not establish statistical optimality).
All960 saved prediction metrics reconstructed, maximum discrepancy0; five private
diagnostic tests pass. Sixteen public source files unchanged by SHA256. R50's1,606-file
seal verified before work. Prior rounds were not rerun. Public Ruff check passes.
Private frozen research scripts retain cosmetic import lint issues; not a claim that
the entire private tree passes Ruff. No public algorithm/API/compiler/default changed.

## 2. Verified secondary-booster gaps

Positive numbers mean **HGB improves over Base**; regression100×(1-RMSE_HGB/RMSE_Base).
Five-seed means and descriptive bootstrap95% CIs. Overlapping random splits are not
independent external replication; intervals omit dataset selection uncertainty.

| Task | HGB gain | 95% CI | HGB wins |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| California |19.2116% RMSE|18.5246,19.7879|5/5|
| Auto MPG |6.3461% RMSE|-0.5613,13.4571|4/5|
| SDSS redshift |4.2979% RMSE|3.9141,4.6224|5/5|
| Diabetes |-5.1911% RMSE|-9.3392,-1.1263|1/5|
| Adult |+.027120 AUC|.026734,.027563|5/5|
| SDSS STAR |+.080443 AUC|.077419,.083567|5/5|
| Breast cancer |+.006680 AUC|.003108,.010251|5/5|
| Wine |+.000694 AUC|0,.002083|1/5;4ties|

Only **two clearly replicated regression failures against this fixed HGB**; Auto MPG
is weaker evidence, not silently promoted to a third robust failure. Diabetes and wine
are controls competitive with this HGB setting, not universal winners. Diabetes linear
control improves3.8651% versus Base; competition depends on the reference.

## 3. Main effects and existing pair memories

EBM fixed additive improves California1.8816% RMSE, Auto MPG1.9062%, Diabetes3.0491%,
but worsens SDSS redshift2.7737%. Adult improves+.026341AUC, almost the HGB+.027120gap;
breast cancer+.011971AUC. SDSS STAR EBM additive is **.043792AUC below Base** despite
HGB being.080443 above it. Thus no single universal main-effect explanation fits the panel.

EBM main + **same public Base pair identities/codes/addresses**, with residual values
refitted on training only, improves California7.4861% [6.8397,8.0916], SDSS.2735%
[.1515,.3679], Auto MPG2.8327% [-.6444,6.8390], Diabetes2.8572% [.4775,4.9612].
This changes the additive scaffold and residual value fitting, not the public code.
Main-only public ablation refits with n_tables=0 and reallocates the lookup budget;
it is not a strict removal of frozen pair outputs. Do not treat its difference as pure
causal interaction contribution. The separate EBM-scaffold comparisons keep mains fixed.

For California, mean paired **MSE gap closure** versus HGB is10.61% for EBM alone,
41.48% for EBM+existing refitted pairs,36.36% for EBM+all collision-free16-bin pairs.
These are nested intervention summaries, not additive causal fractions. SDSS closure
is-68.06%,6.53%,29.43% respectively: better additive-only fitting is insufficient there.
Closure fractions on Auto MPG/Diabetes have unstable or negative denominators and should
not be headline shares. Raw scores and denominator-valid seed counts are retained.

## 4. Resolution, capacity and optimization

**Public n_bins16/64/512 all give bitwise-identical predictions to default n_bins32**
on the20 regression contexts. `_base.py` explicitly caps finest bins at16. There are
three4/8/16 levels. A requested public n_bins increase is not a genuine resolution test.

Separate private collision-free additive grids8..512 jointly fit all coefficients;
they change representation and fitting, and are not proposed replacements. Ridge20 is
fixed per coefficient: higher resolution also changes effective regularization/support.
No independent resolution-only causal claim is possible. Finer is not monotonically
better:512-bin gain versus Base California-21.77%, SDSS-5.21%, Auto MPG-37.23%,
Diabetes-24.37%. This **does not prove fine shapes cannot represent the target**.
Learned shape MSE versus centered EBM contributions, support, feature ablation gain,
train/validation/test residual error are in `feature_resolution.csv`.

Same original public states/selected terms/codes/addresses/intercept, joint least-squares
values with public lambda5: California **+3.8474%** [3.2761,4.5486], SDSS **+1.0543%**
[.9836,1.1196]; Auto MPG+.1783% and Diabetes-.3126%, both inconclusive. No larger
representation was needed for that partial recovery: direct evidence of a **TRAINING_LIMIT
component**, not proof that optimization explains the full gap. ApproximateMSEgap
closure21.68% California/25.02% SDSS. Selected structure still conditions this result.

lookup_budget96 gains California1.6201%, SDSS.2956%; table_size4096 gains1.0920%
(CI crosses0) and.2745%. Auto MPG/Diabetes may worsen. Capacity is partly useful,
not a demonstrated general cure. Changing table size changes hash addresses as well
as capacity; it is not a pure collision intervention. All one-axis curves are retained.

## 5. Continuous and higher-order structure

16-knot clamped interpolation/splines as full additive controls do not beat Base.
Added to Base residual, interpolation gains California1.7967%, SDSS.0774%; splines
1.5243%/.0754%. These are train-fitted correction proxies, not a pure measurement of
quantization error. The observed large gaps are not mostly removed by these controls.

Nested collision-free4-bin pairs → pairs+triples → pairs+triples+quadruples on fixed EBM
mains reduce California trainingRMSE51,578→49,304→46,277 while test worsens
53,268→53,470→54,274. Auto MPG test2.685→2.728→2.794; Diabetes54.58→56.62→56.84.
SDSS test.59066→.58960→.58970: small partial improvement, still below Base/HGB.
At most40 terms/order, lexicographic cap; not exhaustive high-order search or proof of
an interaction-order ceiling. They document **GENERALIZATION_LIMIT** of these controls.

## 6. Teacher approximation and residual spectrum

The available teacher here is HGB, **LightGBM teacher diagnostics remain UNRUN**.
Train on teacher predictions, evaluate teacher NMSE separately from true-targetRMSE:

| Task | Public Base student NMSE | Additive16 | Main+all pairs16 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| California |.11157|.14511|.08063|
| SDSS redshift |.12349|.22282|.09449|
| Auto MPG |.04122|.12614|.08519|
| Diabetes |.24846|.31801|.32598|

These limited ridge students do not exactly reproduce the booster; no proof that every
coded representation fails. All-pair teacher approximation improves on two larger
failure cases, but worse outcomes on small controls warn against universal prescriptions.
Local residual diagnostics record univariate heldout MSE reduction, conditional variance,
roughness, curvature, rank monotonicity, largest jump, support/density imbalance and
index-space Fourier concentration. The latter is **not evidence of physical periodicity**.
Correlated features and fitted residuals prevent assigning mutually exclusive percentages
to main/pair/high-order/noise. No fabricated residual spectrum percentages are reported.

## 7. Sample size and failure taxonomy

SDSS HGB advantage grows with train size5k/10k/25k/50k:
**-.9954%,+1.6704%,+3.5211%,+4.4721%**. California5k→10k:16.9601%→19.1236%.
Same heldout rows, nested train subsets; outer-training categorical vocabulary held fixed.
The larger-booster advantage with more labels supports a capacity/training-scaling concern.
It does not identify its cause alone.100k/250k/500k training sizes unavailable, not resampled
or fabricated. Scaling train scores are over the full outer-training partition, not only
the smaller fitted subset; n_fit is explicitly recorded.

| Case | Supported provisional label | Still unresolved |
|---|---|---|
| California |TRAINING_LIMIT partial; DEFAULT_LIMIT partial; GENERALIZATION_LIMIT for high-order controls|representation ceiling, allocation/discovery vs estimation remainder|
| SDSS redshift |TRAINING_LIMIT partial; DEFAULT_LIMIT small; sample scaling concern|joint geometry/representation vs learner remainder|
| Auto MPG |GENERALIZATION_LIMIT of tested high-capacity controls|robust booster-failure status; small-n variance|
| Adult |main-effect bottleneck; training vs representation UNRESOLVED|same-state classification optimization not executed|
| SDSS STAR |joint-function bottleneck; subtype UNRESOLVED|pair/order/local conditional allocation|
| Breast cancer |main-effect weakness, subtype UNRESOLVED|small-n/default-feature-budget contribution|
| Diabetes/Wine |competitive controls versus fixed HGB only|no universal niche claim|

Numeric-only SDSS fails despite p=5 and no missing: neither categorical handling nor
wide dimensionality is necessary for failure. Adult's additive control succeeds despite
categoricals/missing. The tiny positive controls do not establish a meta-learning law.
Seven sources are too few for stable feature-characteristic correlations.
COMPUTATIONAL_LIMIT not established: timings overlap concurrent local jobs, sparse fit_s
excludes design construction, and complete retained-memory/inference costs for all private
controls were not measured. No systems promotion/cost claim from these timings.

## 8. What is closed, what remains, and Round52

Confidence→alpha remains CLOSED. No new mapping, threshold, admission or public candidate.
Do not spend another round on indiscriminate resolution increases or merely adding dense
higher-order tables without new evidence addressing their observed generalization failure.
No REPRESENTATION_LIMIT theorem/ceiling established; finite diagnostic failure is insufficient.

**Provisional hypothesis only:** the missing capability is task-dependent allocation and
estimation of joint local structure, together with an additive training weakness on some
classification tasks; it is not one universal noisy-pair shrinkage problem. Do not implement
a new architecture from this partial evidence. Proposed Round52 is conditional: first finish
the required fixed LightGBM audit, then falsify local joint-resolution versus fixed-structure
optimization on the verified real failures, keeping heldout evaluation and total cost explicit.
Reopening previously rejected adaptive-refinement/direct-learning candidates requires new
falsifiable evidence beyond these partial improvements.

## 9. Pending scope and reproduction

Required LightGBM panel/scaling/teacher controls remain UNRUN. Also outstanding for a full
A–P diagnosis: teacher-tree-derived pair identities, explicit spatial/temporal extrapolation,
separate missing/category interventions, classification same-state optimization, detailed
predict/memory curves for sparse controls, and finer resolution with support/regularization
properly matched. No categorical/missing/extrapolation causal conclusion is asserted.

Run inventory51.py only before changing research memory (one-shot R50 seal check).
panel51.py, diagnostics51.py, fixed_structure51.py resume complete contexts without refit.
analyze51.py and verify51.py reconstruct summaries from saved predictions. Source freezes
and all raw predictions are local. No closed Round44–50 rerun, commit/push/release/remote.
MASTER/STATE record this as pendingRound51 and keep **last_completed_round=50**.


## ROUND_51B — Local residual allocation diagnostic extension (2026-09-22)


**Decision: no public candidate promoted. Stop here; Round52 NOT RUN.**
LightGBM remains unavailable locally. HGB is **SECONDARY_REFERENCE**, not a substitute
measurement of LightGBM. Required TODO: **REPEAT_WITH_LIGHTGBM_WHEN_AVAILABLE**.
The latest user instruction authorizes this extension despite that dependency; it does
not retrospectively complete Round51 or turn external Taxi scores into internal evidence.

## 1. Budget, reuse and integrity

**1,300 benchmark fits +18 tiny unit-fixture fits =1,318 new fit calls**, below1,400.
1,550 benchmark model evaluations comprise1,300 fitted corrections and250 cached selector
views. 4,400 regional/univariate aggregate table estimates occur inside those model fits;
they are not4,400 independent model fits. Zero Base/HGB/EBM refits. No closed experiment
rerun, download, remote operation, commit, push, release or core modification.

Six tasks ×5 identical cached seeds/splits: California, SDSS redshift, Adult, SDSS STAR;
positive controls Diabetes and wine. Five independent sources: SDSS tasks share one source.
All30 original models supplied two existing pair identities; lexicographic fallback was
defined but **never used**. This is frozen-pair coverage, not a new pair-proposal experiment.

Primary1,250 fits were locked in design/source hashes before outcomes. After observing
Adult's capital_loss gates, a separately source-frozen50-fit **gate-only falsification**
was added; this is explicitly a post-primary diagnostic, not unseen confirmation. No
hyperparameter sweep or test-chosen deployment model. All previous960 R51 fits reused.

Five private tests PASS; Ruff PASS.1,300 models replayed bitwise on all three partitions:
3,900 prediction comparisons, maximum metric error0.1,080 fixed-capacity comparisons
checked at512 cells/two lookups. Sixteen public source files unchanged;1,526 previous
checkpoint artifacts unchanged, excluding only the two intentionally updated memory docs.

## 2. What is controlled, and what is not

Every pair arm has two correction components and512 allocated value cells, except explicitly
labelled fine controls at1,024. Effective occupancy varies and is reported. Global direct
16×16 grids versus two regional16×8 grids trade global resolution for conditioning at
fixed value count. Within-region quantiles are train-fitted: this comparison changes
conditioning AND state placement, not a pure indicator intervention. Allocation policies
change regional resolution with the same gate and total cells. Region selection uses
training residual separation only, but is conditioned on the already-trained Base.

The global hashed control uses the same pairs/grids and random additive codes; collision
rates range9.8% Adult to31.9% SDSS STAR. Direct grids eliminate hash aliases, **not**
quantization/category pooling. More cells can worsen estimation. Nominal categories are
frequency-mapped with a pooled unknown state; categorical and missing cells are not smoothed.

All corrections sit on frozen public Base. The laboratory reference is **global_mean**,
not a renamed public Base. Classification estimates4(y-p), one logistic-majorizer step;
regression estimates y-Base. HGB-delta fits use teacher-minus-Base predictions/logits.
Thus the classification controls do not test converged logistic fitting. No failed arm
establishes a mathematical representation ceiling or a universal failure of local models.

## 3. Principal task results

Five-seed mean gain versus public Base. Positive means improvement; regression in%RMSE
reduction, classification in AUC units. All alternatives/CIs/costs are in paired.csv;
this table reports interpretable controls, not a selected winner.

| Task | Global pair | Local uniform | Validation allocation | Local zero20 | Local interpolation | Double local capacity | Univariate residual |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| California |+.1961%|+.0522%|+.1603%|+.5706%|+.5611%|-.4865%|+2.7426%|
| SDSS regression |-.0288%|+.2569%|+.2370%|+.2988%|+.4203%|+.0948%|+.0079%|
| Adult |+.000393|+.001964|+.002051|+.001979|+.002005|+.002113|+.006536|
| SDSS STAR |+.011194|+.013988|+.014970|+.013327|+.014140|+.018888|+.004169|
| Diabetes |-23.0209%|-23.1071%|-21.6026%|+.0793%|-8.5950%|-18.1091%|-1.3696%|
| Wine |0|-.000694|-.002083|0|0|-.001389|+.000694|

Descriptive bootstrap CIs use five paired seeds, not independent real-data replication.
California local uniformCI[-.7334,.9772]%; SDSS regression[.1321,.3843]%; Adult
[.001744,.002222]AUC; SDSS STAR[.011357,.016699]AUC. Different tasks clearly have
different residual structures; a single averaged gain would obscure the failures.

## 4. Main effects masquerading as conditional interactions

**Adult is the decisive counterexample.** Both pair gates select capital_loss>0 on all
five seeds. The same-condition-only model (four values, no pair grid) gains **+.001961
AUC**, versus local pair+.001964. Paired extra gain of the pair over gate-only is
**+.000002**, CI[-.000289,.000221]. Calling this evidence of missing higher-order
interaction capacity would be wrong. Cached fixed EBM additive already gains+.026341AUC,
closing97.75% of the positive Base→HGB **logloss** gap; the new univariate correction
closes22.56%, local uniform only6.10%. This remains predominantly a main-effect problem.

On SDSS STAR, gate-only gains+.000736AUC, local pair+.013988: paired difference
**+.013252**, CI[.010794,.015710]. On SDSS regression, gate-only-.0022% versus
local pair+.2569%: difference+.2591%, CI[.1349,.3845]. A conditional joint contribution
survives this particular omitted-main control. It is not a pure functional-ANOVA proof
under correlated photometric features. Gate discovery/feature correlation are archived.

## 5. Allocation is not the dominant demonstrated gap

Local uniform versus global pair improves SDSS regression+.2854% and SDSS STAR+.002793AUC
(both5/5, descriptive CIs positive). California-.1430% CI crosses0. Adult+.001571AUC
is largely explained by the gate main effect above.

At the **same local representation and total capacity**, validation-selected64/192,
128/128 or192/64 allocation adds only California+.1078%, SDSS regression-.0199%,
Adult+.000087AUC, SDSS STAR+.000983AUC. The latter CI[-.000312,.002457] includes0.
Train support/magnitude/variance/squared-mean allocation does not consistently dominate
uniform. Finite test-oracle allocation gives total gains California+.3836%, SDSS+.2999%,
Adult+.002051AUC, STAR+.015127AUC. Even this optimistic finite selector leaves most of
the gap. It is not an exhaustive upper bound over all possible regions/allocations.

Oracle condition selects among five already-fitted global/local/mean-threshold/gated arms;
total California+.4002%, SDSS+.3090%, Adult+.001964AUC, STAR+.015052AUC. No oracle
is used by non-oracle arms. Validation selection is charged the cost of all candidates.

## 6. Local estimation, support and interpolation

Mean residual cells are particularly unsafe on small data: Diabetes support averages
roughly1.5 observations in nonempty evaluated cells; about33/89 test rows have an empty
or inactive component. Local mean loses23.11%RMSE. Fixed pseudo-count20 toward zero
returns to+.0793%, CI[-.4165,.5624]: **variance suppression restores the baseline; it
does not establish a new win**. Shrinkage toward empirical row+column residual mains
still loses4.31% globally; those priors are themselves noisy. Wine is mostly tied in AUC
and gives no reliable pair-gain evidence. No confidence→alpha line is reopened.

On SDSS, most test rows are dense (~18,850–18,910/20,000), not rare. Medium-support
regions show larger per-row gains; rare cells can hurt, but rarity alone cannot explain
the full remaining booster gap. Rare local regression cells (~76/20,000) have mean
MSE gain-.0155 with raw values and+.0136 with zero20. These are descriptive conditional
strata whose membership changes across seeds, not the variance of one fixed population cell.

Local pooling gains California+.7298% versus Base (CI[.4103,1.0586]), but only+.1555%
on SDSS regression and harms STAR relative to raw local values. No universal smoother.
At the same stored values/capacity, bilinear interpolation adds California+.5065% over
constant local lookup, SDSS+.1639% (5/5), Adult+.000041AUC and STAR+.000152AUC
(classification CIs cross0). Total gains remain+.5611% and+.4203% regression, far below
HGB's19.21%/4.30%. **CONTINUOUS_LOCAL_LIMIT is partial, not the main demonstrated cause.**
The one-axis linear control is separately recorded; bilinear is piecewise linear in each
axis, not a fitted local slope model. No new slope learner or spline tuning was introduced.

## 7. Where residual structure lives

California gates vary among latitude, housing age and median income. A single gate is
not stable across splits; univariate residual correction2.7426% exceeds all tested local
pair corrections. On Adult, the capital-loss regime is stable and additive. SDSS gates
involve photometric bands, with no single universal third feature. Local and global
correction correlations average about.29 SDSS regression/.55 STAR; local-versus-univariate
correlations about.07/.19. Adult local-versus-univariate correlation is~.51. These overlap
measures are not causal gap shares; gains MUST NOT be summed.

Conditional local mean/variance, monotonicity, curvature, jumps, sign changes, support and
density proxies are in per-context locality.csv. Monotonicity/roughness of nominal code
orders are not interpreted as physical shapes. Sign changes are not proof of periodicity
or multimodality. The data do not identify latent multimodality or irreducible noise.
Missing/complete regimes and categorical candidate gates were measured; no missing or
categorical gate won the train selector here. That does not prove absence of their effects.

## 8. HGB residual/function diagnostic

HGB is reused, never refitted. Heldout **fraction of teacher-minus-Base score energy
explained**, not task gain or causal variance share:

| Task | Univariate | Global pair | Local uniform | Local interpolation | Double local cells |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| California |13.36%|4.06%|5.34%|6.57%|4.21%|
| SDSS regression |2.11%|3.94%|6.22%|7.51%|8.64%|

Negative means worse than zero correction. Adult illustrates why function approximation
and task improvement must be separate: global teacher-pair energy recovery39.7% coexists
with **-.001310AUC** versus Base and worse logloss. A good partial teacher projection is
not guaranteed to compose into a better predictor. Classification energy is in clipped
logit space, not probability MSE; this changes the weighting of confident examples.

## 10. Taxonomy and architectural decision

| Case | Supported provisional findings | Not established |
|---|---|---|
| California |TRAINING_LIMIT (R51); residual mains; LOCAL_ESTIMATION_LIMIT and CONTINUOUS_LOCAL_LIMIT small; local allocation inconclusive|dominant allocation waste; representation ceiling|
| SDSS regression |TRAINING_LIMIT partial; CONDITIONAL_STRUCTURE_LIMIT and CONTINUOUS_LOCAL_LIMIT partial; allocation-policy gains small|most gap explained; missing-pair vs joint geometry separation|
| Adult |main-effect TRAINING/DEFAULT bottleneck; apparent conditional gain is gate-main recovery|true higher-order cause; representation versus fitting fully isolated|
| SDSS STAR |CONDITIONAL_STRUCTURE_LIMIT/DEFAULT_LIMIT partial; doubled local capacity helps; residual estimation/objective unresolved|a general allocation solution or full gap recovery|
| Diabetes |GENERALIZATION_LIMIT/LOCAL_ESTIMATION_LIMIT of dense residual cells; Base remains competitive|useful new correction|
| Wine |near-ceiling AUC control, no stable correction benefit|a competitive niche generalizing beyond this case|

No blanket REPRESENTATION_LIMIT and no unavoidable COMPUTATIONAL_LIMIT: this prototype
has overhead, but no lower-bound proof. Current two-fixed-pair coverage cannot rule out
better joint representations or entirely different useful pairs.

**No architecture is sufficiently justified for public implementation. One research-only
candidate for a future falsification experiment:** fixed conditional coded tables with
joint value estimation under the actual squared/logistic objective.

- Problem: separate weak one-step/local estimation from conditional representation.
- Evidence: R51 same-address joint fitting gains; R51B conditional SDSS gain survives
  gate-only control, while allocation policies themselves add little.
- Mechanism: freeze these pairs, gates, boundaries and512 cells; optimize values jointly
  against the unchanged Base offset. No new region/pair discovery, confidence, merging or
  extra capacity. This is a conditional-scaffold hypothesis, not a revival of generic
  backfitting/direct-additive sweeps or an already-selected v0.2 core.
- Expected cost: retained value capacity unchanged; fitting several sparse passes could
  exceed Base cost. Prediction still pays gating; no promised latency win.
- Risk/failure: small-n variance, additive leakage, correlated features, frozen pairs
  miss useful structure; objective improvement may remain training-only.
- Minimum falsifier: cached Base/HGB/R51 joint controls; compare raw conditional cells,
  fixed-cell joint objective and gate-only on the same four failures plus two controls,
  five seeds. One fixed penalty, no sweep. Reject if it fails to add at least2%RMSE or
  .005AUC on a corresponding real failure versus its raw conditional control, or harms
  small controls systematically; require costs disclosed and paired consistency. This
  threshold is a proposed future development falsifier, not a gate retrofitted to51B.

Keep RETRY_IF: objective/value estimation on the frozen conditional SDSS scaffold, only

When LightGBM becomes locally available, execute the frozen Round51 reference on cached

**Precise Round52 proposal: fixed conditional-state joint-objective falsification above,
development only, no new discovery or public changes. NOT EXECUTED.** No second candidate

Generator-known term oracle uses the same fitted encoder but direct cross-product addresses and **different capacity**. # Round 53 — frozen regression confirmation

Decision: **PARTIALLY_CONFIRMED**. Regression only. Public Base, compiler, API and defaults unchanged. No Round54 executed.

## 1. Freeze and continuity

Candidate SHA256: `d4288b789666defbc7d121460ea73c9dd0e459c65b55829d6fc7ceb32cde791d`. Candidate frozen before panel selection; panel frozen before fits. Same R52 native-address LSMR, lambda5, fixed intercept, max2000, atol/btol1e-8. No conditional states, no retuning, no new representation. Canonical source hashes and every previous private sealed artifact verified.

Historical contrast: R20 confirmation gain0.047% and R21gain0.565% failed their original broad gates. R53 does not erase those failures or invent a new solver algorithm; it confirms only the exact R52 all-native-cell ridge estimator under its own preregistered scope and thresholds. Earlier raw source inventories are unavailable, so this is not a paired reanalysis of R20/21. Generic pass/lambda tuning remains closed.

Round51 is **INCOMPLETE_DIAGNOSTICS_PENDING**, not complete. Its frozen LightGBM work was already completed in R52:40panel+30sample-size+60teacher-student fits. Verified and reused; **zero new R51 fits**. ## 8. Cost and technical behavior

Median of per-dataset paired median ratios: `{"fit_ratio": 1.4384397565974756, "batch_ratio": 1.0287715910394817, "single_ratio": 1.025011406372472, "deep_ratio": 1.0, "pickle_ratio": 1.0, "peak_ratio": 1.0030931047761449}`. Neutral deepcopies retain identical inference structure/parameter counts; pickle and deep ratios avoid the R52 ownership-packing artifact. Shared external train+validation mapping included in fit; test conversion excluded. Public Base.fit includes native encoding+discovery together (not artificially claimed separately timed). Added extraction encoding/addressing and solve recorded separately. Public predict measured warm, alternating11repetitions at1row and min(10k,testsize).

PeakRSS is process-scoped sampled every2ms, with phase baseline/increment recorded; allocator carryover and retained companion models limit exclusive-memory attribution. Deep graph sizes of native booster internals are not authoritative C-heap totals. Candidate/Base retained ratio is the comparison used for the gate.

Inference starts at the shared externally mapped DataFrame and includes the full public predict path; raw-source parsing and common external vocabulary transformation are not timed in prediction. Retained bytes cover the fitted public model, not the identical shared external vocabulary. These are paired model costs, not an end-to-end file-to-prediction service benchmark.

Nonconverged final Joint calls: 0. No extra iterations or fallback. Empty native structures remain no-op models; no-active-parameter calls are not optimization wins. Exact solver unchanged. Harness-only corrections: tolerate normalization roundoff in intercept assertion; report objective for no-op branch; retain capacity failures and continue references. Attempts remain in ledger.

`{"ledger": {"fit_start": 495, "fit_complete": 479, "fit_failed": 15}, "primary_successful_fit_slots": 280, "scaling_successful_fit_slots": 195, "model_evaluations": 475, "split_metric_evaluations": 1425, "primary_paired_contexts": 70, "technical_or_blocked_slots": 20, "solver_noop_calls": 7, "technical_retries": "Same frozen settings only; ledger includes returned fits before reporting-assertion interruptions. No tuning.", "unmatched_ledger_starts": 1, "unmatched_start_explanation": "Initial native Allstate capacity exception preceded the continuation handler; retained in failure trace and harness amendments, not a running job.", "fixture_calls": {"base": 9, "nonempty_solver": 12, "empty_solver_noop": 1, "total": 22, "scope": "Initial8tests(1hash-parser failure), corrected8tests, final9tests; exact same synthetic fixture, not performance evidence."}, "fixtures": "Separate synthetic pytest fixtures; not confirmation evidence or benchmark rows."}`. Counts include successful model slots, not independent datasets. All archived public-model predictions replayed exactly; maximum metric replay error 0.0. Structure/addresses/parameter counts equal. Synthetic technical tests are separate from quality evidence. No test labels enter vocabulary/discovery or joint solve; validation belongs solely to existing Base stopping. Prior private seal verified (14369files), public hashes unchanged; research_private ignored and memory docs trackable.

No simultaneous model-fit workers: timing runs sequential at1thread. No hardware-general speed claim. Some sources are inherently repeated/grouped observations and older complete inventories are absent; independent-source confirmation scope is bounded accordingly. The dataset bootstrap is conditional on this convenience panel, not a guarantee over every future tabular domain. Allstate inability is a deployment limitation, not evidence of its unknown Joint quality.

## 11. Final decision and stop

**PARTIALLY_CONFIRMED**. Native research branch: **PARTIALLY_CONFIRMED_REGRESSION_ONLY_NO_GATING**. No public integration/default/API change. Keep structural/discovery gap and unexplained source heterogeneity open; no classification extrapolation. Reopen value fitting only for a genuinely new falsifiable mechanism with independent preregistered evidence, not a lambda/iteration/capacity rescue on this panel. All Round53 panel sources are now observed and must not be called fresh confirmation for future tuning.

Recommended Round54, **proposal only**: TRAIN-ONLY REGIME IDENTIFIABILITY: keep the R53 estimator fixed; pre-register one whole-model 3-fold out-of-fold RMSE-difference diagnostic computed only inside outer training, with a fixed zero-sign threshold and its full extra fit cost. First test whether it predicts the sign and magnitude on already observed development sources; require a later untouched source panel before any gating claim. This is a whole-Base-versus-Joint diagnostic motivated by new53heterogeneity, not reopening closed per-table confidence-to-alpha or tuning lambda. Stop if transport or cost fails. No gate/API integration now.

Stop after53. No commit/push/tag/release or next-round execution.

# CODADAPT ROUND 54 — Train-only diagnosis

Decisione: **OOF_SIGNAL_STRONG secondo i criteri preregistrati; gating OOF non economicamente giustificato.** ALWAYS_JOINT resta candidato di ricerca regressivo, non default/API. La correlazione prospettica è compatibile con trasferimento, ma cinque fonti non dimostrano una regola universale né una protezione affidabile dal danno.

## Protocollo e indipendenza

Preregistrazione SHA256 `0f8a79281c46177574442c09560799c0ba6dacab57ed4aca98e6893f07b8bd49`; candidato R52/R53 `d4288b789666defbc7d121460ea73c9dd0e459c65b55829d6fc7ceb32cde791d`. Nessuna modifica a solver, lambda 5, stati, encoder, capacità o runtime. Nessun fit finale storico ripetuto.

15 fonti Round53 ×5split ×3fold. La Base scopre la struttura su circa metà dell'outer training; un sesto serve allo stopping interno, un terzo è OOF intoccato. Vocaboli categorici stimati solo sull'inner train. Joint rifitta quegli stessi stati; non una struttura scoperta sull'intero outer train. Il segnale primario è la riduzione RMSE delle predizioni OOF concatenate, non la media dei tre rapporti. Final training/validation mantiene la ricetta R53 60/20/20.

Allstate: **5 contesti OOF,15fold** completati; nessun target esterno Base/Joint disponibile a causa del precedente limite512MiB. Non si ripete il fit completo e non si attribuisce un risultato. Tutte15fonti sono in `historical_signals_all.csv`; analisi paired su14fonti/70split in `results.csv`. Nuove5fonti/25split in `prospective_results.csv`. L'unità primaria è la fonte, i cinque split dipendenti non sono cinque repliche indipendenti.

Pannello nuovo congelato tramite soli metadati: Tecator240×100spettri grezzi (esclusi PCA della fonte e altre risposte chimiche; credito Tecator/Infratec); topo_2_1 8885×266; QSAR-TID-11 5742×1024fingerprint; SAT11-HAND4440×116; MIP-2016 1090×144. ID/ignore della fonte esclusi. Runtime SAT/MIP ha righe algoritmo/istanza ripetute: questo è trasferimento random-row, **non unseen-instance**. Inventari antichi completi assenti: nuove rispetto agli inventari disponibili, non prova di mai-usate universale. Nessuna fonte sostituita dopo i risultati.

`prospective_decisions.json` contiene25decisioni per split e5predizioni aggregate, più calibrazione/soglia apprese solo dalle14fonti storiche. Hash salvato prima della prima lettura dei file heldout da parte del reveal. Tutti i25Base/Joint finali erano già salvati. LightGBM congelato è solo riferimento dopo il sigillo, mai un input alla diagnosi.

## Risultato principale

Storico: Pearson **0.953347**, CI95%[0.9134649678996183, 0.9934323206510554]; Spearman **0.947253**, CI95%[0.7732297464440323, 1.0]. Leave-one-source-out rho minimo0.934066. AUC beneficio **0.975**, CI[0.8500000000000001, 1.0]; accordo a tre classi13/14=92.86%. FPR fonte25%,FNR0%; include neutrali tra i non-benefici. AUC danno1.0 poggia su **una sola fonte negativa**, quindi non è validazione di un classificatore del rischio.

Prospettico: Pearson **0.867354**, Spearman **0.600000**, AUC beneficio **0.750**, segno4/5. CI bootstrap Spearman[-1.0, 1.0]: enorme incertezza con5fonti; nessuna fonte con danno medio<−0.1%, AUC danno non stimabile. FPR0%,FNR25% a livello fonte. Le soglie STRONG erano fissate prima: l'etichetta non implica una stima precisa o una promozione.

Tutti i gain in questo report sono **percentuali di riduzione RMSE**, positivi=miglioramento. Le differenze tra gain sono punti percentuali. MIP è falso negativo/neutralità predetta, QSAR è borderline, Tecator è sottostimato: i numeri smentiscono una previsione perfetta.

A numerosità identica, one-fold heldout rho **0.947253**, validation esterna sugli stessi modelli **0.942857**, OOF pooled **0.947253**. Full-training validation rho **0.916484**. Il segnale non è solo l'effetto di avere un training più piccolo, ma la comparazione non separa causalmente ogni effetto di discovery e stima. Jaccard componenti mediano storico .532, nuovo .276: la struttura riscoperta varia. I confronti Base/Joint **entro fold** hanno struttura identica e isolano il refit dei valori.

47/70contesti storici hanno3fold positivi,87.23%di questi ha test gain>.1%. Tra i nuovi11/25hanno3fold positivi,81.82%ha test gain>.1%. Richiedere3/3 riduce il recupero dei benefici, soprattutto nel nuovo pannello. Dettagli in `*_fold_consistency.csv` e `*_consistency_groups.csv`.

L'oracle per split test-only ha mediana0.790856%, media2.282293%; headroom medio **0.117881punti**, mediano0.165369punti. L'oracle per fonte intera ha media2.197491%. Non è un modello né una selezione utilizzabile.

La soglia0.1% seleziona53/70split: TP45,FP8,FN2; FPR **34.78%**, FNR **4.26%** (qui unità split, diverse dalle metriche fonte). Harmful acceptance tra selezionati11.32%; recupero gain positivo98.46%. Peggior split selezionato **-1.523811%**; peggior media fonte della policy0%, rispetto−.463%AlwaysJoint. I conteggi dettagliati/mediane selected-only e fonti selezionate sono nel CSVpolicies; il guadagno policy include0nei fit che scelgonoBase.

Costi: Base1×; AlwaysJoint **1.438440×**; diagnosi3fold+finale **6.047438×Base**, **4.465620×AlwaysJoint**. Mediana delle mediane dei rapporti entro fonte, coerenteR53. Nessun parallelismo fra fit. La diagnosi include preprocessing,Base,estrazione,solveJoint,deploy,predizioni; i diagnostici esplorativi first-step/geometry e replay sono separati e non necessari a un ipotetico gate. Il costo storico finale è la misura congelataR53, il costo diagnostico è misuratoora: non un benchmark intertemporale perfettamente sincronizzato. L'ordine di grandezza sfavorevole è netto. No misurazione peakRSS diagnostica: non si inventa una stima.

## Proxy economici e meccanismo

Un solo fold ha rho.947storico ma costa circa2.954×Base insieme al finale (mediana delle mediane dei rapporti entro fonte): meno di3fold, comunque troppo per un headroom minimo. Full-validation gain rho.916 è un monitor economico **dopo** aver fattoJoint, non evita il suo fit; è la validation già usata nello stoppingBase. Training gain rho.824; main-effect R² rho.908 sufull fit/.921suinner fit è una correlazione post-Joint, non una causalità né un proxy gratuito pre-fit. Condition e overlap non hanno evidenza più convincente. Le classificazioni basate sulla soglia.1nelle righe geometry del CSV sono puramente descrittive/non dimensionalmente appropriate: interpretare solo ranking/correlazioni di quelle righe, non un gate su n/R²/condition.

Una sola iterazione LSMR riparte dazero per definizione del solver congelato: non è un aggiornamento warm-start dellaBase. Gain OOF medio **-19.489%**; correlazione col gain finale OOF rho **-0.670330**, col test rho−.709890. Il solver di un passo costa circa0.047×Base, ma richiedeBase+estrazione e non recupera il segno utile. Non invertiamo posthoc il segnale per fabbricare un gate.

Interpretazione più plausibile: il refit congiunto corregge errori coordinati dei valori dentro una struttura già informativa. L'utilità si ripete in sottocampioni e quindi si può stimare OOF, ma il beneficio è quasi sempre piccolo/nonnegativo: prevederlo non rende conveniente evitarlo. Finite-sample variability e qualità della struttura restano intrecciate. La statistica residual-feature-autocorrelation usa anche l'ordine dei codici categorici: su categoriche è arbitraria, non una misura di smoothness. Effective rank usa componenti, condition un sottoinsieme deterministico≤256colonne, support minimo include cellevuote; nessuna pretesa di rank/condition esatto globale. In un solo componente il rankproxy è riportato0dall'implementazione, da leggere come proxydegenerato e non rankmatematico.

Calibrazione LODO univariata fissa: MAE gain0.6545punti, Brier beneficio0.1712, Brier danno0.0728; fallbackJeffreys quando manca una classe neltraining. `*_calibration_bins.csv` confronta segnale,guadagnoosservato,predizione calibrata. Nessun modello multivariato, nessun tuning. Bootstrap10kfonti e secondario cluster-split nei supplementi, correlazioni secondarie esplorative senza correzione multipla; nessuna promozione del proxy con valore più alto.

## Contabilità, audit e Round51

**1070 chiamate benchmark completate**: {'Base': 325, 'Joint': 325, 'FirstStep': 395, 'LightGBM': 25}. Sono900fit/solveOOF(100contesti×3fold×3armi),70first-step su modelli storici riusati,75nuovi fullBase/Joint/first-step,25LightGBM reference. 12chiamate fixture separate in due esecuzioni della suiteprotocollo. Calibrazioni univariate finali:41LODOstoriche+3mappinggenerale, non learnerbenchmark; i report sono stati rigenerati per rendere l'aggregazione dei costi coerente conR53, senza ripetere fitBase/Joint o scegliere parametri.

12test passati; replay650predizioni,100contesti/300fold, mismatch0; coperturaOOF una volta per riga, nessuna sovrapposizione stopping/OOF, sigillo prospettico verificato. Il solver congelato fa scale/unscale dell'intercetta fissa, con round-offcirca1ULP: il fingerprint strutturale normalizza solo questa differenza nell'oggetto di audit, mai nel modello o nelle predizioni. Nessun cambio alla ricetta per far passare i controlli. I filedi esecuzione sono stati hashati dopo l'avvio storico (limite temporale dichiarato); preregistrazione/pannello precedono ogni esperimento e i sorgenti non sono cambiati durantei fit.

Round51 resta **INCOMPLETE_DIAGNOSTICS_PENDING**: supplementoLightGBM130fit già completato, nessun residuo frozen fit da eseguire. Mancano controlli pair derivati dagli alberi, extrapolazione spazio/tempo, interventi missing/categorie, curve complete e separazione causale fine risoluzione/supporto/regolarizzazione. Questi richiedono nuovi disegni/fitting, non un diagnostico economico univoco già pronto. Nessuna falsa dichiarazioneCOMPLETED.

## Decisione e continuità

1. OOF_SIGNAL_STRONG nel senso stretto preregistrato; generalizzabilità e harm prediction restano limitate.
2. **Gating vale il costo: NO.** Fallisce qualitàincrementale e costo, anche con soglieLODO; la significatività della correlazione non cambiaquesta decisione.
3. **ALWAYS_JOINT resta candidato: YES**, solo regressione e ricerca congelata; guadagno modesto, non integrazione pubblica.
4. Chiudere la via gating3fold per questoestimatore/popolazione e il positivo-first-step zero-start. Non riaprire tuninglambda/soglieconfidencealpha o classificazione.
5. Aperto: costo/rischio dell'opzioneAlwaysJoint e identificazione causale dell'eterogeneità; harm prediction richiede piùfonti negative indipendenti.
6. Round55 **solo proposta**: audit dell'envelope costi/rischio diAlwaysJoint congelato su fonti nuove scelte per metadati (piccoli campioni,wide,categoriche), senza gate e senza tuning; conteggiare failurecapacità, CIpaired perfonte e costireali. Non avviata.

Core/API/default/compiler invariati. Nessun commit,push,tag,pubblicazione o operazioneGitremota. MemoriaMASTER/STATE aggiornata a54 con51esplicitamenteparziale. Artefatti di ricerca restano locali e ignorati daGit/distribuzioni.

## Decision

**KEEP_RESEARCH_ONLY** under the preregistered rule. No integration, tuning, gate, fallback, classifier extension or public change. Round56 not started.

## Train-only diagnosis, not gating

The historical strong main-effect association attenuates on this panel: Base train-main R² rho≈.482, Joint-main rho≈.437. Native component overlap is weakly related (rho≈.164); the broad claim that measured overlap explains Joint benefit is not supported here. Pair/main redundancy rho≈.283 is also weak. No new threshold or gate is derived.

All four materially negative source means are small datasets (Pyrim, Bodyfat, Cholesterol, Plasma retinol), but the preregistered small/tiny family means remain positive because other small sources improve. This is a downside concentration, not a validated routing rule. Pyrim has only74rows,44training rows: its worst split loses22.106%testRMSE and10.257%trainRMSE while reducing the penalized train objective45.479%. Bodyfat loses1.515%on average and also worsens unpenalized trainRMSE on average. Thus the downside cannot uniformly be called training overfit: the frozen global ridge objective can prefer smaller coefficients with worse unpenalized fit. This round does not identify a remedy or tune lambda.

CPU activity (+31.693%) raises the mean, but removing the two best sources still leaves a positive median. Brazilian houses and forest fires tie exactly because the frozen Base retains no active correction in these fits; Joint adds no missing capacity. The large taxi gain is modest (+.491%) and SGEMM smaller (+.260%); they do not demonstrate closure of the booster gap.

## Capacity and operational robustness

The unchanged pre-fit guard estimates `n_train*(40*p+2*8+80)+24*(p+8*min(p,3))*max(33,131)+32*8*256`, budget512MiB. Rejection happens before native encoding/discovery and before Joint. Increasing memory forJoint cannot fix this under the frozen contract.

Historical Allstate (R53, not refitted) has112990training rows and130predictors: this same formula exceeds512MiB. Its archived100kprefix was executable, full source was not. R55 Buzz and Santander reproduce two routes to the same limit (large n and very large p). The guard concerns a native estimated working allocation, not exhaustion of the machine's physical RAM; Joint introduces an additional temporary sparse design only after Base succeeds.

## Pyrim: precise local explanation

Worst historical split55002: training44,test15. Data loss rises **0.005906334**, penalty falls **-0.078047993**, objective changes **-0.072141660**. Frozen gain **-22.105673%**. Zero penalty train gain **61.314084%**, test gain **-70.705692%**: removing bias from the penalized training target exposes a substantially worse generalization outcome. It is not a discovered replacement.

Main-only gain **0.595462%**,pair-only **-13.274667%**. Restoring only one group after full Joint can increase error because main/pair shifts cancel each other. Neither isolated-group restoration nor allocated group loss is a unique additive causal decomposition. Group objective/norm/mass tables and all cell values/supports are archived.

Medium+high-support delta gain **8.804881%**; low-only **-32.781677%**,very-low-only **-4.999961%**. This localizes harmful changes, but it cannot by itself distinguish regularization bias from estimation variance. No support threshold becomes an algorithm.

## Failure heterogeneity and success controls

Pyrim55002 combines an exact objective tradeoff, low-support delta localization and prediction instability. Yet medium/high-support-only gain is−10.91%on Pyrim55001: the same intervention fails on another split. Bodyfat55005 similarly worsens from−6.34%to−7.77%; both autoPrice failure splits become substantially worse after excluding rare changes. Cholesterol,plasma_retinol and wine_quality demonstrate training improvement that does not reliably transfer to test. AutoPrice/Boston include objective tradeoff and drift associations; only one autoPrice failure meets the extreme-target sensitivity definition. There is no universal support cutoff or component group causing every failure.

Eight strong-success sources remain in the full comparison; six enter the preregistered perturbation panel. Successful fits can lower both data loss and norm with coordinated main/pair redistribution. Bootstrap prediction variance relative to test-target variance is median1.31%in sampled strong-success contexts versus3.92%in negative-source contexts. The table also reports training-target normalization, whose source-level association with gain is−0.7455. This is exploratory association, not causal identification or a train-only routing rule. Raw coefficient variance is not a reliable substitute for prediction variance.

No failure is assigned a demonstrated solver-numerics,ill-conditioning or structure-limit root cause. Non-identifiability is an association in12/13failures and also occurs in successes; ridge resolves the objective minimizer. Nineteen empty-structure no-ops expose a distinct representation/discovery limit without producing downside. The taxonomy's zero unlabelled cases means descriptive coverage, **not complete causal identification**.

## Accounting, limitations and decision

Completed fit/solve calls **1650** (includes5synthetic public discovery fits and5identical structure replays; no realBase refit); diagnostic nonconverged flags **137**, retained. Of these90are intentional one-pass controls, while47are iteration-limit exits (20BCD,9real zero-penalty,9weak-penalty,9synthetic zero-penalty). These are not137 failures of the frozen candidate. Test-only bounded oracle optimizations **13** separately;7fixture solver calls across initial/final suites.10tests passed. Archived replay **115** contexts / **4053** metric views,maximum relative discrepancy **4.7823009707796615e-15**. No source replaced or historical prediction overwritten. Core hashes and Round55 private seal checked. An initial verification-only empty-table dtype error was corrected by explicit numeric casting; initial and final logs are retained and no experimental fit was rerun for that correction.

Round51 remains **INCOMPLETE_WITH_EXPLICIT_REMAINDER** with the six already documented original diagnostics; none became an easy report-only completion. Historical rounds1–55 and Round55 decision preserved.

Dominant conclusion: the penalized objective can deliberately exchange predictive data fit for coefficient norm reduction; removal of penalty is not a cure because finite-sample generalization can deteriorate even more. Rare-state value changes and main/pair redistribution can localize the loss, but ridge non-identifiability proxies alone do not establish its cause. Multiple mechanisms remain possible and taxonomy is not a universal causal classifier.

Close: numerical-bug rescue or universal zero-penalty replacement based on these diagnostics; unconditional integration remains unsupported. Keep open: how an objective balances predictive fit against variance in low-support overlapping components, and independent mechanism validation. Proposed57 only: preregister a matched fixed-structure experiment separating the data-fit/norm tradeoff from prediction variance, with a fresh prospective mechanism panel before any promotion. No gate, no broad lambda sweep, no universal support correction inferred from test oracles. **Stop; Round57 not started.**

## Frozen protocol and coverage

Preregistration SHA256 `6c67d94e1107e911b963e9c3d080eb41ba4aec7bd6194cccb4146cf428344aa6`. Candidate `d4288b789666defbc7d121460ea73c9dd0e459c65b55829d6fc7ceb32cde791d` unchanged. Older incomplete inventories do not prove never-used-ever. Descriptions, target/identifier exclusions, publisher checksums and raw hashes are archived. Random-row evaluation does not establish future-time/unseen-patient/song/device generalization. Every prospective payload was saved and hashed before test scoring; no outcome-based source replacement.

Structure, preprocessing, codes, masks, addresses, dimensions and intercept remain fixed. All groups are main or pair; conditional is absent. Full train discovery plus Base validation stopping precede perturbations: the diagnosis is conditional on that structure, not discovery-independent crossfitting. Perturbations alter only rows and penalty; lambda5*fraction controls the increased relative regularization when rows are reduced.

## Frozen scores and observable signatures

OBJECTIVE_RISK = positive training-data-loss sacrifice / penalty reduction, only when total objective decreases, clipped0..1. VARIANCE_RISK = cross-half prediction mean squared disagreement on up to4096 training feature rows / twice training-target variance. Thresholds .01/.02 fixed before new outcomes. Both high=MIXED/RISKY, one high=that mechanism/RISKY, both low=LOW_RISK/SAFE_POSITIVE. Nonfinite or failed main/half solves=UNKNOWN/UNCERTAIN. No score selects Base or Joint. Empty structures imply ties, not guaranteed positive gains.

Observed operational OBJECTIVE requires train sacrifice and converged zero-full test gain improvement>=.5points. VARIANCE requires current40%-sample mean testRMSE>1.01*full and predictionvariance40%>1.1*80%. Both=MIXED; neither plus nonnegative material gain=LOW_RISK; unexplained losses=UNKNOWN. These intervention-linked labels are **proxies, not causal ground truth**. Reducing sample size also changes bias/coverage; the mass-matched arm addresses relative penalty but cannot remove every confound.

Per-group parameter std/variance, norm and penalty are archived with main/pair prediction variances and their covariance. Negative covariance is compensation, not proof of benefit or harm. The sum includes twice covariance; groups cannot be interpreted as independent causal contributions. Support<20 delta mass and target-free state drift are moderators only. moderation.csv reports exploratory strata fixed at historical medians; these never change prospective scores or become a gate.

Successes>=3%receive the same fields in success_signatures.csv. Conditioning is a deterministic<=256active-column ridge proxy, not exact whole-design conditioning. Extreme-target loss allocation is sensitivity, not a causal outlier intervention.

Pyrim55002 was predicted MIXED but labelled VARIANCE by the frozen rule. This does not erase the exact training objective/data tradeoff proved in56: zero penalty exposes worse test generalization and can mask that tradeoff. The worst prospective case, Pollution57002(-11.00%), is predicted MIXED but remains UNKNOWN. Thus net intervention utility need not uniquely identify simultaneous opposing mechanisms. Labels are not repaired retrospectively. Unknown failures are retained explicitly.

## Accounting, limitations and decision

Resume audit:85prospective and63historical completed contexts were preserved. NYC55004was interrupted before coefficient persistence; its46completed solver states were unavailable, so only that incomplete context was recomputed. New per-solve checkpoints prevent repeating this loss. Two concurrent-ledger append omissions(one start,one completion) were reconciled from valid artifacts, explicitly labelled inferred accounting; no completed synthetic experiment was repeated. One interrupted attempt is separately counted.

Completed fit/solve calls **10216**; exact references **1620** separately;10fixturecalls. No-op solves and intentional one-pass stopping are counted as calls, not newly fitted predictive evidence.10tests passed (8original checks preserved,2new checkpoint tests with fake solvers). A test-stub checkpoint serialization error was fixed without experimental changes; logs retained. A report-file encoding error required rebuilding this reporting script; no measurements or frozen code changed. Replay:

{
  "contexts": 200,
  "metric_values": 115950,
  "max_relative_error": 3.1510534422402943e-16,
  "locks_verified": 200,
  "fixed_structure": true,
  "no_real_refit_in_replay": true
}

Public16source hashes, Round56private seal, frozen execution/preregistration and prediction locks are checked in final_audit.json. Primary analysis was sealed before robustness. NumPy/solver dependencies unchanged; Matplotlib was added only to the local virtual environment for plots, not public package metadata. Fits ran in two one-thread processes concurrently; walltimes are accounting, not system-speed claims. Historical1-56 entries preserved; Round51 remains INCOMPLETE_WITH_EXPLICIT_REMAINDER.

**Conclusion:** operational variance signatures are frequent among new losses, but causal dominance and reliable mechanism prediction are not established. Risk detection is stronger than mechanism attribution; objective tradeoff can coexist with variance and be hidden by a net zero-penalty comparison. Do not force either exclusive explanation.

Close unconditional ALWAYS_JOINT integration and numerical-error rescue on this evidence. Keep predictive uncertainty and objective tradeoff as conditional mechanisms, with the stated identification limits. Proposed58 only: a preregistered discovery-independent versus reused-discovery contrast at matched fixed designs and penalty mass, using separate training feature probes and frozen prospective predictions. It must address this identification failure, not retune57scores or reopen48-50confidence-to-alpha. No new estimator, gate or architectural promotion is justified here. **Stop;58notstarted.**


<!-- descriptive-supplement -->
## Reviewed conclusions

The prespecified outcome is **OTHER: reliable causal separation not established**. This is not evidence that objective tradeoff or predictive variance is absent. Operational variance signatures occur in9/11new material losses, but the necessary source-level risk association remains uncertain. The two UNKNOWN negatives are solar-flare57001 and pollution57002. Overall mechanism accuracy47/85(55.29%) is below the descriptive majority-label baseline56/85(65.88%); mechanism prediction is not established prospectively.

The2x2 interventions expose an interaction between penalty and sampling. On the new sources, removing penalty raises source-median testRMSE1.676% relative to current Joint. Using40%of training raises it1.730%with current penalty and3.237%with zero penalty; the median difference-in-differences is+0.385percentagepoints of full-currentRMSE. These quantities use source means then medians and are not additive across medians. The historical counterparts are+2.350%,+5.872%,+14.041%,+6.427points. No test-selected penalty is recommended.

Prediction variance is non-increasing with more rows (including ties) in155/155intensive contexts on the40/60/80%grid. TestRMSE is non-increasing in63/70historical and74/85prospective contexts, not all. This supports sample sensitivity conditional on the full-training discovered structure, while leaving a discovery/estimation confound. Prospective median half-fit coefficient correlation is0.6844 versus0.9680 for predictions: unstable coefficients do not by themselves establish unstable predictions.

Support and target-free state drift moderate risk descriptively. With the historical support threshold, new-source loss rates are8/38(21.1%)for high low-support delta mass versus3/47(6.4%)otherwise. High state drift gives10/41(24.4%)versus1/44(2.3%). These post hoc strata are correlated with dataset/sample size and are not independent causal effects, score revisions, or a gate.

Pair contributions usually have greater bootstrap variance than main effects:72.7%of new failures and95.9%of nonfailures. Negative main/pair covariance occurs in63.6%of failures and87.8%of nonfailures. Compensation is therefore more common in successful/nonnegative contexts but is not a universal success mechanism. Marginal group variance cannot be added while ignoring covariance.

Huber has nearly zero source-median improvement over frozen Joint on new sources; it rescues6material losses and introduces6new ones.15/85prospective Huber fits reach the iteration limit, so it is not evidence of an optimized robust alternative. Group ridge rescues8and introduces5, with source-median gain change only+0.067percentagepoints; this retrospective outcome cannot become a selected rule. Large zero-penalty diagnostics also have nonconvergence flags, retained explicitly. Numerical agreement on the1620small exact systems does not certify those unconverged large interventions.

All13historical losses are still present:12operational VARIANCE,1MIXED(autoPrice55002). Pyrim55002 remains VARIANCE by the frozen signature despite the independently established training error/norm tradeoff. Pollution57002 is UNEXPLAINED, with11.00%RMSE deterioration. Thus the operational net-test intervention rule can hide simultaneous opposing mechanisms; labels were not changed after observing this defect.

Close unconditional ALWAYS_JOINT integration and reuse of these scores as a promotion argument. Keep fixed-structure uncertainty and objective bias open only as conditional diagnoses. A precise proposed Round58 is a discovery-independent falsification control: allocate disjoint training-only discovery and coefficient-estimation samples, freeze identical designs within each penalty/sampling contrast, evaluate disagreement on a third training-feature probe, and compare against reused discovery at matched estimation size and penalty mass. Before any new test outcomes, preregister whether separating discovery changes calibration and the UNKNOWN rate. Failure to improve those endpoints closes that explanation. This is a proposal, not a new estimator or a started experiment; no confidence-to-alpha or gate is reopened.

- [Sample-size curves](../../research_private/round57/figures/sample_size.png)
- [Locked prospective risk scores](../../research_private/round57/figures/prospective_scores.png)

Final preservation audit normalizes Windows path separators and rejects an empty selection: 1820preexisting primary artifacts were hash-matched unchanged. The initial zero-selection verifier defect was corrected before sealing; no experimental results changed.
