# CODADAPT ROUND58 - Independent discovery versus estimation

**DATA_EFFICIENCY_DOMINANT. Diagnosis only. ALWAYS_JOINT remains KEEP_RESEARCH_ONLY; no gate, rescue tuning, public integration or Round59 execution.**

## Continuity and preregistration

Round57 is COMPLETE/OTHER; its10172-file seal was verified before58. All historical1-57memory is preserved. Round51 remains INCOMPLETE_WITH_EXPLICIT_REMAINDER. An analysis implementation correction before any prospective fits fixed the strong-success retention denominator to the six preselected Round55 sources; the initial code and both hashes remain archived. No protocol threshold changed. Candidate SHA256 `d4288b789666defbc7d121460ea73c9dd0e459c65b55829d6fc7ceb32cde791d` unchanged. Protocol SHA256 `d2678a01c04d043dc3c31b9f0be458d443015aa904d0dd8aad7d81a5283c86ac`; protocol, source manifest, execution modules and analysis were frozen before prospective fitting. Fixedlambda0/5 only; same native discovery/encoder and LSMR max2000/tol1e-8, no selected intermediate penalty.

## Design and causal limits

19historical sources x5splits include Pyrim, Pollution,6other failure-containing sources, neutral empty-scaffold sources and at least6strong sources.17new metadata-selected real sources x5splits form the prospective panel. Newness is relative to documented52-57 source families; older incomplete inventories prevent a universal never-used-ever claim. Sources/checksums/feature exclusions and duplicate removals are archived. IID random rows do not establish time/entity extrapolation. All failures/exclusions are retained, no source replacement after results.

Original60/20/20split; the20%validation is divided into C(public stopping) and H(diagnostic holdout), disjoint from estimation T=A+B. PublicBase is refitted for this changed stopping partition; R55/57 scores are context, not silently reused as an identical baseline. At most1training row is dropped for equal halves. Vocabularies and native encoders learn only the declared discovery rows. All three discoveries share C, making structures statistically dependent; H is never used in discovery or estimation. Per-context train-only predictions were hashed before outer test scoring.

The3x3 D/E factorial changes sample reuse. Within each D, encoder, boundaries, category vocabulary, identities, hashes, support suppression, dimensions and intercept are fixed. Budgets are identical but realized retained capacities may differ across D. The symmetric half comparison holds the two structures and both estimation sample sizes fixed. Full-estimation controls reuse the discovery rows and therefore do not establish complete independence. The frozen intercept also retains discovery-target information, so independence is conditional on that scaffold/intercept. Lambda5penalizes sum-loss: half samples have stronger relative regularization; zero controls diagnose this without tuning.

H1(discovery reuse) andH2(value reuse) need not be separately identifiable from the same contrasts. Oracle has no learned discovery: swapping A/B exchanges the same two estimators, making symmetric reuse contrast exactlyzero. This is a negative control, not proof that a single noisy A/B difference identifies estimation bias. Conditional/multi-label signatures below are operational; no category is causal ground truth.

Effect accuracy uses HELP/HARM at±.5%matched effect; UNKNOWN predictions count as incorrect, not dropped. Mechanism exact accuracy and label coverage are different, neither compares against known causal truth. Undefined correlation bootstrap draws are counted, and defined-only intervals must not be interpreted unconditionally. Every source leave-one-out median is retained in analysis.json and source results in dataset_results.csv.

Weighted Jaccard compares selected component identities with cell-count weights. Address agreement is a coding-layout proxy; adjusted Rand compares H partitions invariant to address labels. Selection-order rank is not an unavailable oracle utility ranking. Value correlations compare A/B coefficients on exactly the same D; H and test prediction disagreements are separated. support.csv and test_estimation_support.csv report occupied/rare/unseen/suppressed states and target-free drift; these are moderators, never gates. Component-removal utility on H and residual coverage are in discovery_quality.csv, with correlated contributions explicitly non-additive.

## Sample size, oracle, random structures and penalty controls

sample_size_curves.csv includes nested5k/10k/25k/50k/100ktraining points when available and reuses the three completed full historical points. At every size the Base is fitted on that size with fixed C/H/test. No unavailable size is invented. Loss of estimation rows, changed retained capacity and penalty/sample mass are kept distinct. penalty_reuse.csv records the0/5contrast, no optimum selection.

Synthetic216contexts span6known generators x3n x2noise x2distractor levels x3seeds. Each has the complete native factorial plus6oracle and6random-structure solves. Oracle matches true generating components exactly; conditional truth uses a3-feature64-state table. Random controls match oracle block arities/table sizes, may occasionally overlap truth, and are explicitly not random versions of differently sized native-selected designs. No synthetic success constitutes real-data confirmation.

Discovery and estimation costs are separate per result. Standalone fit estimates include public discovery, its preprocessing, training design extraction, value solve and separately replayed deployment copy; no free shared discovery is claimed for ensemble deployment. The standalone reconstruction excludes orchestration, persistence, and the separate transformation of the shared T/H probe; it is not a complete cold end-to-end workflow measurement. Diagnostic H/test verification and repeated benchmark time are separately outside those estimator costs. Raw-input timing includes categorical mapping and public prediction. Warm3repetitions at1andup-to1000rows are exploratory local timings, not independent hardware confirmation. Deep memory is recursively estimated retained model+vocabulary, serialized bytes also reported. Context RSS includes allocator carryover and multiple diagnostic models; it is not isolated production-model peak RAM.

## Decision and next research boundary

The frozen decision is **DATA_EFFICIENCY_DOMINANT**. MIXED includes unresolved identification/insufficient evidence; NO_EFFECT requires both relevant CIs within±.5%, not merely a nonsignificant p-value. No result promotes a new estimator, teacher, ensemble, gate or public default. The credible conclusion must distinguish evidence for a matched reuse effect from proof of its unique mechanism.

The reviewed numerical interpretation and conditional Round59 proposal follow below. Round59 is **not started**. All final source hashes, locks, solver accounting, preserved memory and replay checks are recorded in final_audit.json.


## Final scientific interpretation

**DATA_EFFICIENCY_DOMINANT under the frozen decision rule.** The matched intervention provides no reproducible evidence that reusing discovery rows is the main cause of Joint downside. Its source-median RMSE effect is -0.0734% (95% CI -1.4291% to +0.1452%), mean -1.0104%; all leave-one-source-out medians remain negative (-0.1001% to -0.0526%). This does not prove a zero effect: the interval admits meaningful harm and does not satisfy the preregistered equivalence criterion.

Independent half-sample estimation loses 2.7879% versus full reuse (CI loss 1.4376% to 5.2032%). Restoring all estimation rows on the half-discovered structures improves RMSE by a median 2.3743% (CI 1.2997% to 4.0488%), but half discovery/full estimation still loses 0.3428% versus full reuse (CI -0.8348% to +0.2046% gain). On one frozen full-data structure, restoring estimation rows alone improves RMSE by 1.9830% median. These contrasts support data efficiency more directly than the signed A/B main effects, which average over arbitrary labels.

Do not subtract independent medians: half/full has +0.4079% median gain versus Base while full reuse has +0.1560%, yet their paired contrast is negative. Mean gains are +0.0366% and +2.7701% respectively. Independent halves have -0.8710% median and -7.7378% mean gain versus Base. Yacht is especially influential: full reuse +14.716%, independent halves -86.497%, half/full -28.078% as source means. Source-level estimates and leave-one-out results are retained rather than hiding this tail.

Full reuse has 20/85 material negative splits versus Base (threshold -0.1% gain); half/full has 33/85. Full reuse's worst split is synchronous_machine/58004 at -16.7767%; its independent estimate loses 58.1025%. Thus separation does not make the candidate reliably safe. Individual DB_EF has a -336.6942% worst split, also retained in the protocol table. No downside floor or gate is inferred.

### Structure, values and mechanistic predictions

Mean weighted Jaccard is 0.4453; median pair overlap 0.5625, state-partition ARI 0.8493 and Base H prediction correlation 0.8982. Realized A/B capacities agree in only 35/85 contexts, despite identical nominal budgets. Median cells are 2599/2685 and components 21/22. Value-estimation predictions on H, at exactly fixed structure, correlate at median 0.9324. Structure changes and value changes both exist, but their presence does not establish a dominant causal mechanism.

Source correlations with reuse downside are weak and uncertain: weighted Jaccard rho -0.0662 (CI -0.5434 to 0.4407), discovery optimism rho +0.1814 (CI -0.3416 to 0.6301), estimation optimism rho +0.0049 (CI -0.6198 to 0.5434). No undefined bootstrap draws were discarded in these three analyses. No predictor is converted into routing.

Train-only expected-effect accuracy is 50/85 = 58.82%. Exact operational mechanism-label accuracy is 8/85 = 9.41%; inclusion in a multi-label diagnosis is 58/85 = 68.24%. These are different tasks and neither is accuracy against known real-world causal truth. Among the 20 full-reuse failures, the overlapping diagnostic labels occur: OBJECTIVE_EFFECT 20, SUPPORT_SHIFT 16, VALUE_INSTABILITY 16, STRUCTURE_INSTABILITY 13, DATA_EFFICIENCY 8, DISCOVERY_REUSE 4, ESTIMATION_REUSE 2. In particular, OBJECTIVE_EFFECT records a zero/current-penalty response and is not proof that penalty caused every loss.

### Historical failures and successes

Pyrim/55002 full reuse remains -22.1057% versus Base; independent halves and half/full both fall to -138.8036%. In empty half-scaffolds, changing E cannot change coefficients and the discovery-derived intercept remains: this is a scaffold/data-efficiency limitation, not an identified coefficient-reuse effect. Pollution/57002 becomes a full-scaffold no-op under the new C/H stopping partition, unlike its archived Round57 -11.00%; its old failure is not overwritten or claimed cured. Other Pollution splits remain negative and separation often worsens them.

The six prespecified strong sources retain 80.84% of the sum of their full-reuse gains under half/full, but this does not pass the discovery-reuse rule: downside increases and paired improvement is absent. CPU gains fall 31.693% to 24.505%, Fishcatch 14.817% to 11.378%; Pol increases 6.448% to 6.646%. Independent halves erase Airfoil's +6.063% gain (-12.010%) and Sulfur's +3.939% (-2.093%). This is loss of useful fit, not a generally beneficial regularizer.

### Oracle, penalty and averaging controls

Oracle truth MSE with lambda5 has medians 0.03095 using all rows, 0.06008 on A and 0.06386 on B. At n=128/512/2048 the mean full-estimation truth MSE falls 0.15049/0.04225/0.01124; half-estimation remains roughly twice as large. Oracle is better than matched-size random structure in every generator's aggregate full-estimation comparison. Random and oracle cells match in all 216 contexts. Random controls sometimes contain true components: use canonical, order-independent overlap from oracle_capacity_audit.csv; the original oracle.csv overlap field is an ordered-tuple proxy and can undercount reversed-pair matches.

The oracle symmetric reuse/independent contrast is exactly zero by construction. It cannot identify ESTIMATION_REUSE or prove DISCOVERY as the unique alternative. This is an explicit limit of the requested oracle contrast, not a favorable oracle outcome.

Without penalty, prospective independent gain falls to -17.3847% median versus Base and the matched effect to -0.7284%, compared with -0.8710% and -0.0734% at lambda5. All primary lambda5 solves converge. There are 146 zero-penalty iteration-limit exits (60 historical, 12 prospective, 74 scaling); no retry, omission or rescue was performed. Their losses cannot be attributed solely to the exact unregularized optimum. The 194 nonconverged result rows include ensemble propagation and must not be counted as 194 solver calls.

Prediction averaging improves independent halves by median 3.5372% and matched reuse halves by 3.9948%. Cross-swap versus reuse ensemble still gives -0.0602% median: the averaging benefit is not evidence for sample independence. Both ensembles cost about twice the memory/inference and are not promoted.

### Sample size and computational implications

The independence cost often shrinks with more rows, but not monotonically. NYC goes from -1.7418% versus Base at 5k to +0.0067% at full349100; full reuse ends at +0.4789%. SGEMM independent gain is -2.0486% at5k and +0.2378% at full144960, with nonmonotonic intermediate values. Superconduct goes from +0.5414% at5k to +4.0390% at full12756, still below full reuse's +7.8930%. Do not infer a universal sample-size threshold or deploy a size-based gate.

Prospective source-median ratios versus Base: full reuse fit1.526x, batch inference0.995x, deep memory0.995x; D_A E_B fit1.018x, inference0.966x, deep0.991x; D_A E_FULL fit1.146x, inference0.964x, deep0.991x; cross-swap fit2.030x, inference1.930x, deep1.893x. The apparent sub-1 inference differences are small local timing variation, not a demonstrated runtime improvement. These fit numbers are reconstructed estimator costs, not full cold workflow latency; the exclusions described above remain applicable.

## Close, leave open, and conditional Round59

Close half-sample independence, cross-swap and split-ratio tuning as general rescue mechanisms for this frozen Joint candidate. Close the claim that an oracle A/B exchange can uniquely distinguish discovery bias from estimation bias. Keep the conditional unresolved mechanism question open, without assuming that a better model must result.

Recommended Round59 only if genuinely additional independent labels are available: a preregistered full-size independent-pool control, holding realized scaffold, intercept, stopping information and estimation sample size fixed. Its purpose would be to remove the specific remaining confounds, not optimize a split on these observed datasets. If this prerequisite is unavailable, stop this line instead of repeating it. No Round59 experiment, code path, data acquisition or fit is started here.

The answer to the central question is therefore: **no reliable matched benefit from separating discovery and estimation is demonstrated; data loss dominates this intervention. A unique discovery-versus-value bias decomposition is not established.** Core remains frozen; ALWAYS_JOINT remains KEEP_RESEARCH_ONLY. The original Round51 remainder remains explicit.

Replay verified 456 contexts and 48048 metric values, maximum relative discrepancy 2.9923e-16. Eight fixture tests passed. All 12168 research calls are accounted for (1368 discovery,10800 estimation), with fixture calls separate. Final artifact integrity, original-history preservation, privacy and public-core hashes are verified by final_audit.json and final_manifest.json.

## Design and identification boundary

Each seed allocates disjoint D,E,T and extra stopping C/diagnostic H. D and E have identical N, nested across the preregistered feasible sizes5k/10k/25k/50k/100k/250k. Primary N is the largest feasible value<=25k, chosen only from usable row count. T20%,C5%,H5%; no test information fits, selects, or stops a model. Vocabularies learn discovery rows only. Fixed C is shared across arms, and H generates a hashed expected-effect label before T scoring. These extra labeled pools are disclosed and are not silently counted as zero data cost.

The causal primary effect is the mean of the two paired relative gains D_TO_E vs REUSE_D and E_TO_D vs REUSE_E, with exactly the same structure and estimation N in each comparison. This differs from R58's pooled-RMSE ratio, and R58 already contained an equal-N half factorial. R59's novelty is the large-N/source extension and explicit practical comparison, not a newly invented identification contrast. Independent uses2N unique fitting rows versusN for reuse; FullReuse also uses2N. Base_D/Base_E and Base_FULL distinguish corresponding budgets; LightGBM uses2N with the single frozen300-tree reference.

Discovery fixes boundaries, categories, selected features/pairs, masks, addresses, component order, dimensions, and intercept. Realized capacity may differ between the D/E discoveries, but never between two value fits on the same discovery. The discovery-derived intercept remains target-dependent: no claim of an independently learned intercept or complete discovery-information removal is made. H4 versus H5 is not uniquely identified by labeling factorial contrasts. Oracle has no learned discovery and its symmetric exchange is algebraically identical; an oracle null cannot logically prove the absence of selection-induced reuse bias.

Random-row draws target the empirical row distribution. Repeated entities, geography and time dependence in sources such as Tallo, airlines and rainfall prevent a claim of external cluster/time independence. Full raw-row duplicates and missing targets were removed by the frozen source preparation rule; no feature engineering or target clipping was selected from results.

Value metrics compare D/E/F coefficients and H/test predictions at the same scaffold: coefficient/prediction correlation, norm difference, cell and main/pair contribution disagreement. Full values are included, not just the half comparison. See value_summary.csv and value_stability.csv. Support comparisons use the same frozen cell addresses in discovery, estimation and test, counting occupied/rare/unseen/suppressed cells and total-variation drift; see support_summary.csv. Unseen under an estimation sample and permanently suppressed by discovery are distinct quantities.

DISCOVERY_TRANSFER_SCORE is E-fit utility relative to the frozen intercept on E and is explicitly an **in-sample** diagnostic; independent H utility is also recorded. VALUE_TRANSFER_SCORE evaluates D-fitted values on E at the same D scaffold. Neither is used as a gate or claimed an unbiased test of the entire learning process. Source correlation is exploratory and does not by itself establish a failure cause.

## Failures, successes and sample size

Primary prospective splits rescued from a reuse loss: 4; newly harmful splits introduced: 3 (material threshold.1%). 3 sources have reuse mean gain>=3%; independent retained fraction of their summed gains: 0.9589110015675096. This is descriptive stratification on observed outcomes, not a selection rule. Full split details and loss tails are in failures.csv; no worst source is removed.

scaling.csv reports every N. statistical_efficiency.csv reports RMSE and unique fit rows for Base, reuse, independent,full and LightGBM. C/H extra row counts are separate. N is never chosen by test quality; the largest N<=25k primary rule is frozen. Report monotonicity only if observed; do not create a threshold gate from these curves.

Known three-feature conditional truth cannot be called a missed two-feature truth pair; this is stored separately. False pair identities need not imply zero predictive utility. Oracle uses the exact known coded truth space and fixedzero intercept; D/E/F estimation sizes differ only in the full control. The smooth generator is evaluated on64 discrete support locations with exact additive oracle lookup; it is not a proof for arbitrary continuous functions. Random target permutations occur independently within pools; they preserve marginal distributions but break feature-target dependence. Observed negative-control reuse gains are diagnostic, never a reason to tune penalty.

## Costs and reference models

All ratios below use public BASE_FULL at the same2N budget. A lower-N discovery is cheaper partly because it uses less data, not because the solver changed. costs.csv retains absolute fit/solve/preprocessing timings and statistical_efficiency.csv keeps row budgets. No ensemble is a candidate or required here.

Fits are reconstructed from discovery, mapping, design, solve and deployment; orchestration, persistence and shared probe transformations are not included in the standalone estimate. Three warm raw-input inference repetitions are local exploratory measurements. Deep retained/pickle memory is measured for every model. Whole-context peak RSS median 177.54MiB, maximum 1641.01MiB includes shared diagnostics and allocator carryover, not isolated production peaks. No hardware-general speed claim.

## Decision, hypothesis status and research boundary

Decision: **CLOSED_WITH_STRONG_EVIDENCE**. Hypotheses: `{"H1": "MEANINGFUL_MEDIAN_BENEFIT_NOT_SUPPORTED", "H2": "EQUIVALENCE_SUPPORTED", "H3": "MATERIAL_HARM_NOT_SUPPORTED", "H4": "NOT_IDENTIFIED", "H5": "NOT_IDENTIFIED"}`. A strong closure requires excluding a>=.5%median benefit and showing FullReuse no worse within.1%, with sufficient sources and directional consistency. Nonsignificance alone cannot close the line. Reopening requires positive CI,>=.5%median, source/seed consistency and leave-one-out robustness on new sources. No public promotion follows either decision.

The reviewed interpretation follows. Round60 remains NOT_STARTED; Round51 remains INCOMPLETE_WITH_EXPLICIT_REMAINDER. Final hash, continuity and privacy checks are recorded in final_audit.json.


## Reviewed real-data interpretation

The complete prospective primary panel contains 10 new sources × 5 seeds. At equal N per estimation phase and the identical discovery scaffold, independent values have a source-median RMSE gain of **+0.006844%**, source-bootstrap 95% CI **[-0.073465%, +0.124734%]**, mean +0.065883%. Win/tie/loss at the frozen 0.1% tolerance is 2/7/1. Leave-one-source-out medians range from -0.002994% to +0.016682%. The interval lies inside the preregistered ±0.5% equivalence region and excludes the material median benefit required to reopen the line. This is evidence against a systematic material reuse penalty in this frozen candidate and sampled population, not proof that every dataset has exactly zero reuse effect.

The practical answer is stronger: with the same total budget of 2N fitting rows, Independent_N+N loses **1.353764%** RMSE against FullReuse_2N (gain CI **[-2.598506%, -1.004571%]**; mean gain -2.211281%). All 10 source means favor FullReuse. Every leave-one-source-out median remains negative, between -1.360747% and -1.346782%. Thus splitting the available budget is not justified as a general remedy.

Restoring all estimation rows while retaining the N-discovered scaffold improves independent estimation by median **1.223839%** (CI [0.983228%, 2.379255%]); all 10 source means improve. Nevertheless, N-discovery/2N-estimation still loses **0.211272%** against full discovery/full estimation (gain CI [-0.383348%, -0.039456%]). These paired controls support using the available data in both phases more directly than signed D/E factorial main effects.

Reuse_N gains +0.299542% median versus Base_N, Independent_N+N +0.491893% versus Base_N, and FullReuse_2N +0.499995% versus Base_2N. Their respective CIs include zero. These are different denominators and budgets: subtracting these three medians would not recover the paired causal effect. FullReuse remains 3.528148% worse than the fixed LightGBM reference in the source median (gain CI [-7.513522%, -1.785542%]); 9/10 source means favor LightGBM. No Joint/public promotion follows from closing this diagnosis.

### Heterogeneity and downside

Essex is the genuine local exception: independent values gain +1.411439% versus reuse, positive in all five seeds, but still lose 0.473929% against full reuse. This single source must not be concealed, generalized to the panel, or turned into a dataset gate. The worst causal source mean is ae_price_prediction at -0.825112%; its worst seed loses 2.745558%. The worst practical seed is also ae_price_prediction/59003, losing 12.263499% against FullReuse.

At the -0.1% split threshold, Reuse has 8/50 losses versus its same-N Base, Independent 7/50, and FullReuse 7/50 versus Base_2N. Independence rescues four reuse failures but introduces three others. Source-level losses exceeding 0.5% / 1% / 2% are 1/0/0 for reuse, 0/0/0 for independent, and 0/0/0 for full reuse. For the causal independent-minus-reuse effect they are 1/0/0; for the practical independent-minus-full effect 9/8/3. Worst losses against the corresponding Base are 2.176913% (reuse), 0.609921% (independent), and 0.999929% (full).

The three sources with reuse mean gain ≥3% are SoilHydroDB, Tallo and ae_price_prediction. Independent retains **95.8911%** of their summed reuse gains, with the deterioration concentrated in ae_price_prediction. This post-outcome stratification is descriptive only. It does not override the all-source primary result.

Historical primary evidence is consistent: median causal gain +0.017966%, CI [-0.065304%, +0.067635%], whereas the practical gain is -1.138900%, CI [-1.913335%, -0.554729%]. Historical sources are supporting evidence, not new confirmation.

### What stability does and does not identify

Prospective mean weighted component Jaccard is 0.577964, pair overlap 0.606839, state-partition ARI 0.849657 and selection-order rank correlation 0.658106. Realized D/E capacities match in only 14/50 contexts; within a fixed D scaffold all value variants have exactly the same capacity. Median D/E coefficient correlation is 0.661477, but prediction correlation is 0.952544 on H and 0.950772 on test. Coefficient instability is therefore not equivalent to equally large predictive instability, particularly with overlapping coded contributions.

Exploratory source correlations of causal gain with weighted Jaccard and pair overlap are -0.103030 and -0.127273. These do not establish an instability mechanism. The ARI correlation is undefined with the available missing entries and is retained as null, not silently converted to evidence. H4 and H5 remain NOT_IDENTIFIED: the factorial contrasts do not uniquely attribute the exchange effect to discovery or estimation, and the known-structure oracle exchange is algebraically symmetric.

### Cost and scope

Relative to Base_FULL, source-median fit ratios are 0.808x/0.809x for Reuse_D/E, 0.789x/0.827x for D_TO_E/E_TO_D, 1.042x/1.101x for D_FULL/E_FULL, and **1.538x** for FullReuse. The smaller-discovery arms use fewer fitting rows; they are not proof of a faster solver. FullReuse inference is 1.0005x and retained deep memory 0.9980x Base_FULL. Same-scaffold comparisons and serialized bytes are retained in the cost artifacts.

The measurements are local warm exploratory timings, not a second-hardware replication. The interrupted ACSIncome context combines cached pre-interruption discovery timings with resumed work; no timing-based scientific choice is made. Python-recursive deep memory does not necessarily include LightGBM's native allocations, so its deep-memory figure is not a complete native-memory comparison. Whole-context RSS includes shared diagnostic models and allocator carryover.

Interpretation remains bounded by the frozen discovery-derived intercept, shared stopping C, additional labeled C/H rows, selected real sources and random-row dependence assumptions. These limits are not removed by calling D and E independent. The result closes the general splitting remedy for the tested candidate; it does not prove a universal theorem about all discovery procedures.

### Transfer, support and large N

At scaffold D, discovery-transfer utility is 36.0849% on the E rows used to estimate values, but only 31.6362% on independent diagnostic H; the former is explicitly optimistic in-sample utility. D-fitted values transferred to E have 31.7311% utility versus the fixed intercept. The symmetric E-scaffold figures are comparable. Full estimation raises H utility to 32.7295%/32.6603%, consistent with data efficiency. These scores use the frozen intercept as reference, not Base, and must not be read as Joint-vs-Base gains or used as admission rules.

Independent estimation assigns about 0.9856% (D scaffold) / 0.9467% (E scaffold) of its lookup mass to cells absent in discovery; total-variation support drift is about 0.0511/0.0536. On test, cells absent in the estimation sample account for about 0.0323%/0.0357% of lookup mass for independent estimation and 0.00346%/0.00379% after full estimation. Discovery-suppressed assignments are separate (~0.66%/1.05% on test). Occupancy and rare-cell fractions use allocated entries, including unused capacity; they are not percentages of samples. Support diagnostics do not identify a unique causal failure mechanism.

At large N no material independence benefit emerges: at N=250,000 the causal source means are +0.010311% for Airlines and -0.006666% for ACSIncome; at N=100,000 Tallo is +0.003753%. Their practical gains remain negative: -0.184379%, -0.172270%, and -0.278031% respectively. Across available prospective sources, causal medians at N=5k/10k/25k/50k/100k/250k are -0.001497%, +0.009124%, +0.016682%, +0.008233%, +0.003753%, +0.001822%. Source membership changes with N (10/6/5/3/3/2), so this sequence is not a balanced-panel causal slope. Some historical large-N practical exceptions remain visible: Buzz and SGEMM at N=50k favor independent estimation by +0.301389% and +0.438229%. They do not replace the prospective primary conclusion.


## Reviewed synthetic controls and verification limits

All 720 preregistered synthetic contexts and 30 shuffled controls completed. These are six generator families, not 720 independent real datasets. Additive and smooth truth does not depend on the interaction-strength label: 120 of the 720 labelled contexts repeat an otherwise identical generating configuration, leaving 600 distinct configurations including seed. No synthetic interval is presented as a real-source population inference.

At N=1,000, independent estimation can provide a small synthetic benefit: family-mean causal gains are +0.4090% additive, +0.5088% discontinuous, +0.7360% many-distractors and +0.3597% smooth. At N=10,000 they fall to +0.0012%, -0.0027%, +0.0543% and +0.0402% respectively. Product and conditional causal gains at N=10,000 are +0.0220% and -0.0023%. This does not show a material reuse penalty emerging as N grows. Practical gains are generally negative and can be very large in magnitude, e.g. product at N=5,000 (-56.9884% family mean) and many-distractors at N=1,000 (-33.0993%). Conditional at N=5,000 is an exception (+0.5386% practical gain), not concealed or used to choose a method.

True pair recall averaged over settings is 58.33%/55.83%/60.83% for product under D/E/F discovery; 97.50%/94.17%/100% for many-distractors; 97.50%/95%/100% for discontinuous. The three-way conditional truth is not exactly representable by pair terms, even though some pairs can approximate useful projections. Identity-based false-pair fractions are therefore not proof of useless predictive terms. In particular, coded pair tables can contain additive contributions, so the false-pair identity fraction of 1 for purely additive/smooth truth is not a measured false-positive prediction rate.

False-pair-fraction associations with the causal effect are heterogeneous. Product/discontinuous at N=1,000 show exploratory Spearman correlations +0.6389/+0.7255, but at N=10,000 these are +0.0520/-0.0536; many-distractors correlations are -0.1178/-0.1108/+0.1058 across N. Generator settings and seeds are correlated controls, not independent source-level confirmation. There is no universal false-discovery explanation established here.

Oracle symmetric reuse/independent exchange is exactly zero by construction: the same known data-independent structure is used in both directions. Full oracle estimation has median truth MSE 0.001109 versus 0.002416/0.002674 for the D/E estimates. These unpaired aggregate medians illustrate estimation variance but do not identify H4 versus H5. The oracle retains the current positive penalty and is not an unregularized task-optimal upper bound.

All 30 shuffled contexts retain zero coefficient cells in all seven Joint arms; causal gain and Joint-vs-same-scaffold-Base gain are exactly zero. This checks the frozen discovery/stopping behavior under these nulls. It has no power to measure coefficient-reuse bias conditional on a nonempty selected structure. Small practical differences under shuffling are intercept/sample-size effects, not evidence of learned signal.

## Partial checkpoints, execution audit and numerical fidelity

Five historical Buzz contexts at N=100,000 hit the unchanged public 512 MiB allocation guard when discovering FULL on 200,000 rows. The four completed equal-N arms were recovered from checkpoints without refitting, yielding mean causal gain -0.0005705% across five seeds. Their FullReuse comparison is unavailable. These rows remain an explicitly posthoc secondary supplement in partial_scaling_replay.csv and do not replace failed full controls or enter primary source aggregation.

Research work comprises 13,585 completed calls: 3,115 public discoveries, 9,435 value/oracle solves and 1,035 LightGBM reference fits. There are five explicit capacity rejections and one user-interrupted unfinished solve. The latter had no completed coefficient checkpoint and alone was repeated on resume. Previously valid checkpoint artifacts are checked against the saved resume inventory. Eight research tests passed; 22 fixture calls and 12 oracle truth least-squares checks are separate from research call counts.

The initial replay assertion was too strict about the fixed intercept: the already-frozen Round52 solver returns (b / target_scale) * target_scale with free_intercept=False. In 603 checks this differs from the discovery input by exactly one ULP at most (maximum absolute difference 7.276e-12). The verifier now checks exact equality to that existing round-trip operation, not a new tolerance. Models, coefficients, predictions, protocol, execution freeze and decision thresholds were not changed. The original verifier, failed log and diagnostic are retained. The intercept is fixed as an optimization parameter, but is not promised bitwise identical to its pre-normalization representation.

The local sandbox initially blocked loading the existing LightGBM DLL (WinError4551). Approved execution outside sandbox used the same environment and frozen code, with no installation or network operation. Timings spanning the interruption remain exploratory. Original completed rounds and the explicit unfinished Round51 remainder are preserved. Stale current-branch started flags are corrected with their old values archived; historical round entries are unchanged.

## Final decision and conditional next step

**DISCOVERY_ESTIMATION_SPLITTING: CLOSED_WITH_STRONG_EVIDENCE**, under the preregistered material-effect criterion and the stated scope. H1 has no supported material median benefit; H2 equivalence is supported within ±0.5%; H3 material harm is not supported. H4/H5 remain NOT_IDENTIFIED rather than forced into a causal label.

Close additional split-ratio, sample-size or penalty sweeps intended to rescue this general remedy, and do not create routing or confidence-to-alpha rules. Keep the frozen Joint research-only and the public core unchanged. A local Essex benefit does not overturn the same-total-data control or authorize a dataset-specific policy.

**RETRY_IF** naturally independent, already-labelled data become available at no additional acquisition/label cost. Any retry must preregister the source, fixed model/objective, independently fixed intercept/stopping where feasible, same-scaffold/equal-N comparison, all-available-data control and untouched test before outcomes. Preserve this round's result rather than relabeling a selected favorable cohort as replication.

**Round60 proposal: no automatic continuation on this branch.** Only if that new-data condition is actually met, consider a preregistered cost-free independent-cohort replication addressing the residual intercept/stopping and natural-cohort identification limits. Otherwise stop discovery/estimation splitting research. No Round60 model, gate, tuning or experiment has been started, and no old Round51 remainder has been silently completed.


## Replay completion

Replay passed for **1037 contexts**, including both fixtures, with **44763 metric/objective views** and maximum relative error **2.9894e-16**. All 145 prospective locks precede test evaluation. Matched independent/reuse cost ratios (source medians) are fit **1.000512x**, inference **1.003213x**, deep memory **1.000000x**. All five Buzz partial recoveries remain secondary only. Final files are covered by final_manifest.json after the final audit.


# CODADAPT ROUND 60 — Oblique coded states

**Decision: TOO_EXPENSIVE for the tested ordinary-estimation prototype. KEEP_RESEARCH_ONLY.**
There is a real-development quality signal, not just a synthetic win: median RMSE gain
4.147418% across 12 regression sources, but its source-bootstrap 95% CI
[-0.202632%, +12.453771%] includes zero. Three sources lose, including Diabetes in every
split. Costs fail the fixed development gate. No independent confirmation or public promotion.
Historical novelty remains **HISTORICAL_OVERLAP_UNRESOLVED**.

## Protocol and history, including the deviation

Round17's available memory says joint basis/Nystrom, promising development and failed
confirmation. Its original discovery/discovery2 source and reports are absent. Equivalence
to sparse 2–4-feature pre-quantization projection cannot be established or excluded.
Round14 learned per-code low-rank latent factors, which is mechanically different.
See round60_history_check.md and round60/prior_art.md. No novelty claim is made.

The initial gate required >=10% RMSE improvement over Base and >=3% over a 64-cell
true-support pair grid, positive in >=4/5 seeds, on at least two of five families.
Only one passed. **That preregistered result remains FAIL.** However, all five had
positive oracle gains. The agent-chosen numerical gate was stricter than the user's
stop-if-no-useful-oracle-advantage instruction. scope_correction.md explicitly records
the post-outcome continuation. It is exploratory evidence, not a retrospectively passed
primary gate. extension_freeze.json and real_extension_protocol.json froze that work.
The original protocol, initial status and summaries remain preserved as intermediate records.
final_summary.json and this report are the final interpretation.

The real recipe was fixed before any real outcomes: robust train median/IQR, bounded
train-only supports/directions, 64 allocated states per memory, four ordinary stagewise
residual memories, lambda5. No validation/test-driven direction or per-dataset recipe.
No confidence, OOF, independence split or Joint-penalty search. Confirmation was
conditional on the real-development gate; that gate fails fit and inference. Validation
median gain is 3.383986%, seven sources meet the >=1%/4-positive-seed criterion, but
fit is 13.224249x and single-row inference 1.637228x, above the frozen 3x/1.5x ceilings.

## Requested final accounting

1. **Fit/solve calls:** 2,710 checkpointed model/correction/solve calls.
   Counts by stage: {"oracle": 175, "diagnostic": 600, "rotation": 410, "probes": 235, "extension": 310, "real_development": 420, "decomposition": 125, "fine_pairs": 75, "attribution": 285, "main_capacity": 75}. These calls can contain multiple
   tables and candidate aggregates; internal candidate aggregate updates and direction
   linear-algebra solves were not separately instrumented, so this is NOT their exact total.
   Thirty prior Base fits and fifteen compatible prior Joint solves were reused. Thirty
   redundant zero-angle Round60 fits were found and disclosed; they are counted, not
   independent evidence. No concluded prior round was rerun. Technical fixture fits separate.
2. **Synthetic cases:** all 20 named generators, five seeds. Initial oracle25 contexts,
   diagnostic100, rotation50, extension100, representation probes30 and decomposition25.
   These are repeated views, not hundreds of independent datasets. step/discontinuous share
   a generator; sine/scaled views share underlying signal. Cases and outputs remain explicit.
3. **Real development:** 15 tasks x5 splits: 12 regression and3 binary classification,
   14 source families because SDSS shares a source across targets. Historical Round51 splits
   and Base models reused on six tasks. Other nine cached sources use at most50k source rows
   selected by a fixed target-independent seed, then60/20/20 train/validation/test. SDSS
   keeps all100k historical rows to preserve cache identity. Known-source development only.
4. **Real confirmation:** zero. Not started because the fixed validation cost gate failed.
   This is not a measured failure to transfer to new real sources.
5. **History overlap:** HISTORICAL_OVERLAP_UNRESOLVED; source absence prevents equivalence audit.
6. **Base rotation sensitivity:** average case/seed normalized RMSE variance0.028881;
   worst/best ratio1.696593 over sine/step and five rotations.
7. **Projected rotation sensitivity:** one discovered correction variance0.028279,
   worst/best1.678472. Improvement is small and not uniform: sine slightly worsens;
   step improves. Raw-target true-direction oracle is invariant; the Base-residual
   oracle remains orientation-sensitive. LGBM0.026501, HGB0.023796 variance.
8. **Oracle projection gain:** initial residual-oracle gains2.22–12.11% RMSE, five positive
   seeds in every initial family. The raw-target oracle can do much better, but removes
   Base and has privileged direction knowledge; it is not a same-scaffold deployable bound.
9. **Discovered projection gain:** initial18-case panel one-projection regression median
   +5.984616% (16 regression case means); classification+.118957 AUC on only XOR/diagonal.
   Median recovery of the known-direction oracle's MSE improvement on five2D families
   is98.351892%. An oracle of known raw direction need not optimize the Base residual;
   recovery can exceed100% and is not clipped. Real ordinary4 gain below.
10. **Random control:** real random4 regression median+1.162796%; supervised4+4.147418%.
    Synthetic random-support controls are generally weak. The initial random64 control
    used true support and is distinguished from later truly random-support controls.
11. **Pair comparison:** real four64-cell grids +0.704706% and one256-cell grid +0.809121%
    median gain versus Base. Projected4 has paired source-median RMSE advantages2.663264%
    over pair4 and2.953978% over fine_pair256. On product/XOR, grids are stronger controls;
    a single oblique coordinate is not a universal interaction representation.
12. **Memory-normalized comparison:** four projections, four pair tables and four univariate
    tables each add256 allocated cells. Equal cells are not identical bytes: projection
    edges/weights and pair edges differ. Actual retained/serialized bytes are reported.
    Four univariate64-state corrections recover2.608476% versus Base; projected4 beats
    that control by only0.959742% paired median RMSE. Thus some gain is added main-effect
    resolution, not uniquely an oblique-interaction effect. Finite measured frontiers only.
13. **2-feature results:** a useful signal exists. On five initial oblique cases, median
    gains at1/2/4/8/16 memories are6.146891/10.714662/10.386984/9.869704/8.108950%.
    More memories are not monotonically better. Simultaneous4 median6.303402%.
14. **3-feature results:** residual oracle+20.193945%; corresponding64/256-cell grids
    +10.076921/+15.352837%. Discovered3-feature direction+1.824%: substantial discovery gap.
15. **4-feature results:** residual oracle+47.491547%; 256-cell4D grid+12.050030%; discovered
    4-feature direction+11.680%. The64-cell4D grid has only one nonmissing bin per axis
    under the missing-inclusive convention and is degenerate; do not use it as evidence
    of a fair strong competitor. Higher grids are k-dimensional controls, despite legacy
    arm names pair64/pair256. Allocated and occupied capacity differ.
16. **Projection stability:** synthetic initial medians support Jaccard1.0, absolute raw
    weight cosine.999259, prediction correlation.777261. Real first-support Jaccard.333333,
    weight cosine.356497, full-correction correlation.524556. Real correlations use common
    source probes mixing fit/heldout membership across splits, not independent test evidence.
17. **Residual signal:** real best single64-state correction median+1.336558%, one projected
    +1.800947%, pair1+.415175%. residual_mse_recovered in paired CSV records squared-error
    reduction separately from RMSE percentages. Projection-to-main/pair decomposition is
    in decomposition_results.csv and measures contribution energy, not target task gain.
18. **Real regression gain:** +4.147418% median, source-bootstrap CI includes zero.
19. **Real classification gain:** +0.011218 AUC median on3 known tasks;
    gains Adult+.011218, SDSS STAR+.027233, breast cancer+.001521. No classification promotion.
20. **Wins/ties/losses:** regression9/0/3 using +/-0.1% RMSE; classification3/0/0 using
    +/-0.001 AUC. These are source/task means, not a claim of9 independent stable wins.
21. **LightGBM gap:** median Base RMSE18.762558% worse; projected16.354310% worse.
    Paired median recovery of material positive source gaps15.829638%. Different medians
    must not be subtracted to infer that recovery. LGBM fixed300/31/.05/lambda1, one thread.
22. **Fit overhead:** regression13.224249x; classification2.899888x.
    Includes Base plus mapping and search/ordinary values; mapping timer includes preparation
    of all partitions. Historical Base times reused for Round51, so local ratios are
    exploratory, not controlled cross-hardware speed measurements. Prefix1 times in the
    real table are upper bounds from the shared four-memory run, NOT measured one-memory fit.
23. **Inference overhead:** regression single-row1.637228x; classification1.579439x.
    Timings start after common categorical vocabulary conversion. profile.csv separately
    times normalization, projection, quantization, gather and accumulation on saved
    one-projection synthetic models, with explicit one-thread warm sequential replay.
    Batch10k there is tiled, not a new large dataset. Stage timers are not additive walltime.
24. **Memory overhead:** regression deep1.266775x, serialized1.380962x; classification
    deep1.289648x, serialized1.454549x.
    NumericMap currently retains unused quantile arrays even in robust mode, charged in
    full. No speculative optimized memory figure. Native LightGBM heap is not captured by
    Python deep traversal and is excluded from deep-memory Pareto claims.
25. **Quality-memory Pareto:** pareto.csv contains measured nondominance for each context,
    with actual bytes rather than equal-cell assertions. It is a finite local frontier,
    not evidence of a general deployment niche. Joint secondary deployment bytes unmeasured.
26. **Quality-latency Pareto:** same file covers measured latency paths; noisy warm timings
    and different batches are kept separate. The ordinary candidate fails its latency gate.
27. **Failure modes:** COMPUTATIONAL_COST established for this implementation; GENERALIZATION
    / PROJECTION_OVERFIT consistent with train improvements but Diabetes test deterioration;
    cause not uniquely identified. Higher-order oracle/discovery gaps support a bounded
    BAD_PROJECTION_DISCOVERY diagnosis. Quantization/normalization/missing comparisons
    are diagnostic, not a rule selected after test. Scale probes120 comparisons have
    zero bucket/prediction changes. Do not infer NO_REAL_OBLIQUE_SIGNAL from negative tasks.
28. **Prior art:** closely related to projection-pursuit/ridge-function approximation,
    sparse random features and oblique methods. No claim of scientific or internal novelty.
    See cited primary sources in prior_art.md; Nyström equivalence remains unresolved.
29. **Outcome:** TOO_EXPENSIVE for the tested prototype, with positive but unconfirmed
    real-development quality evidence. Not SYNTHETIC_ONLY, not REPRESENTATION_NOT_USEFUL,
    and not a confirmed PROMISING_NEW_REPRESENTATION.
30. **Close:** promotion of this implementation/recipe; larger blind projection-count
    searches; claims that representation is useless; any reopening of confidence-alpha,
    OOF gating or artificial discovery/estimation splitting without their separate conditions.
31. **RETRY_IF:** a behavior/statistically frozen cost-reduction experiment demonstrates
    a plausible discovery/inference budget, preserves existing quality and explicitly
    monitors Diabetes/bodyfat/wine downside, followed by untouched-source confirmation.
    Missing Round17/18 originals can reopen only the overlap audit, not erase these results.
32. **Proposed Round61, not started:** falsify whether repeated candidate quantization and
    direction scoring can be amortized while holding projection/statistical choices fixed;
    preregister cost ceilings and quality equivalence, then independent confirmation if
    passed. Do not rescue by penalty tuning, routing or a new architecture.

## Secondary estimator and scaffold attribution

Median regression gains versus Base: projected+existing fixed pair refit5.182857%;
no-native-pairs control-2.549615%; projection-only-14.085311%; frozen Base Joint1.040074%;
Base+projected Joint11.554285%. All120 reported Joint solutions converge;15 Base Joint
solutions reused from Round52,105 newly solved. No penalty or stopping sweep. Ordinary
estimation already improves development, so the signal does not exist only with Joint.
The secondary score does not replace the failed ordinary cost gate or authorize confirmation.
Removing native pairs or Base leaves coordinates discovered on Base residuals, so those
ablations are not optimized standalone learners and do not prove their universal weakness.

## Integrity, limitations and completion

No core/compiler/API/default file changed. Eleven public source hashes verified against
the pre-round snapshot; the complete prior Round59 seal checks53,923 files unchanged,
excluding the two intentionally updated continuity documents. Existing unrelated Git
changes were present before this task and are retained. research_private remains ignored;
docs/research remains trackable. No commit/push/tag/release or Round61 execution.
Round51's explicit unfinished remainder is preserved. NYC Taxi Fare remains
EXTERNAL_UNVERIFIED; green-taxi tips is a different task.

All fits are checkpointed; completed artifacts are hashed. Replays and metric checks are
reported in verification.json, extension_verification.json, secondary_verification.json
and final_audit.json. Secondary attribution verification checks persisted predictions,
metrics and hashes rather than claiming full deployed-model bitwise replay. Eleven private
tests pass; Ruff passes on the private round. Final manifest covers completed artifacts.
No cold/second-host timing claim. No unseen real-source generalization claim. The broad
regression CI, three-task classification panel, random-row dependence, historical base
timers, prototype memory overhead and post-primary continuation materially limit inference.

The bounded answer is: axis-aligned states can be less efficient on known oblique synthetic
structure; sparse projected states add useful quality on several known real tasks, partly
beyond equal-cell pair and additive controls. Whether this is a reproducible, cost-effective
new-data advantage remains unresolved. Research-only; stop here.

# CODADAPT ROUND 61 — COST COLLAPSE FOR OBLIQUE CODED STATES

Development cost classification: **COST_BREAKTHROUGH**. Final scientific conclusion: **QUALITY_COST_TRADEOFF_UNRESOLVED**.
The new-source sanity gate FAILED: fit3.058558x exceeds the frozen3x ceiling;
the RMSE confidence interval includes zero. No rounding into a pass, retuning, or promotion.
Known-source development; no public promotion, no definitive confirmation. Historical
overlap remains **HISTORICAL_OVERLAP_UNRESOLVED**. Round51's six explicit diagnostic
remainders remain incomplete. No Round62 execution.

## Reconstruction and frozen experiment

Round60 reference is ordinary stagewise Base + four 2-feature projections, 64 cells
each, robust median/IQR normalization, five candidate directions, lambda5, no stopping.
It is not the secondary Joint result. Reference SHA256: `7dc3cc5ebc2912146f14a6fa55fd97c1dd817679e34b33b4b8a6de6aa47c5b7d`.
Quantiles include the same missing state, stable candidate order and tie rules.
Fifteen known tasks / fourteen source families / five persisted splits, including
twelve regression sources. No source was added to recipe selection.
Seven first-seed profiling cases: California, Diabetes, Adult, SDSS redshift,
Superconduct, SGEMM, Buzz. All Round60 artifacts were retained and their seal checked.
The failed initial Round60 numerical gate and its disclosed exploratory scope correction
remain part of the history; this round does not relabel that primary gate as passed.

## Engineering and representation conclusions

The exact path caches train geometry and fixed-direction codes, computes residual curvature
once per stage, materializes each coordinate once, and avoids retaining rejected n-row arrays.
The deployment wrapper reduces metadata and unused feature conversion without changing any
weights, cuts or values. Exact compact single-row/batch ratios: {'1': 1.2843250524185246, '32': 1.2752010553953324, '1000': 1.299297885516124};
original path: {'1': 1.6705783870637398, '32': 1.727462180245041, '1000': 1.5276012485755879}. Exact deep ratios original/compact:
1.181128/1.067466.

Sparse chunking is verified separately from dense BLAS; any mismatched dense computation is
not admitted as exact. Batched results are microbenchmarks, not a silently substituted trainer.
Approximate threshold methods are likewise not installed into the frozen primary recipe.
Controlled scaling exponents over the measured ranges: {'n': 0.7356461019638701, 'p': 0.2330664411053611, 'candidates': 0.7604602020591504}.
    These are empirical local fits, not asymptotic guarantees.

Stability probes use fixed canonical rows only for
description, never fitting or selection. Empty/constant corrections give undefined correlation.

The13.22x historical cost is not wholly intrinsic: exact redundant work can be removed.
The stronger claim, preserving predictive gain at<=3x, must be judged by the explicit selected
recipe gate above, not by the fastest isolated screen or the best test-selected row.

rows but97 numeric features; its projection preparation alone exceeds the Base fit. Taiwan
and Garment have similarly tiny Base denominators. Metro/Tetouan are much larger yet cost
near1.1–1.2x. The same failure pattern is present in development Pyrim/bodyfat/Diabetes.
Small-source setup cost and feature count are consistent explanations; causality is not
identified from18 regression sources. Categories/sample sizes are correlated.

    Tiled small test sets are marked workload timings only. No genuine100k heldout in this panel,
    so no fabricated100k throughput result. Identical runtime pairs expose timing noise.
15. **Memory:** complete retained deep ratios {'development': 1.0420658842506696, 'mini': 1.020688868037512}; retained objects identical
    to Round62. These are source-median figures, not a uniform bound: {'worst_source_mean': 1.1090803740216209, 'sources_above_1_05': 7, 'max_ratio_vs_R62': 1.0}.
    Thus <=1.05× Base is not met on every source; no new retained-memory increase vs Round62.
    Peak process RSS, instrumented separate profile: old
    323.23 MiB, new 316.25 MiB.
    These are process peaks, not isolated heap or an assertion of paired RSS causality.
16. **Practical floor:** Tehran optimistic measured primitive subtotal
    47.535 ms, indicative ratio 2.498×.
    This excludes dispatch/selection overhead and is NOT a proven lower bound. The evidence does
    not establish that all possible exact implementations must exceed2.5×.
17. **Primary gate:** FAIL; P90 exceeds3×, including the timing-only CI. Strong gate also fails.
18. **Transfer cost executed:** NO, guarded before new-source acquisition.
19. **Transfer median/P90:** not measured.
20. **Quality transfer executed:** NO.
21. **Quality-transfer datasets:** 0. Reused six mini sources are not new confirmation.
22. **Inherited RMSE gain:** development +3.851963%; mini +2.069673%; no new quality claim.
23. **Inherited mini source-bootstrap CI:** [-0.021647%, +4.148475%], includes zero.
24. **Inherited mini win/tie/loss:** 4/1/1 at ±0.1% source gain;5/6 strictly positive.
25. **Downside:** worst source Tetouan −0.126727%, worst split −0.633635%;2/30 splits below−0.5%,
    none below−1% or−2%. These are inherited validated outputs, not new trials.
26. **LightGBM:** inherited development cheap oblique remains16.566993% worse RMSE;
    independent transfer gap recovery unmeasured.
27. **More-pair:** inherited development paired advantage +2.756929% vs pair4;
    no new independent control fit needed when outputs are identical.
28. **Joint state:** RATIO_GATE_FAILURE_LOW_ABSOLUTE_COST. Only above3× mini source adds <=100ms under the
    preregistered diagnostic. This does not waive the historical ratio gate.
29. **Close:** this exact-engineering gate attempt, and automatic progression to large confirmation.
    Do not keep adjusting implementation solely until a noisy threshold passes.
30. **Keep open:** a clearly bounded research-only use case with explicitly accepted absolute-fit
    latency; predictive transfer remains uncertain. No evidence for public default change.
31. **Round64 justified:** not as another unbounded gate chase. Only with a new preregistered,
    externally meaningful deployment question and explicit authorization.
32. **Proposed, not started:** independently validate an absolute-latency/memory envelope with the
    candidate frozen, reporting the failed historical gate unchanged. Alternatively stop this branch.

Ladder ordering is not randomized; only primary repeats are alternated. Some stages regress.
The metadata rung reuses existing caching and is a negative control, not an invented gain.
The final candidate was not selected per dataset. Batch1/4/8/16/32/64 sweep is in chunk_results.csv;
the frozen global memory policy is unchanged after observing these microbenchmarks.
Projected values are materialized once per block and released; thresholds need the whole projection,
so an exact statistical one-pass global quantizer is not claimed. Partition already existed in NumPy.
Only index metadata are cached: similar projection weights do not justify reusing sorted orders.
Float32 changes normalized values and is excluded. Runtime remains float64.

## Measurement limits and integrity

Rows-scanned figures are lower-count/proxy annotations, not hardware memory-traffic counters.
Function/object construction counts in calls.csv are not exhaustive Python list/dict/tuple or allocator counts.
Base fit, preparation (including offset predictions), correction and total are accounted separately.
No cost is hidden as preprocessing. CI resamples timing repetitions within each persisted split;
it does not establish independent hardware replication or population quality.
Public source SHA256 frozen before work; all11 public files unchanged. Recipe SHA256
51b89ff02d56f08212c458f77bde063f097c1a34add367463df534468d0ca63b unchanged.
Research source remains ignored by Git, research-memory documents remain trackable.
R17/18 historical-overlap uncertainty and R51 explicit incomplete remainder are preserved.
EBM_PACKAGING remains ENVIRONMENT_BLOCKED (inherited); no policy/DLL workaround attempted.

# Round 70 — completed real-gap tomography

# CODADAPT ROUND 70 — completed real-gap tomography

1. **Panel:** 24 previously observed regression datasets ×5 frozen outer splits =120 contexts. No new confirmation, no split changes.

2. **OOF:**120/120;360 folds;720 Base/teacher model prediction replays (hash-sealed replay reused during final audit);100% coverage, every training row predicted exactly once, zero known INTERNAL group/fingerprint overlap.

3. **Reused checkpoints:**45 original valid OOF contexts;171 valid final Base/LightGBM/HGB checkpoints retained. Final byte-identical reuse count including mappings: 189. These categories overlap and must not be added.

4. **Invalidated:**75 original row-fold contexts and their target-dependent probes remain stored but excluded by run_dir routing. Pre-repair object classifications: {'VALID': 1242, 'INVALID_OOF_GROUP_LEAKAGE': 1063, 'VALID_FINAL_FIT_ONLY': 171}. The1063 initially flagged objects comprise998 estimator/value solves and65 mappings; X-only geometry was subsequently recognized as reusable. The initial status table is preserved, not misrepresented as1063 invalid predictive fits.

5. **Refits:**75×6=450 corrected inner Base/LightGBM fits; dependent probe solves regenerated only where required.17 unnecessary X-only prototype geometry recomputations yielded identical arrays; disclosed in geometry_reuse_deviation.json. Interrupted uncheckpointed attempts are not falsely counted as completed or claimed zero.

6. **Completed accounting:**8438 unique estimator/value-solve checkpoint objects across old+active paths, plus 627 mapping checkpoints. Active: 1080 teacher/Base/HGB and 6360 probe estimators/value solves. A checkpoint is not necessarily one numerical solver invocation inside a library. Reused copies excluded from total; historical failed OOF fits included as superseded completed work.

7. **EXTERNAL_FINGERPRINT_OVERLAP:**Buzz, diamonds, green_taxi_tips, house_sales, pyrim, sdss_redshift, superconduct, wine_quality. Eight sources,31 affected contexts; all five splits of each source excluded in clean sensitivity. The four extra sources beyond the named Buzz/diamonds/superconduct/wine_quality are green_taxi_tips,house_sales,pyrim,sdss_redshift. Primary is not duplicate-independent.

8. **Primary / clean:**24×5 vs16×5; MIXED_NO_DOMINANT_MECHANISM vs MIXED_NO_DOMINANT_MECHANISM; panel-sensitive verdict: False. Both are reported regardless of favorability.

9. **Base→LightGBM RMSE gain:**primary 12.633648% (source-bootstrap95% CI [3.3779131443631027, 22.81361026772696]); clean 10.488402% (CI [-2.9895681421248925, 22.106749718383945]). Recovery uses MSE, not RMSE, is unclipped and undefined at teacher MSE improvement<=1%.

10. **Teacher delta stability:**{'correlation': 0.5322050056395073, 'rank': 0.39513550558296734, 'sign': 0.6390411764705882, 'scope': 'inner-fold models on same untouched outer test rows; not independent host/cohort replication', 'magnitude_nmse': 1.0137787802253557}. Inner fitted models share training samples and common test rows; not independent cohort replication.

11. **LightGBM/HGB:**{'correlation': 0.9487405734688061, 'sign': 0.9009299344066786, 'magnitude_nmse': 0.1075475224022901, 'magnitude_ratio': 0.9375523522531318, 'source_labels': {'SHARED_STRONG_MODEL_GAP': 17, 'MODEL_SPECIFIC_GAP': 7}, 'criteria': 'supplement_policy.json'}. Both deltas share the same Base subtraction, which can increase correlation; this is not independent error confirmation. MODEL_SPECIFIC_GAP includes insufficient/ambiguous agreement, not proof that the teacher is uniquely correct. All split metrics retained.

12. **Additive recovery:**main: primary recovery 5.021% / target RMSE gain 0.023% / delta R² 0.0294; clean 1.177% / -3.650% / 0.0084. EBM_additive: primary recovery 8.922% / target RMSE gain 0.099% / delta R² 0.0863; clean 2.856% / -0.447% / 0.0621.

13. **Additive target utility:**reported separately from delta predictability in the preceding line and panel_summary.csv. A high correlation alone is not task improvement.

14. **Pair recovery:**main_pair: primary recovery -0.820% / target RMSE gain -1.650% / delta R² 0.0541; clean -0.820% / -1.728% / 0.0541.

15. **Pair target utility:**separate RMSE gain above. The bounded seeded pair bank is not exhaustive; negative results cannot rule out all pair structure.

16. **TT:**TT: primary recovery 0.187% / target RMSE gain -0.681% / delta R² -0.0182; clean -2.668% / -4.138% / -0.0696. Cumulative main_pair_TT: primary recovery -1.079% / target RMSE gain -1.670% / delta R² 0.0525; clean -1.079% / -1.735% / 0.0525. The latter is an order-dependent residual fit, not an orthogonal high-order component.

17. **Context:**context: primary recovery 3.192% / target RMSE gain -0.563% / delta R² 0.0316; clean -7.488% / -2.549% / -0.0051.

18. **Oblique:**oblique: primary recovery 5.788% / target RMSE gain 0.019% / delta R² 0.0105; clean 2.478% / -3.760% / -0.0745.

19. **Local/prototype:**local: primary recovery 0.545% / target RMSE gain -0.019% / delta R² 0.0061; clean -1.035% / -0.728% / -0.0106.

20. **Threshold/hinge:**threshold: primary recovery -0.256% / target RMSE gain -0.605% / delta R² -0.0042; clean -6.825% / -3.433% / -0.0400. Hinge, step, linear and missing terms are a single frozen combined arm; they were NOT separately fitted, so their individual effects are not identified. No post-result new arm added.

21. **Conjunction:**conjunction: primary recovery 1.581% / target RMSE gain -0.113% / delta R² 0.0062; clean 0.168% / -2.204% / -0.0142.

22. **Missingness:**missingness: primary recovery -0.004% / target RMSE gain -0.005% / delta R² -0.0003; clean 0.002% / -0.130% / -0.0015.

23. **Categorical:**categorical: primary recovery -0.003% / target RMSE gain -0.004% / delta R² -0.0006; clean 0.044% / -0.004% / -0.0014. categorical_numeric: primary recovery -0.043% / target RMSE gain -0.135% / delta R² -0.0019; clean -1.392% / -0.541% / -0.0114. The additional HIGH128 arm is included in the full CSV.

24. **Teacher vs raw residual:**panel_target_comparison.csv provides every source/family/capacity. training_noise_summary.csv summarizes paired differences without selecting the winning target per source.

25. **Training-noise evidence:**teacher-minus-raw MEDIUM task gain is+1.015pp for main,+4.019pp for pair,+0.104pp for local,+0.365pp for conjunction; teacher fitting is better on20/24,20/24,20/24,19/24 sources respectively. EBM is an exception (-0.270pp,7/24). The paired table includes clean sensitivity. Improvement over a noisy raw-residual fit does not imply improvement over Base. Context/TT/oblique geometry is primary-target supervised and reused, hence teacher advantage there is confounded by representation selection. EBM has common hyperparameters but separately learned knots/shapes. These are diagnostic associations, not a noise-only causal intervention.

26. **Within-bin variation:**{'median_ratio': 0.9845080797244379, 'caveat': 'Marginal within-bin variance includes other features; not an irreducible encoding bound'}. Conditional variation includes other features and cannot itself establish encoding loss.

27. **Encoding signal:**median across splits per feature/level gives {'LOW_WITHIN_BIN_SIGNAL': 2051, 'STRONG_WITHIN_BIN_SIGNAL': 159, 'MODERATE_WITHIN_BIN_SIGNAL': 101}. Classification uses heldout within-bin slope MSE improvement / delta variance:LOW<=.01,MODERATE<=.10,STRONG>.10. Training-only slope fits; descriptive frozen thresholds. Multiple feature tests and marginal confounding prevent causal attribution.

28. **Collisions:**{'median_tuple_collision_rate': 0.1058796506194354, 'caveat': 'Address variance minus within-tuple variance; descriptive, not independently causal'}. Address/hash collision variance and heterogeneity inside an identical code tuple are separate columns in collisions.csv; the latter is not a hash collision.

29. **Locality:**source-median neighbor delta correlation 0.342131 vs shuffled -0.004053. Ordinal categorical code distance and<=2000 test rows limit interpretation; not a causal nearest-neighbor oracle.

30. **Delta sparsity:**top1/5/10/25% absolute correction:net MSE-gap share,positive-gap share,and delta-energy share below. Net shares can exceed100% or be negative; do not clip.

31. **Feature concentration:**source-median top1/top5 permutation sensitivity shares 0.299450/0.816477. This is dependence of teacher-minus-Base predictions on input coordinates, not additive attribution.

32. **Regions:**regions.csv and regions_extended.csv cover target extremes,numeric tails,missing,rare categories,sparse codes,all training-decile intervals and high-cardinality common/rare/unseen/missing states. Regions overlap; no summation or causal interpretation.

33. **Taxonomy:**dataset_taxonomy.csv plus clean-panel copy. Multilabel,LOW/MEDIUM confidence;no HIGH with this historical overlapping-split design. MODEL_SPECIFIC is appended by the fixed teacher agreement rule. No ENCODING_RESOLUTION cause assigned solely from conditional variation.

34. **Dominant,primary:**MIXED_NO_DOMINANT_MECHANISM. Frequency requires>=3/5 split support and>=60% gap-source coverage; superiority requires10pp paired recovery advantage and bootstrapCI>0 versus all other families.

35. **Dominant,clean:**MIXED_NO_DOMINANT_MECHANISM. Same rules on all16 uncontaminated-by-known-fingerprint sources; no favorable subset or outcome-dependent exclusions.

36. **Strongest falsified hypothesis:**A single universal dominant mechanism is not established: no family clears the frozen cross-source dominance gate in both panels. This rejects promotion on that criterion, not every possible representation of that family.

37. **Strongest surviving hypothesis:**Source-dependent, partly teacher-specific correction structure survives. A bounded probe can predict correction without yielding comparable true-target gain; family, capacity and target-noise effects must remain separate.

38. **R51:**INCOMPLETE_WITH_EXPLICIT_REMAINDER. Regression OOF fine-resolution additive students supply new evidence on that specific absent diagnostic; Adult/classification,extrapolation,tree-pair extraction,controlled missing/category interventions,matched-support encoding causality and full deployment cost remain unresolved. No retroactive completion.

39. **Environment:**ENVIRONMENT_TRANSIENT_NATIVE_LOAD_FAILURE; Windows EBM native loader WinError4551 in camera_prices_64001 additive teacher solve, retry succeeded and sealed checkpoint reused. No security-policy,algorithm or teacher change. Failed attempt not counted completed;environment_incident.json retains evidence and attribution scope.

40. **Round70 verdict:**MIXED_NO_DOMINANT_MECHANISM — same qualitative verdict on clean sensitivity. Diagnostic completion with disclosed implementation bounds, not confirmation or architecture promotion.

41. **Round71 architecture justified:**NO. An independent diagnostic replication can be proposed, but no universal-mechanism architecture is justified.

42. **Exact proposal (NOT STARTED):**No architecture Round71 is justified. If further diagnostics are separately authorized, first preregister an equal-training-size delta-transfer falsifier on the same24 sources with the same two panels. Keep Base/LightGBM/HGB and the mainMEDIUM/main_pairMEDIUM/EBM-additive probes fixed. Compare current OOF-to-full-size evaluation with an equal-size teacher/Base evaluation, and one fixed three-repeat cross-fit average, using only training rows for all fitted targets and untouched heldout rows for scoring. Measure delta correlation, magnitude shift, delta R² and target RMSE utility separately. Falsify the cross-fit training-size/variance explanation if matched-size or averaged deltas do not improve source-median delta R² by at least0.10 and target RMSE gain by at least2pp with source-bootstrap CI>0 in both panels. No new architecture, teacher/probe tuning or routing; stop if the falsifier fails. This is a proposal, not an approved or started Round71.

## Additional limitations

OOF targets come from models trained on roughly two-thirds of the outer training set; heldout teacher deltas come from full-training fits. That train-size and resampling shift may limit transfer and was not isolated here. Low probe recovery does not prove that the full-size teacher function lacks a compact representation. Only the MEDIUM main shuffled-delta control was implemented, not every principal family suggested by the initial prose. It does not establish permutation significance for all families. Feature intervals are exhaustive descriptive slices, not heldout-selected rules. Probe pickle size excludes shared encoder/basis storage; fit seconds may exclude geometry discovery and were measured under concurrent workers. They are not end-to-end deployable model memory or isolated hardware benchmarks. Delta MSE has source-specific target units; normalized R² is the cross-source predictability summary. The cumulative mainLOW16→pairMEDIUM16→pair+TT uses matching main grids; mainMEDIUM64 must not be substituted as the additive increment baseline. Group repair is a correctness amendment; no capacity/teacher/probe was tuned after results.
