Main16 → main16+pair16 → same pair + rank4 TT. Order-dependent finite regression projection; not orthogonal ANOVA. Standalone TT spans only four selected features and is not an exhaustive higher-order oracle.

## Integrity and limits

All targets originate from internal out-of-fold Base/LightGBM predictions. Heldout labels are used only for diagnosis and reporting. Existing prepared categorical vocabularies are outer-training label-free mappings, while inner estimators fit states from their own data. Random row splits do not establish temporal/domain transfer.

Fit seconds are operational timings with concurrent independent diagnostic processes, not a hardware-isolated performance benchmark. The probe capacity ladder intentionally changes support and effective regularization; no isolated resolution causal claim. The categorical reference is bounded by each code vocabulary.

Checkpoint SHA256, OOF row exclusion and saved-prediction metric replay are checked by verify.py. Public core hashes and research privacy are checked by audit.py. Test outcomes are recorded separately in test_results.json.

Round51 remains historically incomplete. Round71 has not started. Last completed research round:70.


# Round 71 — completed teacher-delta reliability

# CODADAPT ROUND 71 — Teacher-delta reliability

**MIXED_MEASUREMENT_LIMIT**. Reliability and/or size effects are material, but the joint probe gate does not identify a recovered universal useful structure. Round70 mixed conclusion survives; its interpretation as an absence of structure remains measurement-limited.

Consensus improves all five families relative to single-OOF in paired source comparisons, but this is not equivalent to reliably beating Base. On CLEAN_ALL, main/pair pass their individual improvement gate; the overall gate fails (2/5 rather than3/5). On CLEAN_MATERIAL_GAP no family passes the joint gate. In particular pair consensus has positive median task gain but its source-bootstrap95% CI includes zero on both primary panels. Shared-teacher targets do not establish a cross-dataset useful mechanism either. Thus Round70 MIXED_NO_DOMINANT_MECHANISM is CONFIRMED with a material measurement-noise qualification, not evidence of an absolute absence of structure.

1. **Stage1:**SGEMM, california, melbourne_airbnb, million_song_year, diabetes, RAM_price, bodyfat, ASP-POTASSCO-regression; metadata-only selection, all five external splits.

2. **Stage2:**True; frozen Stage1 gate={'extend': True, 'stability_gate': True, 'size_gate': True, 'median_half_improvement': 0.1510550606352496, 'positive_sources': 8, 'size_correlation': 0.6962727261563546, 'size_nmse': 0.6931885740970929, 'sources': 8}. No dataset substitution.

3. **Repeats:**480 across120 contexts; clean sources get5, overlap sources2 under the passed extension rule.

4. **Reuse:**all valid Stage1 checkpoints retained. Round70 supplies720 original inner Base/LightGBM checkpoint references and360 full Base/LightGBM/HGB references; single/raw probe results are reused, never refitted. These are distinct roles, not repeated fit counts.

5. **Fit accounting:**7,200 new Base/LightGBM/HGB fits plus1,200 consensus/shared probe-value fits;1,200 historical single/raw probe arms reused. Five missing historical local geometries reconstructed deterministically and checked bitwise against original predictions, without predictive refit. Final audit verifies counts; test-only fits are not experimental fits.

6. **Base OOF reliability:**0.812443 source-median Pearson on CLEAN_ALL.

7. **LightGBM OOF reliability:**0.916237.

8. **Delta OOF reliability:**0.582031; Spearman 0.441824,sign 0.688768.

9. **Single vs consensus:**single pair correlation 0.582031; disjoint2-repeat vs2-repeat means 0.736855. This is not an independent5vs5 validation. Assumption-dependent single R² ceiling 0.575940,correlation ceiling 0.757304; no corrected performance claims.

10. **Convergence:**consensus_convergence.csv reports1..5 vs final mean; self-inclusion makes the final endpoint exactly perfect by construction. It is convergence, not independent reliability.

11. **Model-fit variance:**same full rows,changed seed:delta normalized disagreement 0.362467. Frozen LightGBM may be seed-invariant under its default no-subsampling configuration.

12. **Sample composition:**different exact-cardinality2/3 subsets withfixed seed:delta normalized disagreement 0.893375. Subsets overlap; they are not independent cohorts.

13. **Training size:**50%,2/3,80%,100% curves use identical external test rows; Base/teacher utility and correction scale reported separately in train_size_results.csv.

14. **2/3 vs100% delta:**correlation 0.659164; NMSE 0.811428. Size and finite-sample composition are not an orthogonal ANOVA decomposition.

15. **Full-test stability:**MODEL_FIT_SEED rows in variance_contrasts.csv compare the same test,full training rows,and three seeds. This differs from repeated OOF fold assignment.

16–17. **Single/consensus→full:**probe_summary.csv and paired comparisons below separate delta R²/correlation from task RMSE. The matched-size reference is the mean prediction of the inner-fit models on the same test, an ensemble, not a single full-data model.

18. **Shared LGB/HGB:**compatibility gate True; raw arithmetic mean only, no recalibration. Shared and model-specific repeat stability in shared_components.csv. Both deltas share the Base offset.

19–23. **Five fixed families:**mainMEDIUM64,pairMEDIUM16/128 groups,oblique frozen Round70 geometry,threshold/hinge8 knots,local64 prototypes. Tables below show single vs consensus; no capacity or discovery change.

24. **Target utility:**positive RMSE gain means improvement over the same full-training Base. It is not interchangeable with teacher-delta predictability or recovery fraction.

25. **Raw vs single vs consensus vs shared:**all available arms remain in probe_results.csv; no per-source target choice. The oblique mapping was selected on single OOF, which limits fairness to a common fixed representation, not rediscovered optimal geometry.

26. **Sparse corrections:**stability_sparsity.csv reports Jaccard overlap at1/5/10/25%;top_vs_full.csv compares inner-test ensembles with full-training delta.

27. **Locality:**same frozen coded-neighbor graph across repeats,delta-neighbor correlation and shuffled control in stability_locality.csv. Categorical integer distances remain a limitation.

28. **Within-bin:**fixed native Base states; replicate correlations of cell means and within-bin slopes in stability_within_bin.csv. State discovery was not repeated. No causal resolution claim.

29. **Collision signal:**address correction reproducibility in stability_collision.csv;collision_specific.csv isolates rows in actual colliding addresses atlevel0. Within-tuple heterogeneity is not a hash collision and stable address values alone do not establish COLLISION_LIMIT.

30. **CLEAN_ALL:**16 fixed sources,5 external splits each; primary inference. All sources retained regardless of teacher advantage.

31. **CLEAN_MATERIAL_GAP:**ASP-POTASSCO-regression, SGEMM, bodyfat, california, camera_prices, fps-in-video-games, million_song_year, mtp, rossmann_sales. Frozen threshold:Round70 source-mean teacher RMSE gain>=5%; this subset does not replace CLEAN_ALL.

32. **Round70 verdict:**CONFIRMED. Historical artifacts remain unchanged; this is a known-panel diagnostic reassessment, not independent confirmation.

33. **Verdict:**MIXED_MEASUREMENT_LIMIT

34. **Falsified interpretation:**a single noisy measurement cannot automatically be treated as a stable ground-truth correction. Conversely, a reliability boost alone does not establish a useful compact representation; both claims require their separate observed gates.

35. **Surviving explanation:**training-sample/fold variation and size mismatch affect the measured correction, while usable structure can remain heterogeneous. These diagnostics do not establish that no compact architecture could ever represent it.

36. **Architectural Round72 justified:**NO from this known-panel diagnosis alone.

37. **Exact proposed next step (not started):**NOT_JUSTIFIED: no Round72 proposal or architecture is introduced. Reliability alone does not establish reproducible recovery of useful target signal.

## Methods and limitations

Audit repair: probe model.pkl and prediction.npz initially shared a stem.sha256 sidecar, so prediction saving replaced the model sidecar. Original completion manifests independently sealed both files and remained valid. All1,200 saved probe models were subsequently replayed against their saved test predictions, and unambiguous .pkl.sha256 receipts were added without a fit or result change. No-memory-level Base cases have no collision/within-bin diagnostic and are explicitly listed as not applicable, not zero.

Repeat assignment and training-sample composition are related interventions, not independent additive variance components. Fold-slot seed changes follow the fixed original policy. Noise ceilings assume independent additive measurement errors; overlapping training samples violate this ideal and estimates are interpretive only. Comparisons across datasets use source aggregation, not pooled rows. Secondary external-overlap sources remain labelled and cannot establish duplicate-independent generalization. Feature permutation diagnostics use all features on the same first128 heldout rows,fold0 per repeat and full models; they are descriptive sensitivity, not causal feature effects or all-fold importance. Model-specific residuals from an arithmetic mean are opposites and not orthogonal sources. No new architecture,no public changes,no commit/push/tag/release. Round51 remains incomplete;Round72 not started.

Last completed round:71. Round51 remains incomplete. Round72 not started.


# Round 72 — completed composition diagnosis

# CODADAPT ROUND 72 — Cross-fitted multi-mechanism complementarity

**HIGH_ORACLE_LOW_GENERALIZATION**. Known-panel diagnosis; no public model change and no Round73 execution.

There is partial positive evidence, not a null result: nonnegative composition improves CLEAN_ALL RMSE by3.0563% (95% CI0.2192–5.7528%). Ridge gains2.8726% (CI−0.1543–6.1471%) and global LODO2.0171% (CI−0.2872–3.0254%). On MATERIAL_GAP ridge gains6.1471% and LODO3.5828%, both positive CIs, but ridge improves the validation-selected single by only0.8240pp rather than the required2pp. CLEAN_ALL ridge loses18.47% on analcatdata_birthday (worst split52.21% loss). Oracle linear gain6.9233% (CI4.3184–11.6366%) shows a higher test-fitted ceiling, not a deployable result. The joint registered gate fails in both primary panels. Do not interpret this as proof that all mechanisms are redundant or useless.

1. **Reuse:**600 R71 final consensus probe checkpoints, their bitwise-verified test outputs,120 consensus target files and historical Base/teacher predictions. R70 train probe outputs were in-sample and could not serve as OOF combiner features.

2. **New work:**2,880 inner Base/LGB fits;1,800 probe value solves;360 local and360 oblique mapping jobs. Meta-training:14,400 tiny candidate weight solves (including uniform evaluations),147 global LODO solves,120 explicitly oracle test-target solves. Prediction/weight replay is verification, not another experimental fit. Completed counts checked by final audit. Timings of cached teacher/probe solves are descriptive concurrent-run times, not isolated throughput benchmarks.

3. **CLEAN_ALL:**ASP-POTASSCO-regression, ESL, RAM_price, SGEMM, analcatdata_birthday, bodyfat, california, camera_prices, diabetes, fps-in-video-games, jc_penney_products, melbourne_airbnb, million_song_year, mtp, rossmann_sales, water-treatment; five existing outer splits each.

4. **CLEAN_MATERIAL_GAP:**ASP-POTASSCO-regression, SGEMM, bodyfat, california, camera_prices, fps-in-video-games, million_song_year, mtp, rossmann_sales. Threshold inherited unchanged from R71.

5. **Probe correlation:**table below reports OOF Pearson/Spearman/sign, residual correlation and top10% overlap. Correlated target residuals can reflect uncaptured target variation; they are not direct proof of redundant outputs.

6–10. **Unique additive/pair/oblique/threshold/local information:**train-only affine residualization against the internally selected single probe; heldout unique correlations and incremental residual MSE in unique_signal.csv and table below. This diagnostic is not deployed.

17–20. **Teacher/shared recovery, task utility and full transfer:**panel_summary.csv keeps full-LGB delta R²/correlation distinct from matched-size consensus and full-shared references. Task prediction always equals native Base plus correction. Each combiner target is a separate experiment; no per-dataset target choice.

21. **Leave-family-out:**positive removal_loss means the family helped the complete ridge combiner. All five removals and three requested pairings are reported, without post-test architecture selection.

22. **Weight stability:**sign, absolute-weight rank, magnitude drift and active fraction across existing outer splits. Prediction stability aligns canonical original source row IDs, checks matching raw features and compares only common heldout rows. It never correlates unrelated rows.

23. **Capacity:**five identical pair copies are a rank-one control with five coefficients. No independent five-bank capacity-matched OOF artifacts existed; this control does NOT exclude every extra-capacity explanation. No claim of an established architecture-level mechanism follows from it.

24. **Shuffle:**independent per-column permutations in training/validation/test preserve marginal distributions and destroy alignment. Fixed seeds, no favorable permutation selection.

25. **Best-family oracle:**source-mean test selection, explicitly non-deployable; separate from validation-selected single.

26. **Linear oracle:**five weights fitted to test residual itself; descriptive representation ceiling on these outputs, biased high and never used as training labels or deployed weights.

27. **Downside:**every summary includes worst split/source and source/split losses greater than1%,2%,5%,10%. Positive median alone is insufficient.

28. **Gap recovery:**fraction of MSE gap Base→LGB recovered, unclipped and undefined when teacher MSE improvement≤1%. RMSE percentage is a different metric.

29. **Taxonomy:**fixed diagnostic thresholds in analyze.py; known sources and overlapping training splits do not establish prospective causal classes.

30. **Verdict:**HIGH_ORACLE_LOW_GENERALIZATION

31. **Strongest falsified claim:**The frozen composition yields a broadly stable, transferable improvement meeting the joint task/LODO/downside criteria. It does not; this does not falsify all useful complementarity.

32. **Strongest surviving explanation:**Partial useful composition and shrinkage/calibration coexist with source-dependent generalization failures; oracle ceilings exceed honest fits. Extra independent representation capacity and nested target-size shift remain unresolved.

33. **Round73 justified:**NO under the complete registered criteria.

34. **Round73 proposal:**NONE. No meta-CodAdapt or new architecture started.

## Protocol limits

Identity audit correction: fitted categorical vocabularies can assign different codes across splits and collapse unseen categories to NaN. Prepared-row fingerprints therefore cannot identify rows across splits or prove raw-group overlap. All120 contexts were reconstructed exactly from historical source caches (including original sampling IDs), confirming the original group plan and zero raw/group overlaps on CLEAN_ALL. Eight clean contexts have prepared-code collisions. SDSS original CSV is unavailable; its sealed full numeric frame (no categorical conversion) was checked against all five original split frames and targets to verify row order. The initial cross-split prediction-stability CSV was preserved as INVALID_FOR_CROSS_SPLIT_ALIGNMENT and replaced using verified canonical row IDs. No fit, weights, predictions, task metrics, oracle ceiling or frozen gate changed. Historical Round70/71 artifacts remain immutable; their prepared fingerprints must not be reused as canonical cross-split IDs. See identity_audit.json/csv and identity_correction.json.

Manual-resume resource policy: one heavy process at a time, one BLAS/LightGBM/NumExpr thread. Remaining analytical stages streamed contexts and peaked at approximately159MB child RSS; the replay process was observed around330MB, not claimed as its measured lifetime peak. All120 OOF inputs and120 combinations were already complete and reused. A verifier-only C/F layout mismatch at a saturated constrained optimum changed a near-exact penalty tie; restoring the original evaluator layout made strict weight replay pass. No saved fit, weight, prediction or frozen decision was changed. See resume_audit.csv, resource_policy.json, resource_usage.log and replay_layout_incident.json.

No historical valid fit repeated. New OOF targets exclude heldout rows transitively through teacher training and supervised geometry. Inner two-repeat/twofold target estimation differs from the larger R71 final target consensus: size/variance distribution shift remains an interpretation limit. Penalties use the pre-existing xs[1] nested holdout, not an invalid meta-cross-validation over mutually dependent OOF inputs. Training OOF matrix is for fitting weights, not claimed as heldout combiner performance. Secondary TT/context/categorical/missingness remain descriptive/oracle only because their stored training outputs are not OOF. Eight external-overlap sources are secondary; clean/material panels alone drive primary inference. Round51 remains historically incomplete.

# Round 73 — completed combination generalization diagnosis

# CODADAPT ROUND 73 — Oracle-to-deployable gap

**MIXED. No architecture promotion; Round74 not started.** All120 frozen contexts completed with zero new Base/teacher/probe fits. Known-panel diagnosis, not independent confirmation.

The library has useful small compositions, but the~6.92% oracle headline is not a stable attainable target established by these data. Validation-selected2/3-family corrections gain3.0545%/3.1218% with positive source-bootstrap CIs and worst-source losses only0.2517%/0.3529%. Their source-excluded global variants gain2.4344%/2.4394%, with clean CIs crossing zero. All four preregistered small-composition gates fail.

The difference of aggregate medians is4.0507pp; the actual paired-source median oracle-minus-ridge gap is1.4634pp. Subtracting medians must not be presented as the median paired deficit. Seven clean sources with fewer than1,000 weight-training rows have median gap8.4016pp; nine larger sources have0.7016pp.

Raw MSE retains each dataset target units, so cross-dataset comparisons use unitless harm shares/gains, not magnitudes of these raw MSE medians. An oracle can still harm individual rows despite improving total test MSE. Its all-five unregularized span includes zero correction, so nonnegative aggregate gain is guaranteed by test fitting and cannot establish safety.

STABLE_COMPLEMENTARITY is a descriptive threshold label, not independent confirmation or a causal dataset class. RAM_price and birthday achieve most of their extreme test oracle gains with one calibrated family; their enormous deficits are not evidence that five-way interactions are needed.

34. **Verdict: MIXED.** Small-subset complementary signal exists; high oracle/low deployability remains under the frozen generality gates. Local coefficients vary by dataset (ridge between/within variance3.51), while oracle coefficients vary even more within splits (ratio0.34). Scaling, larger validation and repeat averaging are not sufficient explanations or remedies. There is no uniquely identified causal factor.

35. **Strongest falsified hypothesis:**The oracle gap is mostly a trivial scale mismatch or insufficient penalty-validation rows; normalization worsens paired gain, validation-size effects and repeat consensus are small.

36. **Strongest surviving hypothesis:**Small compositions contain useful signal and control downside, but source/split-dependent calibration, probe/target distribution shift and optimistic small-test oracle fitting limit universal transfer. This frozen diagnosis cannot identify a unique causal contribution.

37–38. **Round74 justified: NO. Proposed architecture/experiment: NONE under this gate.** NONE: do not integrate five modules or promote a universal small combiner. Preserve the useful2/3-family diagnostic; reopening needs new independent evidence that resolves the stated OOF/full-size mismatch and oracle optimism, not another post-hoc weight sweep.

# Round 74 — completed small-composition diagnosis

# CODADAPT ROUND 74 — small-composition identity and oracle-to-deployable gap

Verdict: **HIGH_ORACLE_LOW_DEPLOYABILITY_CONFIRMED** for the tested frozen library.
Causal diagnosis: **MIXED**, with substantially more diagnostic headroom in weight
estimation than in subset selection alone. No stable general pair/triple passes the
preregistered transfer, identity/function and downside gate. The current multi-mechanism
promotion line is closed. **Round75 justified: NO; Round75 not started.** This is not
a proof that every possible small composition is useless: the local validation pair
retains positive known-panel utility and passes the restricted cross-split transfer
test. It does not satisfy the global identity/downside requirement.

## Scope, isolation, reuse and resources

24 frozen sources ×5 splits; CLEAN_ALL16 is primary, material-gap9 is sensitivity,
8 historically overlapping sources are secondary. All three targets are retained:
teacher-consensus reconstruction, shared structure, and real target residual. The
tables below use real target RMSE unless explicitly labelled. Reconstruction R²,
full-test teacher-delta transfer R²/correlation and target RMSE are separate columns
in panel_summary.csv; none substitutes for another. No new confirmation, dataset,
representation, family, Base/teacher/probe fit or public change.

R72 private15307 and R73 manifest64214 entries verified before new arithmetic.
120 assembled/canonical contexts reused, all prior subset and global coefficients
reused. **0 Base fits, 0 teacher fits, 0 probe fits; 15204 tiny OLS
solves**, exclusively for new diagnostic donor/mask oracles. Independent replay
repeated their arithmetic for audit, not a new experiment. 720 context/direction/
LOSO checkpoints plus individual oracle jobs have deterministic keys and SHA256.
Runtime 42.20s; watchdog 163.7 MiB peak;
one numerical process/thread. Public test fixtures are not research fits.

Source means over five splits precede source medians; CI95% is a2000-replicate
source bootstrap, seed7474. Directed comparisons average donors within recipient,
then recipients within source. CI describes this known source panel, not an
independent prospective claim. Source counts/coverage vary on purged transports.

Exact per-split MSE identity: subset=A−B; weights=A−C; interaction=B+C−A−D;
total=A−D. Fractions are computed only for positive total; source means then median.
The displayed medians need not sum to1. Contributions can be negative. This is an
algebraic factorial accounting, **not causal identification**. Paired source gain
differences are not the difference between the aggregate A/B/C/D medians.
Subset-only paired gain is effectively zero; oracle weights add0.729/1.173pp.
This strongly weakens the hypothesis that merely choosing the right pair resolves
the gap. Test-OLS optimism means weight headroom is not an attainable performance
estimate. In particular WRONG_WEIGHT on a negative split is partly tautological:
unconstrained test OLS can always choose zero correction; it is not causal proof.

## 33–37. Decision, falsification and next step

| rule | size | quality | transport | coverage | identity | functional | stable_small | function_stable |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| frequency | 2 | False | True | True | False | False | False | False |
| frequency | 3 | False | False | True | False | False | False | False |
| validation_utility | 2 | False | True | True | False | False | False | False |
| validation_utility | 3 | False | False | True | False | False | False | False |

Strongest falsified hypothesis: a single general2–3-family identity, or harmless
label switching between equivalent corrections, explains the high oracle ceiling
and yields controlled deployable gains under the current frozen protocol.

Strongest surviving hypothesis: coefficient/sample instability and changed probe
realizations matter more than subset selection alone; some within-source pair
utility transfers, but oracle weight fitting exaggerates usable generality. The
relative causal roles of OOF/full drift and optimism remain unresolved.

Operational verdict **HIGH_ORACLE_LOW_DEPLOYABILITY_CONFIRMED**; causal label MIXED.
Close current frozen multi-mechanism promotion line, preserve all artifacts, no new
representation or default. **Round75 justified: NO. Proposed Round75: none under
the present gate.** Reopening requires new falsifiable evidence satisfying stable
identity or correction equivalence, independent source/row-group transfer and
bounded downside, not another sweep over this test panel. A truly disjoint prospective
design would address overlap but is not authorized/executed here and does not itself
satisfy the present promotion gate.

Audit: 11352 metric records and 15204 small solves replayed;
120 contexts and480 directions verified, no deployable test selection, correct
canonical/group masks, explicit oracle labels. Public/private checks and memory
continuity are recorded in final_audit.json. Public source hashes and R72/R73 private
manifests unchanged; persistent memory advances to74, historical51 remains explicitly
incomplete. No commit/push/tag/release, no Round75.


Last completed round:74. Indexed history1–74; Round51 remains incomplete. Current frozen multi-mechanism promotion line closed. Round75 not justified and not started.

## POST-R74 OPEN RESEARCH SCOUTING — 2026-09-30 (not Round75)

Eight hypotheses and 22 prior-art family entries reviewed; one conditional internal estimation contrast shortlisted: shared-emission residual posterior circuit, a known probabilistic-circuit construction, not a novelty claim. Historical R17/R18/R22 source gaps remain. Closed lines were not automatically reopened.

Frozen known-real panel: 8 regression sources x 3 archived splits. 168 new model fits/solves plus 24 encoders, zero Base/LightGBM refits. Median of source-mean RMSE gains: -0.012230% vs Base, +0.449826% vs bounded pair. Only 3/8 sources and 9/24 splits improve; worst RAM split -18.488%; Million Song mean -9.969%. Pair budget is numeric-value bounded, not exact byte matching; RAM has no actual pair.

Pooling improves aggregate conditional half-fit stability but not useful target prediction. Incremental fit median0.366s; historical-only total fit diagnostic2.560x on18 cases, not a matched timing. Inference3.616x, deep1.105x, serialized1.144x Base; process peak0.230GiB. No five-split extension or optimizer/capacity rescue.

Verdict: **NO_DISTINCT_SIGNAL_FOUND; NO ROUND75 JUSTIFIED YET**. This falsifies the finite recipe, not all circuits/compact learners. Strategic diagnosis remains MIXED. Required new evidence: a distinct train-identifiable structure connecting stable shared probabilities to useful residual moments, not another contextual/TT or weight-tuning variant.

Replay24 cases/360 prediction vectors, no fit reruns. Ruff public/private PASS. Tests374 passed/4 expected xfail;4 packaging checks ENVIRONMENT_FAILURE (PermissionError reading existing dist archives), confirmed by one read retry. Core and public docs unchanged; no remote/commit/tag/publication. Detailed evidence: `research_private/POST_R74_NEW_HYPOTHESIS_SCOUTING.md`, `research_private/post_r74_scouting/audit.json`.

`last_completed_round = 74`; Round75 neither proposed nor started. Original round index1-74 and compiler evidence remain unchanged.

## POST-R74 MASSIVE COMPOSITIONAL SEARCH — 2026-09-30 (not Round75)

The frozen conditional-composition search enumerated 30,970 canonical
descriptions from 8,663,490 theoretical combinations. Historical and cost
screens left 5,526 and 3,380 respectively. S1 completed 1,200 recipes ×2
cases; 290 passed its fixed gate and a diverse beam carried 200 to S2. S2
completed 200 recipes ×8 cases on four real sources. **No recipe passed the
fixed S2 gate**: all had a split worse than −5% RMSE versus frozen Base, with
water-treatment systematic downside. The best split-median recipe gained
+4.904% median but lost −5.955% mean, worst −70.024%; 5/8 splits and 3/4
sources improved. This is development evidence only. A late, explicitly
non-promotable random-control diagnostic also had 0/50 S2 survivors.

The manual interruption was during that random diagnostic, not the main
search. Resume audit found 2,400 S1 and 1,600 S2 valid rows, 314 valid
random-control final checkpoints and 86 not-started evaluations. Only the
missing diagnostic work was run, in one process/one numerical thread with
per-row durable output, one dataset resident at a time, 8/12 GiB warning/stop.
It completed 400/400 rows in 85.21 s; sampled RSS reached about 1.22 GiB.
The probable prior slowdown was sustained CPU/storage work on S2 Million Song
and thousands of small checkpoints, not a demonstrated RAM/pagefile violation.

**Verdict COMPOSITIONAL_SEARCH_NO_SIGNAL** for the frozen finite grammar and
panel; S3 and SEARCH_HOLDOUT remained unexecuted and sealed under the
preregistered early-stop rule. No final runtime, inference/memory benchmark,
or independent confirmation exists. Round75 is not justified and was not
started. Reopen only for a distinct falsifiable mechanism controlling
source-level downside, with independent prospective evidence, not another
sweep or relaxed gate. See
`research_private/POST_R74_MASSIVE_COMPOSITIONAL_SEARCH.md` and
`research_private/post_r74_massive_search/final_audit.json`. Public core and
round index 1–74 remain unchanged; no remote/commit/tag/release action.
Ruff and five private tests pass; public tests excluding packaging report 371
passed and four expected xfails. The full public run has four packaging
ENVIRONMENT_FAILURE cases because the existing `dist/` wheel cannot be read
(`PermissionError [Errno 13]`, reproduced by direct read). This is not a
candidate-algorithm failure; the frozen package tests were not changed.


## POST-R74 TARGET-AWARE TEACHER DISTILLATION SCOUTING — 2026-09-30 (not Round75)

The frozen strong-teacher compression Stage A selected C3 LARGE_DIAGNOSTIC and retained 48.95% of LightGBM's advantage over Base on five eligible sources; an existing-checkpoint test oracle reached only 67.01%, below the 75% gate. The subsequent bottleneck diagnosis found a dataset-specific mixed failure and error concentrated in teacher-useful regions. This motivated a distinct **objective-only** test: fit the same C3 tables/codes/pairs/capacity with bounded row weights from train-only OOF Base-versus-teacher utility. Historical verdict `PARTIAL_OVERLAP`: earlier weighting of residual correction tables is not the same recorded full-teacher compilation objective. No novelty claim.

No teacher or Base was refitted. Archived Round70 OOF predictions supported 15 cases across California, Diamonds, SDSS, Million Song and Diabetes. Adult's three frozen Round60 cases lack compatible OOF predictions; in-sample substitution and new teacher fits were forbidden, so Adult weighted results are unavailable. The 75 new weighted compiler solves cover W1 positive utility, W2 smooth utility, W3 rank capped, shuffled-W1 and teacher-imitation-error controls; W0 reuses archived C3. All weighted models preserve representation and stand-alone inference.

On the four sources with meaningful teacher advantage, source-balanced matched test retention was W0 41.03%, W1 9.13%, W2 36.52%, W3 35.65%; all three target-aware recipes lost on 4/4 sources and 12/12 splits. W0's five-source original 48.95% is retained as a separate historical figure, not compared unpaired with the four-source weights. Diabetes improved relative to W0 but still lost badly to Base; shuffled weights improved Diabetes similarly. Teacher-error weighting reached 42.85% matched retention and 2/4 source wins, insufficient for the preregistered +10pp and majority gates. Objective weighting sometimes reduced error on teacher-useful rows without improving target retention. Verdict **TARGET_AWARE_OBJECTIVE_NO_SIGNAL** for the evaluable panel; missing Adult limits completeness but cannot rescue the required source-majority gate. No Round75 proposal.

Audit: 15 OOF proofs, 75 sealed weighted checkpoints, 54/54 exact W0 historical metric replays, 75/75 stand-alone loads with teacher/public-core imports blocked, Ruff PASS, public tests excluding inherited packaging permission failure 371 passed/4 xfailed, two private tests passed. Peak fit RSS was not sampled; `tracemalloc` is a Python-allocation lower bound. Public core unchanged; no remote actions/commit/push/tag/release. See `research_private/POST_R74_TARGET_AWARE_DISTILLATION.md` and `research_private/post_r74_target_aware_distillation/final_audit.json`. `last_completed_round = 74`; Round75 not started. Reopen only with a distinct preregistered train-identifiable objective and independent OOF evidence, not another weight sweep on this panel.

## POST-R74 COMPOSITIONAL FAILURE MINING — 2026-09-30 (not Round75)

Read-only mining reused all 30,970 S0 descriptions and 4,500 S1/S2 result rows,
including the late random controls. Zero new fits, candidate evaluations, S3
or holdout access. The original artifacts remain hashed and unchanged.

Verdict **DATASET_HETEROGENEITY_DOMINANT**. Exact single-choice comparisons
among the 200 main S2 recipes yield 323 architecture pairs plus 68
threshold-insertion comparisons against their own pair-only controls.
Threshold-before-pair has a small conditional order effect: +0.467pp median
across sources in 25 two-stage contrasts, but worst effect -16.397pp. Six
contrasts with a final main stage yield +0.278pp with worst -0.085pp; this is a
narrow, selected context and overlaps historical additive-first evidence,
not a distinct new mechanism. Shrinkage protects water-treatment at the
expense of bodyfat/Million Song. Capacity increases are not uniformly useful.
No historical reopen condition or distinct surviving weak signal is met.

Within the selected 200, S1-to-S2 Spearman is +0.199 including reused cases,
-0.193 on the six added cases and -0.119 on new sources. Shared source+seed
gains replay exactly. The search is better than broad random recipes, but its
fine ranking does not transfer; the seven random S1-gate passers also all fail
S2. Missing S2 outcomes for 1,000 S1 recipes prevent a population false-negative
estimate. Million Song versus water-treatment candidate-rank Spearman is
-0.799. All 200 have HIGH_DOWNSIDE and DATASET_SPECIFIC signatures, not
aggregate NO_GAIN; underfit/overfit cannot be inferred without train-loss logs.

Coverage correction: only three mechanisms were executable, and all four-stage
descriptions were statically rejected. The 250 S2 recipes cover about1.18% of
the analytically cost-admissible21,240 recipes; no universal native-space
exhaustion theorem follows. Code inspection also qualifies earlier prose:
the beam uses three lexicographic lists, not the unused nondominated filter;
the pair-only control matches the pair-stage specification, not total
composition capacity. Do not reinterpret these labels as stronger evidence.

Practical priority remains compact standalone experimental compiler deployment,
without a general latency or predictive-niche claim. A native-quality restart
requires a distinct falsifiable hypothesis before further search.
**NO ROUND75 JUSTIFIED; last_completed_round=74.** See
research_private/POST_R74_COMPOSITIONAL_FAILURE_MINING.md and
research_private/post_r74_massive_search/mining_audit.json.

## Post-R74 — Final strategic checkpoint after order-constraint failure

2026-09-30. Sintesi documentale, NON Round75; **last_completed_round = 74**.
Report: `research_private/POST_R74_FINAL_STRATEGIC_CHECKPOINT.md`.

**COMPILER_DIRECTION_PRIMARY**, con pausa della ricerca architetturale nativa. Nessun nuovo fit, solve, modello, benchmark, architettura o operazione remota. Le condizioni di riapertura e i dati precedenti restano invariati. La diagnosi causale resta eterogenea: non è dimostrata impossibilità universale dei learner compatti.

Evidenza successiva alla sintesi iniziale: target-aware objective NO_SIGNAL (0/4 fonti eleggibili migliorate; matched retention uniform41.03% vs miglioreweighted36.52%); compositional search zero survivor S2; failure mining DATASET_HETEROGENEITY_DOMINANT; regime HETEROGENEITY_NOT_PREDICTABLE_FROM_AVAILABLE_SIGNALS; shared covariance NO_ESTIMATION_SIGNAL; order ORDER_MISMATCH (paired median−1.139395% vs unconstrained). Sono attività finite su dati noti, non nuove confirmation. Manteniamo la correzione della copertura search, i limiti LODO, le strutture no-op, le limitazioni numeriche dell'ordine e i caveat di provenienza.

Cinque direzioni distinte:

1. **Native universal learner: PAUSE.** I piccoli gain Joint R53/R55 sono reali ma non risolvono downside/costo. Nessuna nuova ipotesi insieme distinta e sostenuta da causa identificabile giustifica Round75. D1 ancestry resta concettualmente aperta ma senza evidenza, non automaticamente candidata. Riapertura solo con nuovo principio train-identifiable, controlli matched e falsificazione indipendente.
2. **Native niche learner: nessuna nicchia predittiva Base ex ante validata.** Le vittorie risorse storiche non sono equivalenza di qualità; il selector economico non trasferisce in modo utile nei pannelli multi-family. Serve popolazione/workload definito indipendentemente dagli esiti e confronto quality-matched prospettico, non elenco dei vincitori noti.
3. **Strong nonadditive teacher compiler: estensione generale in pausa.** La recipe congelata conserva48.95% del gain LightGBM, oracle della libreria67.01% sotto75%; target-aware non salva. Successi locali non autorizzano una nuova capacità/loss search o claim CatBoost generale.
4. **EBM compiler/deployment: PRIMARY, EXPERIMENTAL, OPT-IN, NON-DEFAULT.** Fedeltà nel contratto, standalone Windows senza interpret36/36, deep0.194802x e serialized0.236125x EBM. Single-row ratio compiled/EBM2.702544Windows e2.532579WSL: vantaggio storico NON replicato. Due ambienti sullo stesso hardware; probability-bitwise cross-OS failure conservate, contratto atol1e-15 invariato e verdetto LOCAL_MULTI_ENV_VALIDATION_MIXED preservato. Nessuna promozione stable/default. Documentazione pubblica già allineata localmente nell'attività successiva al consolidamento, secondo public_compiler_claim_audit.md; il vecchio 'non applicate' è storico.
5. **Research platform: conservare come infrastruttura interna.** Checkpoint, replay e controlli evitano ripetizioni e overclaim; non è prova di prodotto benchmark indipendente. Costi futuri di manutenzione/validazione separati da nuove ricerche.

Risposte: **A NO** nuova ipotesi nativa pronta; **B NO** nicchia predittiva Base ex ante dimostrata; **C YES** compiler EBM per memoria/standalone/fidelity; **D YES** pausa nativa fino a nuova evidenza. Priorità futura solo condizionata a un incarico separato: validazione di un requisito concreto di deployment del compiler congelato. Nessuna nuova coorte o esperimento avviato qui.

California è già CONSUMED dai micro-test covariance/order: i precedenti messaggi SEARCH_HOLDOUT sealed non la rendono intatta oggi. S3 mai eseguita, altre cinque fonti riservate non consumate da quei task. Gli originali storici mancanti e R51 INCOMPLETE_WITH_EXPLICIT_REMAINDER restano tali. Nessuna modifica alle entry1–74.

Verifiche di questa attività limitate a JSON, coerenza del verdetto, continuità, hash fonti/file pubblici e privacy Git. Nessun pytest o replay di modello: potrebbe violare il divieto di fit/solve. PASS Ruff/test e quattro ENVIRONMENT_FAILURE packaging dei report sono evidenze storiche, non riesecuzioni. Core/compiler/API/default e documentazione pubblica invariati. Nessun commit/push/pull/fetch/tag/release/pubblicazione. STOP senza Round75.

# Post-R74 — Grand exhaustive mechanism discovery

Attività completata il2026-09-30, **non Round75**. Output principale: `research_private/GRAND_EXHAUSTIVE_RESEARCH_REPORT.md`; atlante128 schede, knowledge base e `grand_research/final_audit.json`. Le 31 famiglie storiche e l'indice1–74 sono collegati esplicitamente; R51 rimane incompleta nei termini precedenti.

## Risultato dei cinque cicli

1. Mappa A–N, prior art classica/recente,13 stadi:128 domande canoniche uniche,24 schede di ricette storicamente chiuse,74 riferimenti finali con profondità di accesso dichiarata. Nessun claim di novità o esaustività matematica.
2. Sette controlli algebrici/di assunzione più un controllo della vera nondominanza Pareto: recoding biiettivo, trasformata ortogonale con stesso ridge, RLS senza forgetting e WFA a ordine fisso non costituiscono da soli un bias predittivo distinto.
3. Sei fonti note×3 split, checkpoint e36 half fits riusati. Rescue robusto:0/6 fonti passano la concentrazione di errore TRAIN e danno VALID preregistrata. Ciò non equivale a un fit Huber negativo: Current già migliora varie fonti e il gate è ristretto al rescue del danno.
4. Ancestry main→pair:51 osservazioni tabella×split, non fonti indipendenti; solo SDSS ha controllo support/level/split permutabile, rho0.215686 contro p95shuffle0.290402. Dopo esclusione SDSS44001 con overlap storico:0 fonti identificabili. Conclusione UNIDENTIFIED, non falsificazione di ogni norma gerarchica. Decomposizione TEST archiviata successiva alla decisione:3 split Current peggiori Base,1 con danno anche nel body; nessuna regola scelta sul test.
5. Sei composizioni simboliche censite,0 ammesse,0 full evaluation,0 holdout aperti. Stop al budget5 cicli; nessun nuovo fit o solve reale. Due solve8×8 sintetici sono solo sanity algebrico.

## Decisione e condizioni future

**ROUND75_CANDIDATE_FOUND: NO. COMPILER_DIRECTION_PRIMARY.** Le domande residue richiedono un prior esterno verificato (invarianza/ordine/geometria), rumore/leverage identificato, ancestry con un controllo potente oppure un task cronologico reale. Solver/sketch recenti non costituiscono da soli prova di bias adatto al residuo. R17/18 resta storicamente irrisolta dove mancano i sorgenti. Nessuna ricerca composizionale da riaprire usando semplicemente nomi diversi.

L'export EBM mantiene la priorità per evidenza relativa, non per nuove misure: memoria deep mediana~0.1948× teacher, ma single-row attuale~2.70× EBM Win/~2.53× WSL; WSL è stesso hardware, strict-bitwise probabilistico standalone conserva il verdetto storico MIXED. Non promettere velocità universale. Nessuna promozione di API/default/runtime.

## Verifiche

Ruff pubblico/privato PASS;10 test privati fit-free PASS, inclusa ricostruzione degli indici/predizioni archiviati. Hash46 file pubblici invariati; sorgenti e checkpoint originali intatti; JSON valido; round records1–74 invariati. Test pubblici di training non rieseguiti;4 packaging ENVIRONMENT_FAILURE storici non reinterpretati come PASS. `research_private/` ignorata e memoria docs/research tracciabile. Nessun commit/push/fetch/pull/tag/release/PR; solo bibliografia web read-only autorizzata.

**Fermarsi. Non iniziare Round75.**

## POST-R74 — Compiler function tomography (2026-10-01)

Status: COMPLETE; verdict **MIXED**. This is not Round75; `last_completed_round = 74`. Native research remains paused. No new teacher/compiler fit, solve, public code change or remote operation.

Read the persistent history and grand compiler report/audit; verified all470 old model/query hashes and replayed all144 final compiler checkpoints. Eighteen frozen teachers/splits, six known sources. Retrospective diagnosis, not confirmation and not a test-trained selector.

- Frozen global SBD/jitter retention28.355583%, observed28.568644%, active19.421909%, serialized.022855x and standalone144/144 are unchanged.
- Adult: SBD99.09% task retention, .02327 NMSE and .02894x bytes. Archived main-only explains94.01% teacher variance; threshold/logit match plausible, necessity of conjunctions unproven.
- MillionSong: RBF92.38% retention does not mean near-exact or compact compilation: NMSE.17312, bytes1.04423x teacher. Exact MSE decomposition shows82.03% cancellation of compression error by the teacher-residual cross-term. Smooth one-coordinate response coexists with weak nearest-neighbor locality and effective metric dimension31.07.
- Diabetes:264 observed rows, no positive teacher advantage (retention undefined); selected excess MSE is15.27% teacher weakness and84.73% compilation increment. Only1.61% positive excess SSE lies in target tails. Fewer unique thresholds and MORE reuse than Adult; absolute tree size/boundary reuse does not identify failures. Same-code zero variance is uninformative with singleton codes. Common-heldout teacher stability has only7 anchors, explicitly LOW_POWER.
- Twenty-four fixed-dictionary query contrasts reused. Adult1k→5k distinct queries improve NMSE.07027→.03835 in one frozen-teacher split; Diabetes10k/25k query budgets still contain264 unique rows. More real data at fixed function geometry remains untested.
- Main/pair, boundary, locality, leaf support, error-importance and rare-support diagnostics are preserved. Six-source interpretable geometry/support correlations reach|rho|=.60; stronger protocol/duplicate/underpowered-order associations are disclosed, not promoted to a causal signature.

No missing basis admitted after historical/prior-art filter. Three conditional gaps (adaptive product selection, local-affine enrichment, sparse exceptions) lack the required new geometry-to-mechanism evidence. **No microtest; no candidate frozen; Round75 NOT JUSTIFIED.** Existing finite-library failure does not prove every compiler impossible. Exact additive EBM deployment remains the stronger separate direction.

## POST-R77 — Native SafeBlend architecture discovery

Date: 2026-10-03. **Not Round78; last_completed_round remains 77; public version remains 0.2.0rc2.**

Verdict: **NATIVE_CORRECTOR_NOT_YET_FOUND**. Phase A and admission audit completed; **Stage1/Stage2 NOT_STARTED**, no implemented or evaluated native replacement. This is an admission stop, not negative empirical performance evidence for a new architecture.

Reused **78 R75/R76 contexts (26 known sources × 3 splits)** and verified 1,476 original artifacts. Zero new teacher fits, zero native fits, zero OOF Base fits. Diagnostic probes used cached `D=C−B`, with true target residuals used only for diagnosis, not native training. Stage2's twelve sources were sealed in this activity and were historically observed R76 sources; no independent native confirmation is claimed.

Source-balanced median finite main recovery **8.59%**, pair increment **3.58%**, three-way increment **0.56%**, finite unexplained remainder **87.40%**. This is an ordered finite projection, **not unique functional ANOVA**; the remainder is not identified higher-order signal. All-feature/all-available-state main probe did not materially change the median. Native encoder capacity remains the frozen model's capacity even when a diagnostic requests 64 states. Missing/category/tail masks overlap and are not causal shares.

`D`/residual Pearson **0.304**, same-row heldout delta stability **0.435**, main-shape stability **0.307**. Historical fixed-shrink correction captures **6.85%** of squared residual error on this forensic subset. UJI shows a stable/representable correction with poor target utility; Geography shows poor alignment and amplitude downside. These are descriptive subset numbers, not replacements for original Round75/76 confirmation results.

Four proposals considered after Phase A: centered coded TensorSketch, nonseparable ancestry-coupled details, discrete residual-gradient metric, conditional tail-moment correction. **None admitted**. Kernel/sketch proposals lack a cleared R17/18 equivalence and target-alignment witness; ancestry needs contribution-weighted target-stable parent/child evidence; metric rank proxies do not establish target-aligned geometry; unconstrained tail decomposition is algebraically the same conditional cell mean. No broad literature family is newly empirically falsified. OOF residual training, alpha=1/.5 and validation fallback remain **unevaluated** for a new native model; R76 does not validate them automatically.

Technical audit repair: initial pair/triple/local diagnostic maps used heldout predictor maxima to define capacity. v2 uses frozen train-only schemas, preserves original outputs, reuses valid main fits and covariance solves, and recalculates only affected small tables. No heldout target was fit. Constant-feature roughness reporting was repaired without teacher/model refits.

**Fully native SafeBlend is not achieved.** Public SafeBlend still requires LightGBM during fit and no teacher at inference. Removal/deprecation remains a pending product decision; the user's final instruction freezes public SafeBlend during this research activity. No public source/API/default change, no remote operation, commit, push, tag or release.

Next eligible step: recover the missing R17/18 concrete algebra, or establish a genuinely distinct train-only target-aligned ancestry/metric/kernel witness with a matched control; only then one preregistered native micro-test. No automatic Round78.

Artifacts: `research_private/NATIVE_SAFEBLEND_DISCOVERY.md`, `research_private/native_safeblend/` (history, frozen panels, tomography/capacity/local/covariance/shape/calibration diagnostics, candidate admission specs, NOT_STARTED result manifests, checks and final audit). Knowledge base updated with admission status and exact retry conditions; prior conclusions preserved.
